### [1] [Requirements Perception Gap across Stakeholders: A Comparative Survey of Aged Care Digital Health Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21495)
*Yuqing Xiao,John Grundy,Anuradha Madugalla,Elizabeth Manias*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对249名参与者（老年人、护理人员、软件开发者）的混合方法调查，揭示了老年护理数字健康软件中存在的"需求鸿沟"：开发者倾向于优先考虑高级功能，高估了用户对易用性等非功能需求的满意度；而老年人和护理人员更重视简洁性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索并比较老年人、护理人员（正式和非正式）以及数字健康软件开发者三个关键利益相关群体对老年护理数字健康软件功能性和非功能性需求的观点差异。

Method: 基于现有系统综述发现设计调查问卷，采用混合方法（定量问卷+定性开放式回答），对249名参与者（老年人103人、护理人员41人、软件开发者105人）进行数据收集，运用推断性统计进行组间满意度比较，并使用主题分析法分析定性数据。

Result: 发现显著的"需求鸿沟"：开发者倾向于优先考虑高级功能和功能性需求，显著高估了用户对核心非功能性需求（如易用性和响应性）的满意度；而开发者对现有功能特性的批评程度高于老年人和护理人员，后者更重视简洁性和可靠性而非功能密度。

Conclusion: 通过定量与定性分析相结合，确定了利益相关者在功能性和非功能性需求上优先级一致与分歧之处；研究提出的利益相关者差距分析可指导未来协同设计过程、近期产品决策以及老年护理数字健康中的隐私设计建议。

Abstract: We sought to explore and compare the perspectives of three key stakeholder groups: older adults, caregivers (formal health providers and informal caregivers), and digital health software developers on key functional and non-functional requirements. We conducted a survey, designed based on the findings from an existing systematic review, to gather and analyse data related to the three stakeholder groups' (dis)satisfaction with current aged care digital health software and their views on key future aged care software requirements. A mixed-methods survey approach integrated quantitative questionnaire data and qualitative open-ended responses from a total sample of 249, comprised of older adults (103), formal and informal caregivers (41), and software developers (105). Data analysis utilised a mixed methods approach, employing inferential statistics to compare group satisfaction levels and thematic analysis for qualitative open-ended responses. Our analysis reveals a significant "Requirements Gap". Software developers tend to prioritise advanced features and functional requirements, significantly overestimating user satisfaction with core NFRs such as ease of use and responsiveness. Conversely, developers were more critical of existing functional features compared to older adults and caregivers, who prioritised simplicity and reliability over feature density. By combining quantitative and qualitative analysis, we identified where stakeholder priorities align and where they diverge across functional and non-functional requirements in both the current designs they used and the future designs they desire. Our findings present a stakeholder gap analysis that can guide future co-design processes, near-term product decisions, and privacy-by-design recommendations in aged care digital health.

</details>


### [2] [Articulate but Wrong: Self-Review Failures in LLM-Based Code Modernization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21537)
*Gokul Chandra Purnachandra Reddy,Aditya Lolla,Harsha Sanku*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体在迁移遗留代码时，能否可靠地自我识别输出是否改变了行为语义。通过对11个生产级LLM进行1,980次现代化调用实验，发现语义保持漂移普遍存在（39.7% vs 基线7.0%），且自评机制不可靠（31.7%的漂移被产生模型自己误判为行为保持），漂移率与模型能力/价格非单调相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当LLM被用于将遗留代码现代化时，同一模型能否可靠地识别自己的输出是否悄悄改变了代码的可观测行为？这是一个看似简单但实际关键的问题，关乎自动化代码迁移的信任基础。

Method: 使用60个遗留Python 2代码片段（平衡设计），在11个生产级LLM（来自7个家族）上执行1,980次真实现代化调用，对每个输出使用类型严格的行为oracle评估，然后让每个模型自我判断其输出是否保持了行为。

Result: (1) 语义保持漂移普遍：语义陷阱片段漂移率39.7% vs 良性对照代码7.0%；(2) 漂移集中在小部分困难片段上，模型间高度一致（平均Pearson r=0.52）；(3) 自我审查不可靠：31.7%的漂移被产生模型自己误判为行为保持，且模型自我漏报率呈双峰分布（0%~100%）；(4) 漂移率与模型能力和价格非单调相关（5.6%~46.7%）。

Conclusion: LLM在代码现代化任务中的语义保持漂移是一个结构性的、与模型规模无关的根本问题，自我审查机制不能作为安全网，需要外部验证机制来确保行为保持。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to migrate legacy code to modern stacks. We ask a deceptively simple question: when an LLM modernizes legacy code, can the same model be relied upon to recognize when its own output silently changes observable behavior? We run 1,980 real modernization calls across 11 production LLMs from 7 distinct families on a balanced 60-snippet legacy-Python-2 corpus, evaluate every output with a type-strict behavioral oracle, and then ask each model to judge whether its own output preserves behavior. We report four findings. (1) Semantic-preservation drift is prevalent and sharply separable from a cleanly-controlled baseline: semantic-trap snippets drift in 39.7% of attempts versus 7.0% on benign-control code that requires no real modernization (+32.7 percentage points; n=660 each). (2) Drift concentrates on specific snippets that fail across models: pairwise model agreement on which snippets are hard is high (mean Pearson r=0.52), and a small core of numeric-semantics snippets fails for nearly every model and every prompt phrasing. (3) Self-review by the producing model is not a reliable safety net: across all semantic drift cases, 31.7% are silently endorsed by the same model that produced them (83/262), and the per-model self-miss rate is strongly bimodal -- ranging from 0% on five models to 100% on one widely deployed model -- with several models explicitly articulating the very Py2/Py3 semantic distinction that broke their output, then declaring behavior preserved. (4) Drift rate is non-monotone in model capability and price: per-model rates range 5.6%-46.7% and do not track model capability cleanly, indicating the failure is task-structural rather than driven by model scale. All code, prompts, the 60-snippet corpus, the behavioral oracle, the output extractor, and the raw model outputs are released.

</details>


### [3] [RefusalBench: Why Refusal Rate Misranks Frontier LLMs on Biological Research Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21545)
*Lukas Weidener,Marko Brkić,Mihailo Jovanović,Emre Ulgac,Aakaash Meduri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RefusalBench是一个包含141个提示（47组匹配三元组）的基准测试，用于评估19个前沿大语言模型在不同生物风险等级（良性、边缘、双重用途）下的拒绝行为。研究发现：各模型严格拒绝率差异巨大（0.1%-94.6%）；司法管辖区不能预测拒绝行为，但提供商身份可以（Anthropic的OR=21.03）；严格拒绝率会错误排序安全校准（Grok 4.20区分度最高但拒绝率排名第7）；9/18模型在双重用途层级表现出"部分顺从"模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型越来越多地被部署为生物研究工作流的编排骨干，但缺乏共享的证据基础来比较它们对合法研究提示的拒绝行为。现有研究无法区分模型拒绝是基于任务内容还是生物风险等级，因此需要一个能够控制任务框架、仅变化风险层级的基准来评估模型的安全校准能力。

Method: 构建了141个提示的匹配三元组基准（47个捆绑包），保持任务框架恒定，仅变化生物风险层级（良性、边缘、双重用途）。设计了15个"应当拒绝"的阳性对照提示建立校准基线。对2026年5月快照中的19个前沿模型进行测试，使用Mann-Whitney U检验、聚类逻辑回归（GEE）、Youden's J指数等统计方法分析拒绝行为、司法管辖区效应、提供商效应和安全校准。

Result: 严格拒绝率从0.1%到94.6%不等；3个模型连阳性对照提示也未能拒绝。司法管辖区与拒绝行为无显著关联（p=0.393）；Anthropic的API栈预测拒绝行为优势比为21.03。99.8%的Anthropic严格拒绝带有相同的safety_policy裁决原因码。Grok 4.20的Youden's J指数最高（0.787）但拒绝率仅排第7；Claude Opus 4.7的J指数较前版本下降65%。9/18模型在双重用途层级表现出"对冲但协助"的部分顺从模式。

Conclusion: 现有前沿模型的生物安全拒绝行为存在极大不一致性，且严格拒绝率作为安全校准指标具有误导性。提供商效应主要体现在访问路径层面而非模型权重层面。需要更精细的、超越二元拒绝指标的安全评估方法，特别是针对双重用途场景中模型表现出的"部分顺从"混合行为模式。

Abstract: Frontier large language models are increasingly deployed as orchestration backbones for biological research workflows, yet no shared evidence base exists for comparing their refusal behaviour on legitimate research prompts. RefusalBench, introduced here, is a matched-triple benchmark of 141 prompts in 47 bundles that holds task framing constant while varying only biological risk tier (benign, borderline, dual-use), enabling tier-conditioned comparisons robust to subdomain confounding. A 15-prompt should-refuse positive-control module establishes per-model calibration floors; three models fail to refuse even these prompts. Across 19 frontier models in the May 2026 snapshot, strict refusal rates span 0.1% to 94.6% on identical prompts. Jurisdiction does not predict refusal in this snapshot (Mann-Whitney U, p = 0.393; EU n = 1, US bimodal); provider identity does, with Anthropic's API stack predicting refusal at OR = 21.03 (95% CI: 14.58-30.34 prompt-clustered; 5.70-77.55 under model-clustered GEE). This effect is best read as access-path-level rather than model-weight-level: 99.8% of Anthropic's strict refusals carry the same safety_policy adjudicated reason code, consistent with a small set of canonical refusal templates rather than case-by-case model reasoning. Strict refusal rate misranks safety calibration: Grok 4.20 achieves the highest tier discrimination (Youden's J = 0.787) while ranking only seventh by overall refusal rate, and Claude Opus 4.7's J dropped 65% from prior versions with no improvement in dual-use detection. Nine of 18 frontier models exhibit a hedge-but-help partial-compliance pattern at dual-use tier that binary refusal metrics cannot detect.

</details>


### [4] [A Dataset of Reproducible Flaky-Test Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21677)
*Suzzana Rafi,Mahbub-Ul-Hoque Sumon,Md Erfan,Maruf Morshed Khan,August Shi,Wing Lam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ReproFlake，一个包含 1115 个可复现的跨四个类别的不稳定测试数据集，提供了可重现环境、失败复现脚本、自动修复脚本以及执行日志，优于此前仅提供部分信息的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不稳定测试数据集往往由互不相交的测试集合组成，每个数据集仅提供单一类型的信息（如特定类别、失败日志或开发者/工具报告的区别），缺乏一个统一、可复现且信息全面的数据集来支持不稳定测试的深入研究。

Method: 从开发者问题报告和流行的不稳定测试数据集中收集并整理测试用例，构建可编译的可重现环境，提供失败复现脚本、自动修复应用脚本、修复后验证脚本以及执行日志。同时制定了贡献指南以扩展数据集。

Result: 创建了 ReproFlake 数据集，包含 1115 个可复现的不稳定测试，涵盖四个不稳定测试类别。通过实验展示了利用该数据集可以理解不稳定测试失败复现的挑战、修复位置与测试类别的相关性，以及收集额外信息（如代码覆盖率）的困难。

Conclusion: 错误信息有助于识别不稳定测试类别并指导修复；未解决的编译错误突显了构建遗留项目的挑战；了解典型修复位置有助于优先安排修复工作。

Abstract: Flaky tests pass and fail non-deterministically when run on the same version of code. Although many techniques have been proposed to detect, debug, and repair flaky tests, reproducing their failures remains a major challenge due to their inherent nondeterminism. Many flaky test datasets exist to help researchers study them, but these datasets are often composed of disjoint sets of flaky tests, where each dataset provides unique information, such as flaky tests of different categories, failure logs of flaky tests, or flaky tests reported by developers vs. flaky tests found by automated tools. In this work, we aim to create a reproducible dataset of flaky tests, curated from both developer issue reports and a popular dataset of flaky tests. Compared to prior flaky test datasets, our dataset is the first to provide (1) a reproducible environment to compile flaky tests, (2) scripts to reproduce failures, (3) scripts to automatically apply flaky test fixes and ensure that the tests are no longer flaky, and (4) execution logs of flaky test passing and failing. We present ReproFlake, a dataset of 1115 reproducible flaky tests across four flaky test categories. We create guidelines to help others contribute to this reproducible dataset, and demonstrate how to use our dataset to understand challenges in reproducing flaky test failures (e.g., challenges researchers may face when using prior flaky test datasets), the characteristics (e.g., location of the fix and its correlation with the flaky test category), and difficulties researchers may face in using our dataset to collect additional information (e.g., code coverage) about flaky tests. Our findings show that error information helps identify flaky test categories and guide repairs, that unresolved compilation failures highlight challenges in building legacy projects, and knowing typical fix locations can help prioritize repair efforts.

</details>


### [5] [The 2nd Workshop on Agile Practice & Research: A Summary and Call For Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21690)
*Karen Eilers,Michael Neumann,Eva-Maria Schön,Mali Senapathi,Maria Rauschenberger,Tiago Silva da Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于第二届敏捷实践与研究研讨会（XP 2026，圣保罗），聚焦敏捷软件开发中研究与实践之间的三大鸿沟（理论鸿沟、时间鸿沟、转移鸿沟），通过结构化协作研讨提出四项改进主张（改善科学沟通、对齐研究与实际需求、强化持续合作激励、整合教育方法），并形成三项研究呼吁（开放科学、高经验质量、明确价值贡献），旨在弥合学术界与工业界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷软件开发领域存在研究与实践之间的显著差距，具体表现为理论鸿沟、时间鸿沟和转移鸿沟。本文旨在通过组织研讨会，汇聚研究人员和从业者共同识别根本原因并制定联合解决方案，进而提出改进研究-实践交集的具体主张和研究方向。

Method: 在XP 2026大会上举办第二届敏捷实践与研究研讨会，采用结构化协作研讨方式，参会者分为若干小组分别聚焦三大鸿沟之一，对可能的原因和补救措施进行反思与讨论。组织者对研讨结果进行综合归纳，形成四项主张和三项研究呼吁。

Result: 提出四项改进主张：（1）改善科学沟通；（2）使研究更贴近新兴工业需求；（3）为持续合作创建更强激励；（4）将教育方法整合到研究实践中。形成三项研究呼吁：（a）更广泛采用开放科学实践；（b）通过更强理论基础和严谨设计提升经验研究质量；（c）产出更明确、以价值为导向、清晰阐明实践与科学相关性的贡献。

Conclusion: 本文既作为研讨会纪要，也作为加强研究-实践合作的号召，强调通过开放科学、高质量经验研究以及价值导向的贡献来缩小敏捷软件开发领域学术与工业实践之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Agile software development has been shaped by the interplay between academic research and industrial practice for over two decades, yet notable gaps persist between both domains. This paper focuses on three research-practice gaps: the theory gap, the time gap, and the transfer gap. To address these, the 2nd Agile Practice & Research Workshop was held at the International Conference on Agile Software Development (XP) 2026 in São Paulo, Brazil, bringing researchers and practitioners together to identify root causes and develop joint solutions. Building on two preceding sessions in which contributions of participants had been presented, participants engaged in a structured collaborative session, working in small groups on one of the three gaps and reflecting on possible causes and remedies. The organizers synthesized the results into four propositions for improving the research-practice intersection: (1) improving scientific communication, (2) aligning research more closely with emerging industrial needs, (3) creating stronger incentives for sustained collaboration, and (4) integrating educational approaches into research practice. From these, three calls for research were formulated: (a) broader adoption of open science practices for transparency, reproducibility, and cumulative evidence; (b) higher empirical quality standards through stronger theoretical grounding and rigorous design; and (c) more explicit, value-oriented contributions that clearly articulate their practical and scientific relevance. The paper offers both a summary of the workshop and a call to strengthen research-practice collaboration.

</details>


### [6] [PITMuS: A Tool for Automated Bug Dataset Generation via Source-Level Mutant Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21930)
*Tasfia Tasnim,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PITMuS是一个工具，它结合PIT的XML突变报告和Java类文件的调试信息，将字节码级别的突变逆向重构为源代码级别的编辑，自动生成包含正确/错误代码对、文档和元数据的结构化数据集，用于LLM-based软件工程的训练和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如Defects4J）是静态的且易受数据污染影响，而突变测试生成的bug工件难以作为结构化源代码级数据集复用。需要一种方法从突变测试中自动生成可检查、可重放、可复用的源代码级bug数据集。

Method: PITMuS将PIT（字节码级变异测试工具）生成的XML突变报告与Java类文件的调试信息结合，定位并重建每个突变对应的源代码编辑，自动输出结构化的源代码级正确/错误代码对、文档上下文和元数据。

Result: 在8个开源Java系统上评估了PITMuS，表明其可应用于任何能集成PIT的Java系统，生成可用于下游训练和评估的结构化数据集。

Conclusion: PITMuS提供了一种互补于静态基准的策略，通过从突变测试中自动生成截止感知（cutoff-aware）的源代码级bug数据集，支持LLM-based软件工程中bug定位、修复、测试和文档自动化等任务的训练与评估。

Abstract: LLM-based software engineering increasingly depends on executable, context-rich bug artifacts: paired correct and buggy code, methods under test (MUTs), documentation, and metadata. These artifacts support the training and evaluation of automated bug localization and repair techniques, testing and test oracle generation methods, and documentation-driven automation. Although curated benchmarks (e.g., Defects4J) remain valuable, they are static and increasingly vulnerable to contamination as code models are trained on large public corpora. A complementary strategy is to generate fresh, cutoff-aware datasets by selecting real system versions and injecting controlled bugs at the source level.
  Mutation testing is a natural basis for this strategy: it applies predefined mutation operators to programs and records whether the existing test suite detects each injected change. PIT is a state-of-the-practice mutation testing tool for Java that performs mutation at the bytecode level. This design makes mutation testing fast and practical, but PITMuS reports mutants primarily through XML, making them difficult to inspect, replay, or reuse as structured source-level dataset records. To address this gap, we present PITMuS, which combines PITMuS XML metadata with debug information from compiled Java class files to localize and reconstruct the source edit corresponding to each mutant. PITMuS then automatically produces structured datasets containing source-level buggy and fixed code pairs, documentation context, and metadata for downstream training and evaluation. Although we evaluate PITMuS on eight open-source Java systems, it can be applied to any Java system where PITMuS can be integrated.

</details>


### [7] [Deterministic vs. Probabilistic Summarisation: An Empirical Trade-off Study in Design Pattern Centric Java Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21943)
*Najam Nazar,Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对确定性（基于规则/SWUM）和概率性（基于Mixtral LLM）的代码摘要方法进行了受控实证比较，使用面向设计模式的Java代码作为测试基准。结果表明：概率性方法在语义对齐和上下文覆盖方面更优，而确定性方法在简洁性和可重复性上占优，两者存在明确的权衡取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化代码摘要对于程序理解和文档化至关重要，但确定性（启发式）与概率性（基于LLM）两种范式在面向设计模式的代码摘要中的相对优势与局限性尚不明确，亟需系统性的实证对比。

Method: 以150个涵盖9种设计模式的Java文件为结构化测试基准，比较了基于规则的自然语言生成（NLG）流水线、基于软件词汇使用模型（SWUM）的方法和基于Mixtral LLM的概率性流水线。使用BERTScore和余弦相似度进行自动评估，辅以Llama 3在五个维度（准确性、简洁性、充分性、代码上下文感知、设计模式保真度）上的评分卡判断，并采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验、Friedman检验和Spearman相关性分析进行统计验证。

Result: 概率性摘要表现出更强的语义对齐能力和更丰富的上下文覆盖，而确定性方法输出更简洁且完全可重复。LLM输出存在提示敏感性和多轮变异性，但相对趋势保持稳定。

Conclusion: 两种范式存在明确的权衡：概率性方法更适合追求语义深度和上下文准确性，确定性流水线则在简洁性和可重复性方面更优。研究成果为代码摘要技术的选择提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Background: Automated code summarisation supports program comprehension and documentation, yet the relative strengths and limitations of deterministic (heuristic-based) and probabilistic (LLM-based) pipelines remain unclear. Aims: This paper presents a controlled empirical comparison of these paradigms for intent-oriented design-pattern code summarisation. Method: Using design-pattern-centric Java code as a structured testbed (150 files from three open-source repositories covering nine patterns), we compare a rule-based natural language generation (NLG) pipeline, a Software Word Usage Model (SWUM)-based approach, and a probabilistic pipeline based on the Mixtral LLM. Summaries are evaluated against human references using BERTScore and cosine similarity, complemented by rubric-based judgements produced by Llama 3 across five dimensions: accuracy, conciseness, adequacy, code-context awareness, and design-pattern fidelity. Statistical analysis includes Wilcoxon signed-rank tests (with effect sizes), Friedman tests with post-hoc corrections, and Spearman correlation for sensitivity analysis of rubric consistency. Results: Probabilistic summaries show stronger semantic alignment and richer contextual coverage, while deterministic approaches produce more concise and fully reproducible outputs. Prompt-sensitivity and multi-run analyses indicate variability in LLM outputs, though relative trends remain stable. Conclusions: A clear trade-off emerges: probabilistic methods favour semantic depth and contextual accuracy, whereas deterministic pipelines are preferable for brevity and reproducibility. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting code summarisation techniques.

</details>


### [8] [From Patches to Trajectories: Privileged Process Supervision for Software-Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21996)
*Murong Ma,Tianyu Chen,Yun Lin,Shuai Lu,Qinglin Zhu,Yeyun Gong,Zhiyong Huang,Peng Cheng,Yan Lu,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Patches-to-Trajectories (P2T) 方法，利用开发者编写的参考补丁作为特权信息，将轨迹构建建模为双目标优化（每步有效性与轨迹长度），仅用 1.8k 实例在 SWE-bench Verified 上提升 Pass@1 达 10.8 点，同时降低推理成本约 15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SFT 方法仅依赖二元终态验证器筛选教师轨迹，无法直接优化每步有效性与效率（信息冗余/循环），且对教师失败的实例缺乏监督。大多数真实问题包含开发者编写的参考补丁，但标准流程将其丢弃。

Method: P2T 包含两个阶段：(1) 反向阶段：将参考补丁蒸馏为潜在过程图 G*，包含上下文事实与解决方案里程碑；(2) 前向阶段：在屏蔽教师搜索路径的条件下，根据每步对 G* 的进展打分（通过防泄漏的 grounding 检查），保留最短有效轨迹段。

Result: 仅用 1.8k SWE-Gym 实例，P2T 在 SWE-bench Verified 上 Pass@1 提升高达 10.8 点，单实例推理成本降低约 15%；在 SWE-bench Lite 上取得一致提升；消融实验和定性分析验证了轨迹质量优于数据规模。

Conclusion: 利用参考补丁作为特权信息进行轨迹优化，能在不增加数据规模的情况下显著提升 SWE 智能体的推理有效性与效率。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on long teacher trajectories is the dominant way to instill investigation and reasoning in open software-engineering (SWE) agents. Since every retained response becomes an imitation target, the student inherits the final outcome and intermediate flaws, including ungrounded leaps and redundant loops. High-quality training data must be effective(each step is grounded and narrows the agent's epistemic gap to the correct fix) and efficient(each step is information-bearing rather than redundant or looping). Existing recipes filter or relabel teacher rollouts using only a binary terminal verifier, which does not directly target these axes and provides no supervision on instances where the teacher fails.
  Most real issue includes a developer-authored reference patch, $p^\star$, revealing the file paths, runtime behaviors, and coding conventions presupposed by the correct fix, yet standard pipelines discard it. We propose Patches-to-Trajectories (P2T), which uses $p^\star$ as privileged information during curation and formulates trajectory construction as bi-objective optimization over per-step effectiveness and trajectory length. A reverse phase distills $p^\star$ into a latent process graph, $G^\star$, of contextual facts and solution milestones. A forward phase curates trajectories from blinded teacher continuations by scoring per-step progress against $G^\star$ under a leakage-blocking groundedness check and retaining the shortest effective segments.
  Using only 1.8k curated SWE-Gym instances, P2T improves effectiveness and efficiency over outcome-filtered SFT and its tool-error-masking variant. On SWE-bench Verified, it raises Pass@1 by up to 10.8 points while reducing per-instance inference cost by ~15%, with consistent gains on SWE-bench Lite. Size-matched ablations and qualitative analysis further isolate trajectory quality from data scale.

</details>


### [9] [Finding Missing Input Validation in TEEs via LLM-Assisted Symbolic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22058)
*Chengyan Ma,Jieke Shi,Ruidong Han,Ye Liu,Yuqing Niu,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SymTEE提出了一种LLM辅助的符号执行框架，无需真实TEE环境即可检测TEE应用中的输入验证缺失漏洞，在26个漏洞上实现了100%精度和92.3%召回率，平均分析成本仅0.05美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE应用分析面临两大挑战：配置完整TEE构建和运行时环境的高成本与复杂性，以及硬件隔离带来的有限可观测性。现有方法难以高效、低成本地检测TEE应用中的输入验证缺失漏洞。

Method: SymTEE首先通过AST分析提取可能缺乏充分输入验证的TEE代码切片，然后利用LLM（GPT-5）自动将提取的切片转换为KLEE兼容的harness程序，其中包含轻量级模拟执行环境，用于符号分析。

Result: 在26个漏洞（11个真实世界漏洞和15个合成漏洞）上，SymTEE实现了100%的精度和92.3%的召回率，平均分析成本仅为0.05美元。

Conclusion: SymTEE开创了LLM辅助符号执行的新范式，LLM自主生成模拟环境以实现无需复杂配置的自动化安全分析，为可信计算系统提供了更易访问和可扩展的漏洞检测框架。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) provide hardware-enforced isolation that protects sensitive code and data from untrusted software. Despite their strong security guarantees, analyzing TEE applications remains challenging due to the high cost and complexity of configuring complete TEE build and runtime environments, as well as the limited observability imposed by hardware isolation. This paper presents SymTEE, a novel large language model (LLM)-assisted symbolic execution framework for detecting missing input validation issues in TEE applications without requiring real TEE setups. SymTEE begins by leveraging Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) analysis to extract TEE code slices that may lack sufficient input validation, and then employs an LLM (GPT-5 in our case) to automatically convert the extracted slices into KLEE-compatible harness programs containing lightweight mock execution environments for symbolic analysis. Evaluations on 26 vulnerabilities (11 real-world and 15 synthetic) show that SymTEE achieves 100% precision and 92.3% recall in detecting missing input validation vulnerabilities while incurring an average analysis cost of only $0.05. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and practicality of SymTEE's pioneering paradigm of LLM-assisted symbolic execution, where LLMs autonomously generate mock environments to enable automated security analysis without complex setup, providing a more accessible and scalable framework for trusted computing systems.

</details>


### [10] [Automated Repair of TEE Partitioning Issues via DSL-Guided and LLM-Assisted Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22087)
*Chengyan Ma,Jieke Shi,Ruidong Han,Ye Liu,Feng Li,Yuqing Niu,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TEERepair是一个自动修复TEE应用中不安全分区问题的框架，通过领域特定语言编码修复规则、利用大语言模型理解代码语义并生成上下文感知补丁，在基准测试中修复成功率达87.6%，并已有2个修复补丁被真实项目合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE应用通过SDK接口与不可信OS交互时，不当分区会导致数据泄露和代码注入等严重漏洞。现有工作主要关注静态分析检测，自动修复技术仍几乎空白，且面临缺乏安全开发指南、C代码语义提取困难、缺少成熟测试验证方法三大挑战。

Method: 提出TEERepair框架：(1) 设计领域特定语言(DSL)编码修复规则，捕获常见TEE安全模式并实例化为带占位符的补丁模板；(2) 利用大语言模型(LLM)推理代码语义，合成上下文感知补丁；(3) 自动生成测试客户端验证修复正确性。

Result: 在TEE分区错误基准测试集上达到87.6%的修复成功率，显著优于基线方法；应用于真实TEE项目后提交5个修复PR，其中2个已被项目维护者确认并合并。

Conclusion: TEERepair有效解决了TEE应用自动修复问题，验证了DSL驱动规则与LLM语义理解相结合的可行性，为可信执行环境的安全开发提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) provide hardware-based isolation to protect sensitive data and computations from potentially compromised operating systems (OS). However, TEE applications inevitably interact with the untrusted OS through SDK interfaces, and improper partitioning can introduce severe vulnerabilities such as data leakage and code injection. While prior work has proposed static analysis tools to detect such issues, automated repair remains largely unexplored. This problem is particularly challenging due to three TEE-specific factors: the lack of standardized secure development guidelines, the difficulty of extracting semantic information from low-level C code, and the absence of mature testing and validation methods. In this work, we present TEERepair, a framework for automatically repairing bad partitioning issues in TEE applications. Our approach tackles the above challenges by introducing a domain-specific language (DSL) to encode repair rules that express and capture common TEE security patterns, which are instantiated as patch templates with placeholders for context-specific variables. We then leverage large language models (LLMs) to reason about code semantics and synthesize context-aware patches, and further generate test clients to validate the repairs. We evaluate TEERepair on the TEE Partitioning Errors Benchmark (PartitioningE-Bench), achieving a significantly higher repair success rate of 87.6% compared to baselines. Furthermore, applying TEERepair to real-world TEE projects, we submitted 5 repair pull requests, 2 of which have been confirmed and merged by project maintainers.

</details>


### [11] [SWE-Mutation: Can LLMs Generate Reliable Test Suites in Software Engineering?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22175)
*Yuxuan Sun,Yuze Zhao,Yufeng Wang,Yao Du,Zhiyuan Ma,Jinbo Wang,Mengdi Zhang,Kai Zhang,Zhenya Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Mutation基准，通过系统性变异解决方案来评估LLM生成的测试套件质量。实验表明，即使最强模型DeepSeek-V3.1的验证率也仅10.20%，检测率36.15%，揭示了当前LLM生成可靠且具判别力测试套件的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量测试套件对LLM的软件工程能力评估和强化学习至关重要，但人工标注成本高、LLM自动生成质量浅薄，缺乏判别力。亟需构建高质量测试套件的评估基准。

Method: 提出SWE-Mutation基准，通过系统性变异解决方案生成能"欺骗"测试套件的突变体来评估测试套件质量；设计了一种语言无关的智能体框架自动生成复杂突变体，涵盖9种编程语言、2636个变异实例。

Result: 7个LLM的实验中，DeepSeek-V3.1验证率仅10.20%、检测率36.15%；智能体变异策略将平均检测率从71.04%降至39.81%，显著提升了变异的真实性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM生成的测试套件在可靠性和判别力方面存在持续性缺陷，亟需改进；SWE-Mutation可作为推动高质量测试套件生成的重要基准。

Abstract: Evaluating software engineering capabilities has become a core component of modern large language models (LLMs); however, the key bottleneck hindering further scaling lies not in the scarcity of high-quality solutions, but in the lack of high-quality test suites. Test suites are indispensable both for synthesizing program repair trajectories and for providing precise feedback signals in reinforcement learning. Unfortunately, due to the high cost and difficulty of annotation, high-quality test suites have long been hard to obtain, while those automatically generated by LLMs tend to be superficial and lack sufficient discriminative power. As a first step toward constructing high-quality test suites, we introduce SWE-Mutation, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-generated test suites. The benchmark characterizes test suites by introducing systematically mutated solutions that attempt to ``fool'' the test suites and pass validation. We further propose an agentic, language-agnostic framework for automatically generating complex mutants. Our benchmark consists of 2,636 mutated variants derived from 800 original instances and includes a multilingual subset spanning nine programming languages. Experiments on seven LLMs reveal that even DeepSeek-V3.1 achieves only 10.20% verification and 36.15% detection rates, highlighting the inadequacy of current LLMs. Additionally, our agentic mutation strategy enhances realism, reducing average detection rates from 71.04% to 39.81% compared to conventional methods. These findings expose persistent deficiencies in the ability of current LLMs to generate reliable and discriminative test suites.

</details>


### [12] [An Architecture for Decentralised Deployment and Operation of Blockchain Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22239)
*Fabian Stiehle,Kirill Inozemtsev,Ingo Weber*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合DevOps最佳实践的区块链智能合约去中心化部署架构，通过扩展注册表模式支持确定性部署，并实现了包含集成部署流水线、用户界面和版本控制集成的去中心化开发框架，各参与方在参与新合约前需独立实现并验证测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链应用的DevOps基础设施（如自动化测试、持续集成与部署）通常由单一实体控制，缺乏去中心化；而大型应用依赖的DAO治理架构复杂，且难以与合约发现和升级模式集成。本文旨在解决这些痛点，提供一种去中心化且与不同治理、升级实现无关的架构。

Method: 将DevOps最佳实践与区块链治理概念集成，扩展注册表模式以支持确定性部署；设计去中心化部署框架，包含集成和部署流水线、用户界面及版本控制集成；各参与方在合约使用前需自行实现并验证测试；提供开源参考实现。

Result: 提出了一个与具体治理和升级实现无关的去中心化部署架构，提供了开源参考实现，并进行了全面评估。该架构可作为未来集成的参考，降低了实际采用此类流程的复杂性。

Conclusion: 本文的架构成功将DevOps实践去中心化，解决了智能合约部署中的信任和控制单点问题，为区块链应用开发提供了更可靠和可扩展的流程。

Abstract: Blockchains and distributed ledger technologies allow the operation of manifold decentralised applications (dApps). Such applications are based on smart contracts, a programmable abstraction that is executed in a decentralised manner. To ensure the correctness of smart contracts, blockchain application developers rely on DevOps practices such as automated testing and continuous integration and deployment. However, such infrastructure is often controlled by single entities. For larger blockchain applications, this issue is resolved by relying on concepts of Decentralised Autonomous Organisations (DAOs), which allow proposals to be autonomously executed once they reach a pre-defined quorum. Such a governance architecture is complex and requires integration with existing patterns for contract discovery and upgradeability. In this paper we integrate these concepts considering DevOps best-practices into a novel architecture that remains agnostic to different governance and upgrade implementations. We extend the known registry pattern to support deterministic deployments and present a decentralised deployment framework, including integration and deployment pipelines, user-interfaces, and version control integration. In our approach, each party implements and verifies their own tests before engaging in the use of a (newly deployed) smart contract. We provide a reference implementation, available as open-source, and evaluate the proposal thoroughly. Our architecture can serve as a reference for future integrations, while our open-source framework is aimed at reducing the complexity of adopting such a process in practice.

</details>


### [13] [At What Cost? Software Developers' Well-Being in the Age of GenAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22349)
*Mariam Guizani,Maduka Subasinghage,Sherlock A. Licorish,Sofia Ouhbi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式人工智能（GenAI）在软件开发中过度关注生产力和性能，忽视了对开发者福祉的负面影响，如认知负荷增加、监督劳动新形式、压力与倦怠加剧等，并呼吁研究议程转向关注人类体验、社会背景和可持续生产力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI在软件开发中的应用日益广泛，但现有研究过度聚焦于生产力和性能提升，忽视了其对开发者认知负荷、工作压力、职业倦怠及工作生活平衡等方面的负面影响，亟需重新平衡研究视角。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，通过理论分析提出一个以人类体验、社会背景和可持续生产力为中心的新研究框架。

Result: 揭示了GenAI工具在软件开发中引发的认知负荷增加、监督劳动新形式、产出与节奏期望升级等问题，以及职业规范转变、进入门槛变化、持续适应需求和不平等加深等现象。

Conclusion: 呼吁将GenAI在软件开发中的研究议程从狭隘的性能指标转向更全面地关注开发者福祉、社会可持续性和人类体验。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is rapidly reshaping software development, with growing emphasis on accelerating productivity and optimizing performance. However, excessive focus on such dimensions risks overlooking the critical implications for developer well-being. GenAI tools can amplify cognitive load, introduce new forms of oversight labor, and escalate expectations around output and pace, contributing to stress, burnout, and diminished work-life balance. The GenAI movement is also transforming professional norms, altering career entry points, demanding continuous adaptation, and deepening inequalities in access and support. This position paper calls for a reorientation of the GenAI research agenda in software development and proposes a theoretical framework to move beyond narrow performance metrics toward investigations that also center on human experience, social context, and sustainable productivity.

</details>


### [14] ["Refactoring Runaway": Understanding and Mitigating Tangled Refactorings in Coding Agents for Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22526)
*Zhao Tian,Zifan Zhang,Tao Xiao,Dong Wang,Masanari Kondo,Junjie Chen,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了代码智能体在软件问题修复中引入的"纠缠重构"现象。相比人类开发者（36.72%），智能体引入纠缠重构的频率更低（21.43%）、强度更弱（0.66 vs 1.75），但重构类型更多样。逻辑回归分析表明纠缠重构与编译失败显著相关，但与功能正确性无关。基于此，作者提出重构感知优化方法，将编译成功率从19.34%提升至38.33%，并额外解决了2.79%之前未解决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的软件问题修复常包含重构操作（即纠缠重构），人类开发者自然地将重构融入修复过程。鉴于LLM训练于大规模开源仓库，编码智能体可能继承这一行为。现有研究缺乏对智能体在问题解决中纠缠重构行为的系统性理解，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在Multi-SWE-bench基准上，分析3个智能体框架结合12种LLM生成的3,691个有效补丁，统计纠缠重构的频率、强度和类型多样性；使用逻辑回归分析纠缠重构与编译成功率和功能正确性的关联；提出重构感知优化方法，评估重构的必要性和安全性，选择性移除或修复有问题的重构。

Result: 编码智能体引入纠缠重构的频率（21.43% vs 36.72%）和强度（0.66 vs 1.75）均低于人类开发者，但重构类型更丰富。纠缠重构与编译成功率显著负相关，与功能正确性无显著关联。提出的方法将编译成功率从19.34%提升至38.33%，额外解决2.79%的未解决问题。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统揭示了编码智能体在问题解决中的纠缠重构行为特征，并证明通过感知重构的优化方法可有效提升补丁编译成功率和问题解决率，为未来改进代码智能体的重构行为提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in coding agents have shown remarkable progress in software issue resolution. In practice, real-world issues are typically bug fixes or feature requests in which human developers naturally incorporate refactoring as part of the resolution process, resulting in tangled refactoring. Since LLMs are trained on large-scale open-source repositories, coding agents may inherit such behaviors. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study on Multi-SWE-bench, analyzing 3,691 valid patches generated by three agent frameworks with 12 LLMs. We find that coding agents introduce tangled refactorings less frequently (21.43% vs. 36.72%) and with lower intensity (0.66 vs. 1.75) than human developers, although they exhibit a broader diversity of refactoring types. Logistic regression analysis further shows that tangled refactorings are strongly associated with reduced compilability, while exhibiting no significant association with functional correctness. Based on these findings, we propose a refactoring-aware refinement approach that assesses the necessity and safety of tangled refactorings and selectively removes or repairs problematic operations. Our approach improves compilability from 19.34% to 38.33%, and additionally resolves 2.79% previously unresolved issues. Overall, this work presents the first step towards understanding tangled refactoring practices in agentic issue resolution and opens up avenues for future work.

</details>


### [15] [Contractual Skills: A GovernSpec Design Framework for Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22634)
*Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出“契约型技能”（contractual skills），一种基于GovernSpec启发的设计框架，用于将SKILL.md文件组织为可读的任务契约，在保留轻量级技能发现和渐进加载的同时，明确目标、输入边界、权限、证据要求、输出合同、质量标准、验证步骤、人工审批点和交接规则。实验表明，契约型技能在生成质量上优于无技能和最小技能基线，但与信息丰富的纯扩展技能相比提升有限；在工具调用中能减少高风险尝试，但仍需运行时护栏。结论认为契约型技能应被理解为一种治理层，而非独立的安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业场景下，技能文件需要表达的不只是任务指导，还需包含目标、输入边界、权限、证据要求、输出合同、质量标准、验证步骤、人工审批点和交接规则等可检查（inspectable）的信息。现有技能封装方式缺乏对这些治理要素的系统化组织。

Method: 提出契约型技能（contractual skills）框架，借鉴GovernSpec设计思路，将SKILL.md文件组织为可读的任务契约。通过两个离线实验评估：（1）文本生成实验：3个企业技能、15个合成任务、4种指令条件、8个生成模型，共960个输出和1680条交叉评分记录；（2）工具调用挑战：8个模型、192个模拟工具调用记录。

Result: 文本生成：契约型技能在所有测试模型上优于无技能和最小技能基线；与信息丰富的纯扩展技能相比提升有限且混合。工具调用：技能通常能减少高风险工具尝试，但模型间差异显著，运行时工具护栏仍然必要。

Conclusion: 契约型技能最适合被理解为一种治理层（governance layer），用于显式化任务意图、边界和验收标准，而非独立的安全机制。其核心价值在于提升可检查性和可维护性，而非原始生成质量。

Abstract: Skills are increasingly used to package agent instructions, workflows, scripts, and reference materials. In enterprise settings, however, skills often need to express more than task guidance: they must make goals, input boundaries, permissions, evidence requirements, output contracts, quality criteria, verification steps, human approval points, and handoff rules inspectable. This paper proposes contractual skills, a GovernSpec-inspired design framework for organizing SKILL.md files as readable task contracts while preserving lightweight skill discovery and progressive loading. The framework clarifies the boundary between contractual skills, GovernSpec YAML contracts, Model Context Protocol surfaces, tool adapters, runtime guardrails, tracing, and evaluation systems.
  We evaluate the framework with two offline experiments. A text-generation study covers three enterprise skills, fifteen synthetic tasks, four instruction conditions, and eight generation models, yielding 960 outputs and 1680 cross-judge score records. Contractual skills outperform no-skill and minimal-skill baselines on all tested models. Relative to information-rich plain expanded skills, the gains are small and mixed, suggesting that contractual fields mainly improve checkability and maintainability rather than raw generation quality. A tool-calling challenge covers eight models and 192 simulated tool-call records. Skills usually reduce high-risk tool attempts, but model differences remain and runtime tool guardrails are still required. The results suggest that contractual skills are best understood as a governance layer that makes task intent, boundaries, and acceptance criteria explicit, not as a standalone safety mechanism.

</details>


### [16] [FAME: Failure-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Message-Level Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22779)
*Huanchi Wang,Zihang Huang,Yifang Tian,Kristina Dzeparoska,Hans-Arno Jacobsen,Alberto Leon-Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FAME提出了一种标签高效的、消息级别的日志异常检测框架，通过Mixture-of-Experts架构，仅需离线使用一次LLM，在BGL和Thunderbird数据集上取得了优异性能，显著降低了标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有日志异常检测方法多为会话级或窗口级，粒度粗糙，无法定位具体异常消息；而消息级检测面临模板歧义、异构故障、标注成本高等挑战。

Method: FAME框架包含：(1) 每个模板最多标注K条消息获取正常/异常标签和示例；(2) 利用LLM离线将模板划分为故障域；(3) 通过认证步骤验证划分；(4) 训练轻量级路由器和领域专家模型，部署在本地进行消息级异常预测和故障域标注。

Result: 在BGL数据集上，K=100时F1=98.16，标注量减少76倍，能检测86.3%的非训练集EventID异常；在Thunderbird数据集上F1=99.95，召回率完美。

Conclusion: FAME通过离线LLM + MoE架构实现了高效、细粒度、低标注成本的日志异常检测，证明了在保持高性能的同时大幅降低标注开销的可行性。

Abstract: Production systems generate millions of log lines daily, yet most anomaly detectors operate at the session or window-level, flagging groups of lines rather than identifying the specific message responsible. This coarse granularity forces operators to inspect many routine lines per alert. Message-level detection offers finer granularity, but remains challenging. A single event template may correspond to both normal and anomalous messages, failures arise from heterogeneous subsystems, and line-level labeling at scale is impractical. Although large language models (LLMs) can reason over log semantics, applying them to every line is too costly for continuous monitoring. We present FAME (Failure-Aware Mixture-of-Experts), a label-efficient message-level mixture-of-experts framework that uses an LLM only once offline. We annotate at most K labeled lines per template to derive binary normal/anomaly indicators and representative examples. The LLM proposes a partition of templates into failure domains, and a certification step validates the proposal before training. FAME trains a lightweight router and domain experts that run on-premise and output anomaly predictions and failure-domain labels. On BGL, FAME achieves F1 = 98.16 at K = 100 reducing annotation effort by 76x and detects 86.3% of anomalies from unseen EventIDs. On Thunderbird, FAME reaches F1 = 99.95 with perfect recall.

</details>

### [17] [Guiding Multi-Objective Genetic Programming with Description Length Improves Symbolic Regression Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22374)
*Gabriel Kronberger,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca,Deaglan J. Bartlett,Harry Desmond,Pedro G. Ferreira*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在含噪环境下，将描述长度（DL）和分数贝叶斯因子（FBF）作为遗传规划符号回归中模型选择准则的效果，发现DL/FBF后选择优于AIC/BIC基线，但直接作为适应度函数会导致过早收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传规划符号回归（GPSR）在含噪数据中容易过拟合和结构膨胀，现有启发式方法缺乏原则性和数据效率，需要更优的模型选择准则来平衡拟合度与泛化能力。

Method: 使用基于Fisher信息的参数编码实现DL准则，在多个数据集（含噪合成基准和真实回归问题）上比较DL、FBF与AIC、BIC。研究三种策略：(i)多目标搜索+DL/FBF后选择；(ii)以DL为目标的多目标搜索；(iii)以DL/FBF为适应度的单目标优化。

Result: DL/FBF后选择相比AIC/BIC基线提升了测试性能；BIC结合DL/FBF的函数复杂度惩罚可产生类似结果；但DL/FBF直接作为单目标适应度函数时，常导致过早收敛到过于简单的模型。

Conclusion: DL/FBF可作为遗传规划工作流中稳健的模型选择工具，推荐用于后选择阶段而非直接作为适应度函数。

Abstract: Symbolic regression with genetic programming (GPSR) may suffer from overfitting and structural bloat, especially when noise is present. In this paper we evaluate description length (DL) and fractional Bayes factor (FBF) criteria as principled, data-efficient alternatives to heuristics for selecting compact expressions that generalise well. We implement DL using a Fisher-information-based parameter encoding and compare it to AIC and BIC across multiple datasets, including noisy synthetic benchmarks and real-world regression problems. We study three search/selection strategies: (i) multi-objective search for accuracy and program length followed by DL/FBF selection; (ii) multi-objective search using DL directly as an objective; and (iii) single-objective optimisation with DL/FBF as the fitness. Across datasets we find that DL/FBF post-selection improves test performance compared to AIC/BIC baseline and that BIC in combination with the same function complexity penalty from DL/FBF produces similar results. In contrast, using DL/FBF directly as a fitness function in single-objective GPSR frequently induces premature convergence to overly simple models. We conclude with practical guidance for using DL/FBF as robust model-selection tools in genetic programming workflows.

</details>


### [18] [Quantum Genetic Optimization for Negative Selection Algorithms in Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22527)
*Giancarlo P. Gamberi,Calebe P. Bianchini*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出量子遗传负选择算法(QGNSA)，将量子遗传算法集成到EvoSeedRNSA中，利用量子叠加态和概率幅调整增强检测器生成效率，在元宇宙金融交易数据集上取得了更优的异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统负选择算法(NSA)在异常检测中受限于检测器生成效率，现有进化优化方法存在搜索空间探索和收敛效率不足的问题。

Method: 将量子遗传算法(QGA)引入EvoSeedRNSA框架，替代其经典进化优化过程，利用量子叠加态和概率幅调整机制进行搜索空间探索和收敛效率提升。

Result: 在Metaverse Financial Transactions Dataset上的实验表明，QGNSA相比经典方法实现了更优的异常检测精度，并在不同超参数配置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 量子计算在人工免疫系统尤其是高维异常检测任务中具有潜在优势；未来将优化量子电路设计、部署在真实量子硬件、探索混合量子-经典方法。

Abstract: Negative Selection Algorithms (NSAs), inspired by the self/non-self discrimination mechanism of the human immune system, have been widely employed in anomaly detection. However, their effectiveness is often constrained by the efficiency of detector generation. This paper presents the Quantum Genetic Negative Selection Algorithm (QGNSA), a novel approach that integrates a Quantum Genetic Algorithm (QGA) into the EvoSeedRNSA algorithm, replacing its classical evolutionary optimization process. The proposed method exploits quantum superposition and probabilistic amplitude adjustment to enhance search space exploration and convergence efficiency in the detector generation process. Empirical evaluations using the Metaverse Financial Transactions Dataset demonstrate that QGNSA achieves superior anomaly detection accuracy compared to its classical counterpart while maintaining robustness under varying hyperparameter configurations. The experimental results highlight the potential advantages of quantum computing in artificial immune systems, particularly in high-dimensional anomaly detection tasks. Future research will focus on further optimizing quantum circuit design, deploying the algorithm on real quantum hardware, and exploring hybrid quantum-classical approaches for improved computational efficiency.

</details>

### [19] [When Support Escalates Distress: Regulation and Escalation in LLM Responses to Venting and Advice-Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21569)
*Vivienne Bihe Chi,Adithya V Ganesan,Ryan L Boyd,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在心理健康支持中的心理安全性，发现LLM对发泄型与求助型两种求助风格会产生不同的情绪调节与升级反应，且普通用户难以察觉情绪升级，标准安全评估无法捕捉这一风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM广泛用于心理健康支持，但不同求助风格（发泄 vs. 寻求建议）下LLM的回应是否心理安全尚不清楚，需要系统评估。

Method: 使用178,800条Reddit帖子区分两种求助风格；基于人际情绪调节理论构建测量框架，将调节（Regulation）与升级（Escalation）作为独立维度；在不同角色条件（默认、朋友、治疗师）下测试GPT-5.3的回应；通过众包人类研究评估用户体验与情绪升级的可检测性。

Result: 发泄型求助引发更多调节与更多升级；治疗师角色减少升级同时维持调节，朋友角色则两者都增加；普通用户无法可靠检测情绪升级，看似支持性的回应可能同时加剧痛苦。

Conclusion: 仅靠共情指标无法替代同时测量调节与升级的框架，标准安全性评估存在盲区，需要更细致的心理安全测量方法。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for mental health support, yet little is known about whether their responses are psychologically safe across different help-seeking styles. We examine a foundational distinction in emotional disclosure, venting vs. advice-seeking, and whether LLMs respond in ways that regulate or amplify distress. Using 178,800 Reddit posts, we first show the two help-seeking styles are linguistically distinguishable at scale. We then introduce a measurement framework grounded in interpersonal emotion regulation theory that captures Regulation and Escalation as empirically independent dimensions. Across persona conditions (default, friend, therapist), GPT-5.3 responses systematically mirror help-seeking style: venting elicits more regulation, but also more escalation. Therapist personas reduce escalation while maintaining regulation, whereas friend personas increase both. A crowdsourced human study finds no user experience penalty for the safer therapist condition, but reveals that lay raters cannot reliably detect escalation without expert knowledge. Responses that feel supportive may simultaneously intensify distress in ways standard safety evaluation cannot see, and empathy metrics alone cannot replace a framework that measures both.

</details>


### [20] [Simulating Learners' Task-Selection Strategies and System Constraints in Mastery Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21613)
*Haley Noh,Aarna Chowdhary,Jeroen Ooge,Vincent Aleven,Conrad Borchers*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于模拟的框架，利用261名学生在两个数学领域（方程求解和图解读）的交互数据，模拟学习者的任务选择策略（如弱点导向、交错练习）与系统约束对掌握学习效率的影响。结果表明，风险规避策略会导致更高的过度练习，而针对性系统约束能显著减少低效策略的负面影响，且对高效策略影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能辅导系统中学习者与系统共享任务选择控制权，但学习者的任务选择策略（如回避挑战）可能与掌握学习系统基于知识估计的优化任务选择产生冲突。在真实课堂中测试系统约束成本高昂，因此需要一种低成本的模拟方法来评估不同策略与系统约束的交互效应。

Method: 基于261名学生在两个数学领域（方程求解、图解读）的交互数据，模拟了弱点导向和交错练习等任务选择策略，评估这些策略在系统约束下对过度练习（作为效率度量）的影响。

Result: 不同策略间存在显著差异，风险规避策略导致更高的过度练习水平，尤其在复杂多步骤问题上。针对性的系统约束能显著降低适应不良策略的低效性，同时对高效策略影响极小。

Conclusion: 基于学生数据的模拟可以有效指导共享控制辅导系统的重新设计，在课堂部署前识别并缓解潜在效率问题。

Abstract: Intelligent Tutoring Systems often grant learners shared control over skill and problem selection. Prior work suggests learners exhibit diverse task-selection strategies, such as avoiding challenge, which may interact with mastery learning systems that optimize task selection based on estimated knowledge. Algorithmic constraints on problem selection may help mitigate these effects, but testing such constraints in classrooms is costly. We propose a simulation-based framework to examine how learner task-selection strategies and system constraints shape mastery learning efficiency. Using interaction data from 261 students across two mathematical domains (equation solving and graph interpretation), we simulate strategies such as Weakness Targeting and Interleaving. We evaluate how these strategies affect overpractice as a measure of efficiency. Results show substantial variability across strategies, with risk-averse strategies producing higher levels of overpractice, especially for complex multi-step problems. Targeted system constraints significantly reduce inefficiencies for maladaptive strategies while minimally affecting already efficient strategies. These findings show how simulation grounded in student data can guide the redesign of shared-control tutoring systems before classroom deployment.

</details>


### [21] [Exploring the Effectiveness of Using LLMs for Automated Assessment of Student Self Explanations in Programming Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21614)
*Arun-Balajiee Lekshmi-Narayanan,Mohammad Hassany,Peter Brusilovsky*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文比较了大语言模型（LLMs）与语义相似度方法在自动评分学生解释性文本（如步骤自解释）中的效果，以二元分类任务为框架进行严格对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用工作示例结合自解释策略可提升学习效果，但评估学生解释的正确性是主要挑战。传统方法基于语义相似度判断学生解释与专家解释的相似性，而LLM自动评分技术的进展使得比较这两类方法的有效性成为必要。

Method: 将自动评分任务建模为二元分类问题，系统对比LLM方法与语义相似度方法在学生解释性文本评分上的表现，强调需要质量均衡、领域特定的标注数据集。

Result: 研究提供了LLM与语义相似度方法在自动评分上的严格对比结果，揭示了在存在高质量数据集条件下两类方法的性能差异。

Conclusion: 在LLM自动评分技术快速发展的背景下，需要重新评估语义相似度方法是否仍是最有效的学生文本自动评分技术；高质量数据集的构建对公平比较至关重要。

Abstract: Worked examples are step-by-step solutions to problems in a specific domain, offered to students to acquire domain-specific problem-solving skills. The effectiveness of worked examples could be enhanced by combining them with self-explanations, which ask students to explain rather than passively study each problem-solving step. The main challenge of this approach is assessing the correctness of the student's explanations. In the prevailing approach, student explanations are judged by their semantic similarity to an instructor's or domain expert's explanation. Given recent advances in LLM-based automated scoring, it remains unclear whether semantic similarity methods are still the most effective technique to automatically score textual student responses like essays or code explanations. Comparing these methods also requires quality datasets that offer distinctive features such as balanced class distributions and domain-specific labeled data for automated scoring tasks. In this paper, we present a rigorous comparison between LLMs and semantic similarity used for automated scoring, framed as a binary classification task.

</details>


### [22] [Addressing the Synergy Gap: The Six Elements of the Design Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21635)
*Tommaso Turchi,Ben Wilson,Matt Roach,Alan Dix,Alessio Malizia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文指出人机协同中广泛存在“协同鸿沟”（synergy gap），即人机组合未能超越各自单独表现，并提出一个包含六个相互关联要素的设计空间框架（社会技术背景、决策框架、人类决策参与者、AI能力、交互、整体评估），以弥合这一鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机协同的真实效果往往不如预期，多数研究聚焦于可解释性、信任校准或界面设计等工程问题，忽略了更广泛的决定因素，导致真正的协同（组合表现超越单独表现）难以实现。

Method: 通过系统梳理人机协同中存在的“协同鸿沟”现象，提出一个由六个相互关联要素构成的设计空间映射框架，并阐述各要素的内涵、相互影响关系及其设计启示。

Result: 构建了一个六要素框架（社会技术背景、决策框架、人类决策参与者、AI能力、交互、整体评估），为从业者、研究者和评估者分别提供了共享词汇、分析视角和评估起点。

Conclusion: 弥合人机协同鸿沟需要超越仅聚焦准确性的评估方式，明确地从更广泛的设计空间出发，系统性考虑社会技术背景、决策框架、人类参与者、AI能力、交互设计及整体评估等多维要素。

Abstract: AI is now embedded in healthcare, finance, policy, and many other domains, yet genuine human-AI synergy - combined performance that exceeds what either party achieves alone - is uncommon. Meta-analyses show that AI assistance tends to improve human performance compared to working alone, but studies finding true synergy are scarce. We call this persistent shortfall the synergy gap. Most current work treats human-AI combination as an engineering problem and concentrates on interpretability, trust calibration, or interface design. These matter, but they cover only part of what determines whether combination works. Closing the synergy gap, we argue, requires explicit engagement with a wider design space. We map that space through six interconnected elements: sociotechnical context, decision-making frameworks, human decision participants, AI capabilities, interaction, and holistic evaluation. For each element, we describe what it covers, how it shapes the others in practice, and what it implies for design. The result is a shared vocabulary for practitioners building hybrid systems, an analytical lens for researchers studying combination patterns, and a starting point for evaluators interested in the full quality of human-AI decision-making rather than accuracy alone.

</details>


### [23] [Understanding Perspectives of Patients, Caregivers and Clinicians towards Emerging Collaborative-decision Making Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21777)
*Ray-Yuan Chung,Athena Ortega,Zixuan Xu,Daeun Yoo,Jaime Snyder,Wanda Pratt,Aaron Wightman,Ryan Hutson,Cozumel Pruette,Ari Pollack*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过定性方法，探讨了儿科医疗决策中患者、照护者与临床医生对协作决策技术（交互式仪表盘、VR模拟器、AI语音助手）的看法，发现用户意见存在群体差异，且技术接受度与用户信任度密切相关，需在设计与实施中建立信任或适当的不信任以支持有效协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在儿科医疗中，患者、照护者和临床医生共同承担健康决策责任，但合作不足会影响治疗结果，因此需要探索如何通过技术促进协作决策。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，考察不同决策者（患者、照护者、临床医生）对交互式仪表盘、VR模拟器和AI语音助手三类协作决策技术的感知与看法。

Result: 不同用户群体对技术的意见存在差异；技术接受度与用户对技术的信任程度密切相关。

Conclusion: 在技术能够有效支持协作决策之前，技术开发者和研究者需探索设计与实施策略，以建立并促进用户与这些新型技术之间的信任或适当的不信任。

Abstract: In pediatrics, patients, caregivers, and clinicians share responsibility for health decisions, but limited collaboration can undermine outcomes. We conducted a qualitative study examining decision-makers perceptions toward collaborative decision-making technologies, including interactive dashboards, VR simulators, and AI voice assistants. Findings reveal differences in user opinions across groups and indicate technology acceptance is linked to users trust of these technologies. Technology developers and researchers need to explore design and implementation strategies that build and facilitate trust or appropriate distrust between users and these novel technologies before these tools can effectively support collaborative decision-making.

</details>


### [24] [Narrative Sharpens Gender Gaps: Surveying Film Characters with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22091)
*Vivienne Bihe Chi,Reyhan Jamalova,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过将电影角色转化为可调查的LLM智能体，研究发现电影叙事会放大性别态度差异，且这种刻板化在AI模型训练前就已存在于语料中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流电影是AI系统学习的重要文化内容来源，但现有工具缺乏对其中编码的性别价值观进行量化测量的能力。

Method: 构建概念验证框架，将160部美国电影（1990-2019）中的734个虚构角色通过剧本对话和场景描述转化为LLM智能体，通过专家式反思凝练人格，模拟世界价值观调查的性别态度回应。

Result: 智能体无需显式人口统计提示即可复现系统性性别差异；与历史调查数据相比，智能体夸大了性别差距，且年代间波动更大；叙事强化而非均质化了性别对比。

Conclusion: 电影叙事中的性别刻板化在AI模型层面的放大之前就已存在于训练语料中，这对培养理论的"主流化"机制中关于一致性输入的假设提出了挑战。

Abstract: Mainstream film is one of the richest sources of cultural content that AI systems learn from. Yet we have few tools for measuring the gender values it encodes. We present a proof-of-concept framework that turns fictional film characters into surveyable LLM agents. Using 160 U.S. films (1990--2019), we build 734 character agents from script dialogue and scene descriptions, condense their personas via expert-style reflections, and simulate World Values Survey gender-attitude responses. Agents reproduce systematic gender differences without explicit demographic prompting, suggesting attitudes emerge from behavior rather than identity labels. Benchmarked against historical survey data, agents exaggerate gender gaps and show greater decade-to-decade volatility than real populations. Narrative sharpens rather than homogenizes gender contrasts, complicating the consistent-input assumption underlying cultivation theory's mainstreaming mechanism. AI systems trained on such corpora may inherit this stylization before any model-level amplification occurs.

</details>


### [25] [Cross-Subject EEG Emotion Recognition Based on Temporal Asynchronous Alignment Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22379)
*Ying Xie,Yi Zheng,Zehui Xiao,Wenkai Lu,Mengting Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时序异步对齐的对比学习框架（TA2CL），借鉴NLP中ColBERT的延迟交互机制，将传统的全局硬对齐相似度计算改为细粒度局部匹配，以解决不同被试间EEG信号的时序不对齐问题，在多个公开数据集上取得了良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG情感识别方法多聚焦于优化编码器结构以增强特征提取能力，但对相似度计算策略关注不足，尤其忽略了不同被试间响应存在的潜在时序不对齐问题。

Method: 受NLP中ColBERT的延迟交互机制启发，提出TA2CL框架，将传统全局"硬对齐"相似度计算转变为细粒度局部匹配机制，使模型能够自适应搜索并对齐两个EEG信号中"局部高度相关"的片段。

Result: 在FACED数据集上，九分类任务准确率64.5%，二分类任务准确率79.5%；在SEED和SEED-V数据集上分别达到86.4%和70.1%的准确率。

Conclusion: 所提方法在多个公开数据集上均取得优异表现，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: With the advancement of science and technology, the importance of emotion research has become increasingly evident. Electroencephalography (EEG)-based emotion recognition has emerged as an active research area in recent years, owing to its objectivity and high temporal resolution. However, most existing methods focus on optimizing encoder structures to enhance feature extraction capabilities, while paying relatively little attention to similarity calculation strategies, particularly overlooking the potential temporal misalignment of responses among different subjects. To address these shortcomings, this paper draws inspiration from the late interaction mechanism of ColBERT in natural language processing (NLP) and proposes a Temporal Asynchronous Alignment-based Contrastive Learning (TA2CL) framework. This method transforms the traditional global "hard alignment" similarity calculation approach into a fine-grained local matching mechanism, enabling the model to adaptively search for and align "locally highly correlated" segments between two EEG signals, thereby effectively mitigating the effects of inter-subject differences and temporal delays. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves strong performance across multiple public datasets. Specifically, on the FACED dataset, it achieves an accuracy of 64.5% for the nine-class classification task and 79.5% for the binary classification task, while on the SEED and SEED-V datasets, it achieves accuracies of 86.4% and 70.1%, respectively, validating the method's effectiveness and generalization capability.

</details>


### [26] [Perceived Safety of Workers in Encounters with Large Industrial AGVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22461)
*Ansgar Howey,Tim Schreiter,Andrey Rudenko,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过真实场景和虚拟现实实验，研究了工业工人在与大型AGV共享空间时的感知安全性，发现1.5-2米的通行距离最受偏好，且VR中感知威胁略高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究存在三个局限：大载荷AGV研究不足、被试多为学生而非工业工人、模拟实验的安全性感知结果能否迁移到真实场景尚不明确。本文旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 在真实场景和虚拟现实中，改变AGV的通行距离和避障轨迹形状，使用手持压力感应触发界面和问卷评估感知威胁等级，并让被试根据体验自行设定避障参数。

Result: VR中的威胁感知略高于真实场景；1.5-2米的通行距离在预设轨迹和自定轨迹中均最受偏好。

Conclusion: 研究为大型AGV与工人共享空间的安全交互参数提供了实证依据，验证了VR作为研究工具的可行性，并指出了真实与虚拟环境感知差异的细微之处。

Abstract: Automated Guided Vehicles (AGV) in factory automation are increasingly capable of moving autonomously in close proximity to human workers. While their physical safety is regulated by standards and directives, perceived safety and workers comfort in close-proximity interactions are being actively investigated in studies. There are three limitations in the prior art research to that end. Firstly, AGVs with larger payloads are understudied. Secondly, the test participants are usually students and not working professionals. Thirdly, while conducting in-person experiments with heavy machinery can be dangerous, the transfer of safety perception results from simulated experiments remains open. In this paper, we investigate industrial workers perceived safety in shared spaces with large AGVs in a real-world encounter and in virtual reality. We vary the passing distance and the shape of the collision avoidance maneuver, and evaluate perceived threat level using a handheld pressure-sensitive trigger interface and a post-experiment questionnaire. Additionally, we ask participants to set their own collision avoidance parameters based on their experience with the demonstrated trajectory profiles. In a within-subject study, we found that, while the threat levels are perceived overall slightly higher in VR, the passing distance of 1.5 to 2 meters is preferred among the demonstrated profiles, as well as in the self-defined trajectories.

</details>


### [27] [Summarizing Time-Varying Digital Image Correlation Strain Fields Using Sankey Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22627)
*Victor Persson,Christofer Boo,Mohit Sharma,Ingrid Hotz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Sankey图的可视化摘要方法，通过追踪冯·米塞斯等效应变场的超水平集连通分量随时间的变化，将高应变区域的演化（出现、持续、合并、消失）压缩为单个图表，从而补充传统空间应变可视化，提供全局时间概览。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DIC应变场分析主要依赖逐帧空间可视化，难以识别全局时间模式，因此需要一种能够紧凑呈现应变浓度演化过程的可视化摘要方法。

Method: 从冯·米塞斯等效应变场构建超水平集，通过空间重叠追踪连通分量随时间的变化，将高应变区域的演化编码为Sankey图，记录应变的出现、持续、合并和消失事件。

Result: 在四种不同缺口几何形状的拉伸测试数据集上，该方法紧凑地捕获了变形模式的差异和失效前的定性前兆，补充了传统的空间应变可视化。

Conclusion: 该Sankey图可视化方法为DIC应变场分析提供了有效的全局时间概览，有助于理解应变局部化和失效机制的时间演化规律。

Abstract: Digital Image Correlation (DIC) enables dense, time-resolved measurement of surface strain in deforming materials, providing insight into strain localization and failure mechanisms. However, the resulting strain fields are typically explored frame-by-frame through spatial visualizations, making global temporal patterns difficult to discern. We present a visual summarization approach that represents the evolution of high-strain regions as a single Sankey diagram constructed from superlevel sets of the von Mises equivalent strain field. By tracking connected components over time via spatial overlap, the diagram encodes the birth, persistence, merging, and disappearance of strain concentrations. Applied to four tensile test datasets with varying notch geometries, the approach compactly captures differences in deformation regimes and qualitative precursors to failure, complementing traditional spatial strain visualizations with a global temporal overview.

</details>


### [28] [Student programming behavior with and without phone notification suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22657)
*Gavin Eddington,Christopher Warren,Seth Poulsen,John Edwards*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过CS1课程中22名学生的实验，发现启用手机通知抑制（如勿扰模式）能显著降低编程中的中断频率并延长专注时长，但效果呈现双峰分布——多数学生受益，少数学生反而受负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程需要持续专注，而手机通知频繁打断。先前研究表明抑制通知可能减少干扰，但缺乏在真实编程环境中的量化证据。

Method: 在CS1课程中对22名学生采用被试内设计，随机指定编程作业启用/不启用通知抑制。同步采集手机状态日志与毫秒级IDE键盘输入数据，测量注意力和专注度。

Result: 通知抑制条件下，学生的中断率显著降低，专注间隔延长。但效果呈现双峰分布：多数学生积极受益，少数学生受到负面影响，极少数无显著变化。

Conclusion: 对多数学生而言，手机通知抑制工具（如勿扰模式）能改善编程时的注意力与专注度。研究结果对教育干预（将勿扰模式作为干预手段）和学术研究（理解手机分心效应）具有启示意义。

Abstract: Background and Context. Computer programming often involves extended periods of sustained activity and mobile phone notifications introduce frequent opportunities for interruption. Prior work demonstrates that suppressing phone notifications may reduce these disruptions.
  Objectives. Our primary research question is: How does suppressing phone notifications affect students' task engagement and productivity while programming?
  Method. We report on a replication and methodological extension study conducted in a CS1 course involving 22 students. Using a within-subject design, selected programming assignments were randomly designated for enabling notification suppression. Phone state logs were synchronized with millisecond-resolution IDE keystroke data to measure student attention and focus when in the control and notification-suppression conditions.
  Findings. Assignments completed with notification suppression enabled significantly lower break rates and longer intervals of focus compared to assignments completed in the control condition for many, but not all, students. This study provides evidence that notification suppression is associated with measurable differences in programming engagement and behavior. We also find a remarkable bimodality in the effect across students -- many students are positively affected, a small number are negatively affected, and very few experience little or no effect. This finding is consistent with other studies in diverse disciplines.
  Implications. Our results show that, for many students, phone notification suppression tools, such as Do Not Disturb, can improve attention and focus. Implications apply to educational settings (do-not-disturb as an intervention) and scholarship (understanding the effects of phone distraction).

</details>

### [29] [The Neural Compiler: Program-to-Network Translation for Hybrid Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22498)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Neural Compiler 是一个将类Scheme一阶表达式语言编写的程序翻译为冻结且可微的PyTorch模块的系统。编译后的模块精确匹配源程序的浮点精度，并通过autograd提供梯度。在混合模型中，编译模块精确编码已知物理定律，而学习组件建模未知余项。在六个实验领域（费曼物理方程、Lotka-Volterra动力学、阻尼摆、一维热方程、三维向量力学、组合泛化）的评估显示，编译模块在数值精度上与手写PyTorch实现一致，且仅用1-4个可训练参数即可高精度恢复物理常数，而标准PINN基线（>8500参数）误差达7-93%。编译器的主要价值在于系统性可组合性，无需为每个方程手写代码即可生成正确的可微模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习常需将已知物理知识与数据驱动的学习相结合，但现有方法要么忽略已知结构，要么将其编码为软惩罚，要么需要为每个方程手写PyTorch代码，缺乏系统性和可组合性。

Method: 提出Neural Compiler系统，将类Scheme一阶表达式语言编写的程序翻译为冻结且可微的PyTorch模块，通过autograd提供梯度，支持51种原始操作（包括向量和矩阵代数），实现符号规范到可微模块的自动化编译。

Result: 在六个实验领域中，编译模块与手写PyTorch实现数值精度一致；仅用1-4个可训练参数即可将物理常数恢复误差控制在1%以内，而标准PINN基线（>8500参数）误差达7-93%；编译模块组合误差为零，而神经近似在深层组合中累积大误差。

Conclusion: Neural Compiler的主要价值在于系统性可组合性：无需手写方程即可从符号规范生成正确的可微模块，其"字符串输入-模块输出"接口也为大语言模型将科学描述翻译为可执行可微模块提供了自然目标。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning often requires combining known physics with unknown parameters or correction terms learned from data. Existing approaches either ignore known structure, encode it as a soft penalty, or require hand-written PyTorch code for each equation. We present The Neural Compiler, a system that translates programs written in a first-order Scheme-like expression language into frozen, differentiable PyTorch modules. These modules match the source program to floating-point precision and provide gradients through autograd. In hybrid models, the compiled module encodes known physics exactly while learned components model the unknown remainder. We evaluate the compiler across six experiment domains: Feynman physics equations, Lotka-Volterra dynamics, a damped pendulum, a one-dimensional heat equation, three-dimensional vector mechanics, and compositional generalization. Compiled modules match hand-coded PyTorch implementations numerically for single equations, showing no accuracy loss from compilation. With only 1 to 4 trainable parameters, compiled models recover physical constants to less than 1 percent error in most cases, while standard PINN baselines with more than 8500 parameters show 7 to 93 percent error. Compiled modules also compose with zero error, while neural approximations can accumulate large errors in deep composition chains. The main value of the compiler is not improved accuracy over hand-coded equations, but systematic composability: it generates correct, differentiable modules from symbolic specifications without rewriting each equation by hand. The system supports 51 primitive operations, including vector and matrix algebra, enabling PDE discretizations and hybrid scientific models. This string-in, module-out interface also provides a natural target for large language models that translate scientific descriptions into executable differentiable modules.

</details>


### [30] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT)，一种用于金融交易序列表征学习的自监督对比学习框架。实验表明，单独使用TCT嵌入可取得有意义的预测性能（AUC 0.8644），但与领域工程特征结合时未见提升（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），说明学习到的表示与现有特征抽象存在较大重叠。该工作处于时序表征学习在金融犯罪检测中的中间阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少金融犯罪检测中对人工特征工程的依赖，通过自监督表征学习自动捕捉金融交易序列中的行为模式和时序动态。

Method: 提出Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT)，利用Transformer架构捕捉时序动态，通过自监督对比学习目标训练得到交易序列的嵌入表示，将嵌入作为梯度提升分类器的输入特征用于欺诈检测。

Result: TCT嵌入单独使用AUC为0.8644，表明模型捕捉到了有意义的时序结构；但与领域工程特征结合后AUC为0.9205（基线AUC 0.9245），未观察到可测量的提升。

Conclusion: TCT作为表征学习方法能够捕捉相关行为信号，但实现超越强领域特征的增量价值仍具挑战。达到与强特征工程基线相当的性能本身是有意义的成果，表明学习到的表示无需手动工程即可近似领域特定特征，为减少特征工程依赖提供了有希望的方向。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [31] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了语言模型在无需实验的情况下预测研究想法成功与否的能力。通过构建11,488对基于PapersWithCode客观结果的想法对数据集，发现8B参数的小模型经过SFT后准确率达77.1%，超越GPT-5的61.1%；通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）将评估建模为推理任务，达到71.35%准确率且可生成可解释理由。结果表明计算高效的小型语言模型可作为有效的客观验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型加速科学研究，自动生成和实现假设，出现了新的瓶颈：需要在不进行详尽实验的情况下评估和筛选数百个AI生成的想法。因此，研究者希望探索语言模型是否能在任何实验运行之前预测研究想法的实证成功。

Method: 构建了基于PapersWithCode的11,488个想法对数据集，研究比较性实证预测任务。采用两种训练范式：1) 监督微调（SFT）；2) 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），将评估建模为推理任务，让模型发现潜在推理路径。

Result: 离线8B参数模型仅30%准确率；SFT后提升至77.1%，超越GPT-5的61.1%；RLVR方法达到71.35%准确率，同时生成可解释的理由。模型对表层启发式具有鲁棒性，并在跨域时间分割测试集和独立构建的测试集上均表现出迁移能力。

Conclusion: 计算高效的小型语言模型可以作为有效的、客观的验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展的路径。

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [32] [Can Transformers Learn to Verify During Backtracking Search?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22221)
*Yin Jun Phua,Tony Ribeiro,Tuan Nguyen,Katsumi Inoue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于累计轨迹训练的因果解码器Transformer在回溯搜索任务中存在"分散检索"和"历史纠缠"两个根本性问题，并提出局部化处理和选择性状态注意力机制（SSA）作为结构性修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer推理系统使用离线求解器轨迹进行自回归训练，模型输入是累积的完整决策历史。但最优的"继续或回溯"预测应仅依赖于当前搜索状态，而累积因果训练方式导致模型无法满足这一要求。

Method: 提出两种修复方法：(1) 局部化（localization），在trace级别重写每个决策块，使状态特征在局部位置暴露；(2) 选择性状态注意力（SSA），一种固定注意力掩码，在不修改训练数据、目标或参数的情况下，从结构上强制执行基于状态的决策。

Result: 在3-SAT、图着色、积木世界和回溯解析四个任务上验证，对于仅在历史轨迹上不同但状态相同的样本对，SSA能够输出一致的决策，而基于累积训练的因果基线则不能。

Conclusion: 本文诊断了Transformer在序列化轨迹数据上的行为缺陷并提供了结构性修复方案，指出预训练语言模型在自身推理步骤上进行搜索时可能面临同样问题，并提出了推理时上下文清除作为无需重新训练的应用方式。

Abstract: Backtracking search underlies classical constraint solvers, planners, and theorem provers. Recent transformer-based reasoning systems explore search trees over their own intermediate steps. A common training recipe fits an autoregressive next-token loss on offline solver traces. The model's input at each step is a cumulative trace of all prior decisions. The optimal continue-or-backtrack predictor depends only on the current search state, since two trajectories reaching the same state admit the same viable continuations. We show that decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative traces fail this requirement in two ways: the trace can scatter state features across many positions (scattered retrieval), and the predictor can condition on the trajectory rather than the state (history entanglement). We address scattered retrieval with localization, a trace-level fix that rewrites each decision block to expose state features locally. We address history entanglement with Selective State Attention (SSA), a fixed attention mask that enforces state-based decisions structurally without modifying training data, objective, or parameters. We focus on reactive verification, after propagation has exposed a contradiction. We test SSA on 3-SAT, graph coloring, Blocks World, and backtracking parsing. On same-state pairs that differ only in prior history, SSA emits identical decisions while a cumulative-trained causal baseline does not. Our contribution is a diagnostic of transformer behavior on serialized trajectory data, paired with a structural fix. Pretrained language models that search over their own reasoning steps may face the same failure. Our analysis opens up inference-time context clearing as a candidate way to apply the same isolation without retraining.

</details>


### [33] [What are the Right Symmetries for Formal Theorem Proving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22257)
*Krzysztof Olejniczak,Radoslav Dimitrov,Xingyue Huang,Bernardo Cuenca Grau,Jinwoo Kim,İsmail İlkan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出重写范畴（rewriting categories）这一范畴论框架，用于形式化形式定理证明中的对称性概念，发现基于LLM的定理证明器缺乏对称性归纳偏置，并提出测试时聚合等效重写的方法来提升鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的形式定理证明器对问题表示的表面变化高度敏感，语义等价的语句可能具有截然不同的证明成功率，表明模型未能尊重形式数学中固有的结构对称性。

Method: 引入重写范畴（rewriting categories）框架，形式化两种对称性：证明等变性（proof equivariance）和成功不变性（success invariance）。提出测试时聚合方法，对输入进行多种等效重写并聚合结果。

Result: 基于状态的下一个策略（state-based next-tactic）证明器自然满足证明等变性，但最先进的LLM证明器两种对称性均不满足。提出的测试时聚合方法在理论上能在采样极限下恢复成功不变性，实验上在固定推理预算下提升了鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: 对称性是LLM定理证明中缺失的关键归纳偏置，测试时计算是近似实现对称性的实用途径。

Abstract: Formal theorem provers based on large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements can exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises a central question: what are the right symmetries for formal theorem proving? We introduce rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing the compositional, generally non-invertible transformations induced by proof tactics, and use it to formalize two symmetry notions: proof equivariance, governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and success invariance (i.e., invariance of success probability), requiring equivalent statements to be solved with the same probability. We observe that state-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither property, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations. To mitigate this, we propose test-time methods that aggregate over equivalent rewritings of the input, showing theoretically that they recover success invariance in the sampling limit, and empirically, that they improve robustness and performance under fixed inference budgets. Our results highlight symmetry as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving and suggest test-time computation as a practical route to approximate it.

</details>

*Rahul D Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GOEN是一种结合多尺度特征、L2归一化、马氏距离和校准头的OOD检测方法。研究发现CenterLoss虽然能提升分类准确率，但会显著降低OOD检测性能。最优变体GOEN-NoCenterLoss在CIFAR-10上达到0.9483 OOD AUROC，超越深度集成、KNN和ODIN等基线方法，训练时间不到20分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法通常使用仅针对分类准确率优化的特征表示，忽略了认知不确定性的独特需求。本文旨在设计一种既高效又有效的OOD检测方法，并探究特征几何结构对OOD检测性能的影响。

Method: 提出GOEN流水线，包含多尺度特征提取、L2归一化、马氏距离计算和基于真实困难OOD样本训练的校准头。通过系统性消融实验分析CenterLoss等组件的影响。

Result: CenterLoss将OOD AUROC从0.9483降至0.9366；GOEN-NoCenterLoss达到0.9483，超越深度集成(0.8827)、KNN(0.8967)和ODIN(0.8870)；单GPU训练时间低于20分钟。

Conclusion: 更好的分类几何结构并不自动带来更好的认知不确定性估计。过紧的特征簇会压缩类间间隔并扭曲协方差结构，损害OOD检测性能。GOEN为构建可靠识别自身局限性的AI系统提供了实用蓝图。

Abstract: The ability to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs is fundamental to safe deployment of machine learning systems. Yet, current methods often rely on feature representations that are optimised solely for classification accuracy, neglecting the distinct requirements of epistemic uncertainty. We introduce GOEN (Geometry-Optimised Epistemic Network), a simple pipeline that combines multi-scale features, L2 normalisation, Mahalanobis distance, and a calibration head trained with real hard OOD examples. Through systematic ablation we uncover a counter-intuitive finding: CenterLoss, a popular regulariser for feature compactness, significantly degrades OOD detection performance, reducing average OOD AUROC from 0.9483 to 0.9366 despite improving classification accuracy. The best variant, GOEN-NoCenterLoss, achieves an average OOD AUROC of 0.9483, surpassing all baselines including deep ensembles (0.8827), KNN (0.8967), and ODIN (0.8870) on CIFAR-10 benchmarks, while maintaining competitive in-distribution accuracy. Our results challenge the prevailing assumption that better classification geometry automatically leads to better epistemic uncertainty. Instead, we show that overly tight feature clusters compress inter-class margins and distort the covariance structure needed for effective OOD detection. GOEN is efficient, training in under 20 minutes on a single GPU, and provides a practical blueprint for building AI systems that reliably recognise their own limitations.

</details>

*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将五种学习规则（BP、FA、PC、STDP、随机基线）在相同模型权重下跨物种比较（人类fMRI vs 猕猴电生理），发现：早期视觉皮层（V1/V2）的脑对齐跨物种稳健（电生理信噪比更高导致相关性更高），STDP和PC表现最优；高级视觉区（IT）的学习规则排名跨物种无显著相关（Kendall's tau=0.00），且IT对齐主要受模型容量和训练数据限制而非学习规则（预训练ResNet-50显著优于自定义CNN）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究学习规则与脑对齐之间的关系是否跨物种泛化，即在人类fMRI发现的规律能否在猕猴电生理数据中复现。

Method: 使用相同模型权重（来自人类研究的五种学习规则CNN），对猕猴两个电生理数据集（MajajHong2015 V4/IT和FreemanZiemba2013 V1/V2）进行表征相似性分析（RSA），并加入预训练ResNet-50对比，控制噪声上限、多种子变异性（5 seeds）和刺激集差异。
