Result: （1）所有模型在猕猴早期视觉皮层的对齐度（rho=0.15-0.30）高于人类fMRI（rho=0.01-0.08）；（2）STDP和PC在猕猴V1/V2对齐最高（rho~0.30/0.28），与人类V1排名一致；（3）IT层学习规则排名跨物种无相关（Kendall's tau=0.00, p=1.00），但统计效力不足（n=5）且受刺激集差异干扰；（4）预训练ResNet-50在猕猴IT达到rho=0.25，远超自定义CNN（rho=0.07-0.14）。

Conclusion: 早期视觉对齐跨物种稳健，但高级视觉区对齐主要受模型容量和训练数据域限制，而非学习规则本身。

Abstract: Does the relationship between learning rules and brain alignment generalize across species? We extend our prior finding that untrained CNNs match backpropagation at human V1 by testing the same five learning rules against macaque electrophysiology. The rules are backpropagation (BP), feedback alignment (FA), predictive coding (PC), spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), and an untrained random-weights baseline. The macaque data come from two datasets: MajajHong2015 (V4/IT, 3,200 stimulus presentations, 88/168 neurons) and FreemanZiemba2013 (V1/V2, 135 stimuli, 102/103 neurons). Using RSA with identical model weights from our human study, we find: (1) all models achieve higher alignment with macaque early visual cortex (rho = 0.15-0.30 at V1/V2) than with human fMRI (rho = 0.01-0.08), consistent with the higher signal-to-noise ratio of electrophysiology; (2) STDP and PC produce the highest macaque V1/V2 alignment (rho ~ 0.30 and 0.28), consistent with their leading position among trained rules in human V1; (3) at IT, learning rule rankings show no detectable correlation across species (Kendall's tau = 0.00, p = 1.00), though this null result is expected given that n = 5 provides power only at tau = +/-1.0, and is further confounded by stimulus set differences; (4) a pretrained ResNet-50 (ImageNet) achieves rho = 0.25 at macaque IT, substantially above all custom CNN conditions (rho = 0.07-0.14), suggesting IT alignment is limited by model capacity and training data rather than by the learning rule. Noise ceilings, multi-seed variability (5 seeds), and a stimulus-control analysis are reported. These results demonstrate that early visual alignment is robust across species, while higher-area alignment is modulated by model capacity and stimulus domain.

</details>


### [36] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限折扣MDP中基于风险敏感的强化学习问题，假设存在MDP的生成模型。针对优化确定性等价（OCE）风险度量族（包括熵风险、CVaR、均值-方差等），分析了在递归OCE下学习最优状态-动作价值函数（价值学习）和最优策略（策略学习）的样本复杂度。给出了效用函数u使得对应OCE目标可PAC学习的精确刻画，证明当u的定义域不为全体实数时问题不可PAC学习，并建立了紧的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感强化学习在实际应用中非常重要，但现有工作对OCE风险度量族的样本复杂度缺乏系统性的理论分析，特别是对于递归OCE框架下的价值学习和策略学习的PAC可学习性边界尚不清楚。

Method: 采用基于生成模型的模型驱动方法，对OCE风险度量族下的强化学习问题进行PAC样本复杂度分析。通过精确刻画效用函数u的数学性质与可学习性之间的关系，建立上下界匹配的理论结果。

Result: (1) 给出了u使得OCE目标PAC可学习的充要条件；(2) 当dom(u)≠R时问题不可PAC学习；(3) 对价值学习和策略学习都建立了紧的样本复杂度下界，其中SA依赖是紧的；(4) 对CVaR_τ，将τ的依赖改进为1/τ²，比现有最优结果提升了一个1/τ因子，但1/(1-γ)的依赖非最优。

Conclusion: 本文为递归OCE风险度量下的强化学习提供了系统的样本复杂度理论分析，揭示了效用函数定义域与可学习性之间的本质联系，并在CVaR情形下取得了依赖关系的实质性改进。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [37] [Double descent for least-squares interpolation on contaminated data: A simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21494)
*Tino Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在含有污染数据的线性回归中，高度过参数化的最小二乘插值估计器展现出“双重下降”现象，其泛化性能甚至超越了鲁棒估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在训练数据被污染（存在异常值）的情况下，过参数化模型是否仍会出现双重下降现象，以及非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器与鲁棒替代方法的性能对比。

Method: 在含有污染数据的线性回归设定下，比较高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器与多种鲁棒替代方法的泛化性能，并观察双重下降现象是否出现。

Result: 大幅度过参数化确实引发了双重下降现象，使得最小二乘插值估计器展现出非常优异的泛化性能，甚至超过了鲁棒替代方法。

Conclusion: 过参数化不仅不会加剧过拟合，反而在污染数据场景下能通过双重下降使非鲁棒估计器获得超越鲁棒方法的泛化能力。

Abstract: Overparametrized models can exhibit an excellent generalization performance, although they should be prone to overfitting according to classical statistical theory. The discovery of the "double descent", indicating that the generalization error decreases after a certain model complexity has been reached, opened a new line of research. Robust statistics considers statistical estimation on contaminated data, which, due to assumptions that do not hold on real data, let data points appear as outliers w.r.t. the assumed "ideal" distribution, potentially severely distorting any classical estimator. We address the question whether a double descent phenomenon can be observed in a linear regression setting with contaminated training data. We compare the performance of the highly non-robust least-squares interpolation estimator with several robust alternatives. It turns out that large overparametrization indeed allows for a double descent phenomenon, resulting in a very good generalization performance of the least-squares interpolator, surpassing that of the robust alternatives.

</details>


### [38] [Vector Policy Optimization: Training for Diversity Improves Test-Time Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22817)
*Ryan Bahlous-Boldi,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Akarsh Kumar,Mehul Damani,Sebastian Risi,Omar Khattab,Zhang-Wei Hong,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为向量策略优化（VPO）的强化学习算法，该算法通过显式训练语言模型生成多样化解决方案以适配不同奖励函数，从而提升推理时搜索（如AlphaEvolve）的性能，在四个任务上匹配或超越标量RL基线，且差距随搜索预算增加而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM后训练范式优化的是预设标量奖励，导致模型产生低熵响应分布，缺乏推理时搜索所需的多

Method: VPO利用奖励通常是向量值这一特点（如代码生成中每个测试用例的正确性、多个用户画像或奖励模型），作为GRPO优势估计器的即插即用替代方案，训练LLM输出一组解决方案，每个解决方案专门针对向量奖励空间中的不同权衡。

Result: 在四个任务上，VPO在测试时搜索（如pass@k和best@k）上匹配或超越最强标量RL基线，差距随搜索预算增加而扩大。在进化搜索中，VPO模型能解决GRPO模型完全无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 随着测试时搜索日益标准化，优化多样性可能需成为默认的后训练目标。

Abstract: Language models must now generalize out of the box to novel environments and work inside inference-scaling search procedures, such as AlphaEvolve, that select rollouts with a variety of task-specific reward functions. Unfortunately, the standard paradigm of LLM post-training optimizes a pre-specified scalar reward, often leading current LLMs to produce low-entropy response distributions and thus to struggle at displaying the diversity that inference-time search will require. We propose Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL algorithm that explicitly trains policies to anticipate diverse downstream reward functions and to produce diverse solutions. VPO exploits that rewards are often vector-valued in practice, like per-test-case correctness in code generation or, say, multiple different user personas or reward models. VPO is essentially a drop-in replacement for the GRPO advantage estimator, but it trains the LLM to output a set of solutions where individual solutions specialize to different trade-offs in the vector reward space. Across four tasks, VPO matches or beats the strongest scalar RL baselines on test-time search (e.g. pass@k and best@k), with the gap widening as the search budget grows. For evolutionary search, VPO models unlock problems that GRPO models cannot solve at all. As test-time search becomes more standardized, optimizing for diversity may need to become the default post-training objective.

</details>


### [39] [HealthCraft: A Reinforcement Learning Safety Environment for Emergency Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21496)
*Brandon Dent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HealthCraft 是首个基于 FHIR R4 标准、面向急诊医学的强化学习环境，通过双层奖励规则（安全关键违规即零奖励）评估语言模型在轨迹级别的安全性与工具使用能力。在 205 个任务上测试表明，前沿模型（Claude Opus 4.6 Pass@1 24.8%，GPT-5.4 12.6%）在多步骤工作流中表现崩溃（接近 0%），且安全失败率高达 27.5%-34.0%。环境、任务和评估框架已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型正被部署到临床流程中，但缺乏足够的基础设施来安全评估它们。静态医疗问答基准无法捕捉急诊医学中至关重要的失败模式：轨迹级别的安全性崩溃、工具误用以及在持续临床压力下的妥协行为。

Method: 基于 Corecraft 改编，构建在 FHIR R4 世界状态上（14 种实体类型、3,987 个种子实体），暴露 24 个 MCP 工具。定义双层奖励规则（dual-layer rubric）：一旦违反任何安全关键标准即奖励归零。包含 195 个任务（后扩展至 205 个），共 2,337 个二值评估标准（其中 515 个为安全关键）。采用确定性 LLM 评判覆盖层来限制评估者噪声。

Result: 在 V8 版本测试中，Claude Opus 4.6 Pass@1 为 24.8% [21.5-28.4]，GPT-5.4 为 12.6% [10.2-15.6]；安全失败率分别为 27.5% 和 34.0%。多步骤工作流中性能接近零（Claude 1.0%, GPT-5.4 0.0%）。版本间基础设施修复（v2→v8 共 6 个 bug）改变了模型排名，证明基础设施保真度会影响评估结果。负类烟雾测试显示安全约束的通过率为 0.929，表明奖励信号可直接用于评估但不适合直接作为训练奖励。

Conclusion: 现有前沿模型在模拟急诊医学环境中表现堪忧，尤其是在需要多步骤协作的真实工作流中性能崩溃。环境开源有助于推动临床 AI 安全评估研究，但模型作为训练奖励的适用性仍需进一步消融实验验证。

Abstract: Frontier language models are being deployed into clinical workflows faster than the infrastructure to evaluate them safely. Static medical-QA benchmarks miss the failure modes that matter in emergency medicine: trajectory-level safety collapse, tool misuse, and capitulation under sustained clinical pressure. We present HealthCraft, the first public reinforcement-learning environment that rewards trajectory-level safety under realistic emergency-medicine conditions, adapted from Corecraft. It is built on a FHIR R4 world state with 14 entity types and 3,987 seed entities, exposes 24 MCP tools, and defines a dual-layer rubric that zeroes reward whenever any safety-critical criterion is violated. We release 195 tasks across six categories, graded against 2,255 binary criteria (515 safety-critical); a post-hoc 10-task negative-class slate extends this to 205 tasks and 2,337 criteria. V8 results on two frontier models show Claude Opus 4.6 at Pass@1 24.8% [21.5-28.4] and GPT-5.4 at 12.6% [10.2-15.6], with safety-failure rates of 27.5% and 34.0%. On multi-step workflows - the closest proxy to real emergency care - performance collapses to near zero (Claude 1.0%, GPT-5.4 0.0%) despite partial competence on individual steps. Six infrastructure bugs fixed between pilots v2 and v8 re-ordered which model "looks stronger," evidence that infrastructure fidelity is part of the measurement. A deterministic LLM-judge overlay bounds evaluator noise, and a 60-run negative-class smoke pilot shows the reward signal is not drop-in training-safe: restraint criteria pass at 0.929 prevalence, a gameability an eval harness can tolerate but a training reward cannot. We scaffold coupling to a Megatron+SGLang+GRPO loop per Corecraft Section 5.2 and leave training-reward ablations as future work. Environment, tasks, rubrics, and harness are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [40] [Harnesses for Inference-Time Alignment over Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21516)
*Boyuan Wang,Bochao Li,Minghan Wang,Yuxin Tao,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从推理时轨迹对齐的视角研究LLM Agent的harness设计，将harness分解为任务分解和引导执行两种机制，量化了工作流粒度、重试预算和引导动作重加权对性能的影响，揭示了过分解、过剪枝和幻觉执行等失效模式，并发现仅指定初始步骤的部分引导harness可能比完整结构化工作流获得更高通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent的harness工程技术（任务分解与引导执行）在实际应用中并非越复杂越好，有时增加分解或引导反而会降低任务成功率，缺乏统一的量化分析框架来理解这种非单调性能变化及其背后的失效机制。

Method: 提出推理时轨迹对齐（inference-time trajectory alignment）视角，将harness形式化为任务分解（将任务结构化为子目标）和引导执行（在执行过程中重塑局部动作分布）两种机制的分离，并通过量化工作流粒度、重试预算和引导动作重加权来推导性能极限，结合合成实验和真实终端Agent基准进行验证。

Result: 揭示了过分解（over-decomposition）、过剪枝（over-pruning）和幻觉执行（hallucinated execution）三种具体失效模式；发现部分引导的harness（仅指定初始步骤）可以比完整结构化工作流实现更高的通过率。

Conclusion: Harness设计不应盲目追求高分解度和强引导；适当的"部分引导"策略——仅约束初始步骤而将后续执行交由Agent自主完成——可能更为有效，为LLM Agent的推理时工程优化提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Harness engineering has emerged as an important inference-time technique for large language model (LLM) agents, aiming to improve long-term performance through task decomposition and guided execution. However, more elaborate harnesses are not uniformly better: increasing decomposition or guidance can sometimes improve execution, but can also reduce final task success. We study harness design through the lens of inference-time trajectory alignment. This perspective separates harness into two mechanisms: task decomposition, which structures a task into sub-goals, and guided execution, which reshapes local action distributions during execution. This decomposition allows us to quantify how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and guidance-induced action reweighting shape the performance limits of harness design. It further reveals concrete failure modes, including over-decomposition, over-pruning, and hallucinated execution. We validate these predictions through controlled synthetic experiments and real terminal agent benchmarks. Inspired by the theory, we further show that effective harnesses can be partial: specifying only the initial steps and leaving the remaining execution to agent can achieve higher pass rate than fully structured workflows.

</details>


### [41] [Memory-R2: Fair Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21768)
*Sikuan Yan,Ahmed Bahloul,Ercong Nie,Susanna Schwarzmann,Riccardo Trivisonno,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memory-R2是一个面向长程记忆增强型LLM智能体的训练框架。其核心算法LoGo-GRPO通过局部和全局分组优化解决多会话RL中因记忆状态不同导致轨迹比较不公平的问题。此外，框架采用共享参数协同设计（同一LLM骨干通过角色提示实例化事实提取器和记忆管理器）以及渐进式课程学习（从8到32会话递增训练长度），实现对记忆构建和演化的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习训练记忆增强型LLM智能体时，由于不同轨迹会写入、更新或删除不同的记忆，导致中间记忆状态不共享，破坏了GRPO等分组相对方法（group-relative methods）中"所有rollout来自相同有效环境"的核心假设，使得轨迹级奖励为长程记忆操作提供有偏或不准确的信用分配信号。

Method: 提出Memory-R2框架，包含：(1) LoGo-GRPO算法，结合全局目标（保持长程轨迹级奖励的端到端学习）和局部重rollout（从相同中间记忆状态出发比较不同记忆操作结果，实现更公平的分组比较和更精确的记忆构建监督）；(2) 共享参数协同设计，通过角色特定提示从同一LLM骨干实例化事实提取器和记忆管理器，联合优化记忆形成与记忆演化；(3) 渐进式课程学习，训练长度从8个会话逐步增加到16、32个会话。

Result: Memory-R2为长程多会话场景下的记忆增强型LLM智能体提供了一种有效的训练范式，解决了信用分配难题，并通过协同设计和课程学习实现稳定的多步RL训练。

Conclusion: 通过局部与全局分组优化结合、共享参数协同设计以及渐进式课程学习，Memory-R2有效应对了多会话RL中记忆状态不一致带来的训练挑战，为长程记忆增强型LLM智能体的训练提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents enable interactions that extend beyond finite context windows by storing, updating, and reusing information across sessions. However, training such agents with reinforcement learning in multi-session environments is challenging because memory turns the agent's past actions into part of its future environment. Once different rollouts write, update, or delete different memories, they no longer share the same intermediate memory state, making trajectory-level comparisons fundamentally unfair. This violates a key assumption behind group-relative methods such as GRPO, where rollouts are compared as if they were sampled from the same effective environment. Consequently, trajectory-level rewards provide noisy or biased credit signals for long-horizon memory operations. To address this challenge, we introduce Memory-R2, a training framework for long-horizon memory-augmented LLM agents. Its core algorithm, LoGo-GRPO, combines local and global group-relative optimization. The global objective preserves end-to-end learning from long-horizon trajectory-level rewards, while local rerollouts compare different memory-operation outcomes from the same intermediate memory state, yielding fairer group comparisons and more precise supervision for memory construction. Beyond credit assignment, Memory-R2 jointly optimizes memory formation and memory evolution with a shared-parameter co-learning design, where a fact extractor and a memory manager are instantiated from the same LLM backbone through role-specific prompts. To stabilize multi-step RL over long memory horizons, we adopt a progressive curriculum that increases the training horizon from 8 to 16 to 32 sessions. Together, these components provide an effective training paradigm for memory-augmented LLM agents in long-horizon multi-session settings.

</details>


### [42] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出yvsoucom-iterkit，一个确定性、日志驱动的自动化机器学习框架，将管道优化编码为可追踪的日志实体。在Pima印第安糖尿病和Stroke数据集上，通过超过18,000个管道配置的实验，发现搜索空间结构化且存在冗余，性能由少数交互组件主导。关键贡献包括组件归因分析、相似性分析和跨种子的鲁棒性分析，揭示了性能-鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疾病风险预测面临特征异构、样本有限和严重类别不平衡的挑战，自动化机器学习管道的优化缺乏可再现性和可解释性。

Method: 提出yvsoucom-iterkit框架，将管道优化编码为确定性、可追踪的日志实体，支持组件归因、交互分析、相似性分析和跨种子鲁棒性分析。使用随机森林重要性分析识别关键组件，通过RMS距离度量组件相似性，采用集成策略提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在Pima数据集上，集成模型达到Weighted-F1 0.89、Macro-F1 0.88；Stroke数据集上Weighted-F1 0.94，但Macro-F1仅0.67。关键组件分析显示，Pima上增强(0.454)、模型选择(0.198)和类别不平衡处理(0.101)是主要驱动力，Stroke上不平衡处理(0.406)占主导。组件相似性分析揭示强冗余性，集成方法跨种子变异性(0.023-0.026)低于SVM。

Conclusion: 有效的AutoML优化可以聚焦于少数高影响组件，搜索空间存在结构化冗余，集成策略在性能和鲁棒性之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [43] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualOptim+ 提出了一种基于基态和增量态的大语言模型机器遗忘优化框架，通过自适应平衡共享与解耦状态来协调遗忘与保留目标之间的冲突，并提供了8bit量化版本以降低内存开销。实验表明其在遗忘、安全对齐和多任务学习任务中实现了更优的目标权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的机器遗忘面临遗忘目标与保留目标之间梯度方向冲突的挑战，现有方法难以有效平衡二者，导致遗忘效果或模型性能受损。

Method: 提出DualOptim+框架，引入基态（base state）捕获遗忘与保留目标的共享表征，以及增量态（delta states）保留各目标特有的残差。优化器根据遗忘与保留梯度的方向冲突自适应地桥接共享状态与解耦状态。同时提出DualOptim+ 8bit量化变体减少内存开销。

Result: 在虚构/真实遗忘、安全对齐和多任务学习任务中，DualOptim+ 一致实现了不同目标之间的更优权衡，且量化版本在不损失性能的情况下降低了内存开销。

Conclusion: DualOptim+ 是一种有效的LLM遗忘优化框架，通过基态-增量态架构自适应协调冲突梯度，在多个任务上取得更优的遗忘-保留平衡，且量化版本兼顾了内存效率。

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [44] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联合包络共形选择（JECS）方法，用于多模型基准去污染，能够在给定假设下实现全球污染率（GCR）的可控性，实验证明该方法在保持GCR控制的同时比基线方法具有更高统计效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据污染检测方法缺乏理论保证，且共形方法只能针对单一模型进行假阳性控制，分别应用会导致模型特定基准，破坏跨模型公平比较。因此需要一种多模型联合去污染方法。

Method: 提出JECS方法：计算每个模型的共形p值，按样本取最大值聚合；从右尾观测数据驱动阈值重构最大p值的保守包络分布；应用自适应Benjamini-Hochberg（BH）程序对包络重缩放值进行选择，实现全局污染率控制。

Result: 在多种模型和基准上的大量实验表明，JECS在保持目标GCR控制的同时，比最大p值基线方法具有更高的统计效能（power）。

Conclusion: JECS为多模型联合基准去污染提供了首个具有理论保证的共形方法，有效解决了跨模型公平比较与污染控制的权衡问题。

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [45] [Tabular foundation models for robust calibration of near-infrared chemical sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21544)
*Robin Reiter,Denis Cornet,Fabien Michel,Lauriane Rouan,Gregory Beurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了表格基础模型TabPFN在近红外光谱化学传感中的校准能力，在66个数据集上与PLS、Ridge、CatBoost和CNN-1D等方法进行对比。结果表明，经预处理优化的TabPFN在回归任务中表现最佳，原始光谱TabPFN在分类任务中排名第一，但两者在光谱异常值和外推样本上的优势有所下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱技术虽广泛应用，但其实际部署依赖于能处理高维、共线光谱、小样本、预处理依赖性、异常值和外推问题的校准模型。现有方法在这些挑战下仍有局限，因此需要探索新的校准策略。

Method: 使用TabPFN对66个NIR数据集（54个回归、12个分类任务）进行基准测试，比较原始光谱和预处理优化两种推理方式，与PLS/PLS-DA、Ridge、CatBoost和1D-CNN进行对比，采用统一的验证框架，预处理和模型选择仅在校准数据上进行。

Result: 回归任务中，预处理优化的TabPFN平均排名最佳，显著优于PLS、CatBoost、原始光谱TabPFN和CNN-1D，与Ridge统计相当；分类任务中，原始光谱TabPFN获得最佳平均排名。TabPFN在异常值和外推样本上的优势减弱，传统化学计量学模型仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型可补充传统化学计量学工作流程，尤其适用于中小规模校准场景，但需开发面向光谱的先验知识和不确定性感知部署策略。

Abstract: Near-infrared spectroscopy is increasingly used as a rapid, non-destructive chemical sensing technology for the analysis of food, pharmaceutical, biological, and environmental samples. However, the practical deployment of NIR sensors still depends on calibration models able to handle high-dimensional, collinear spectra, limited sample sizes, preprocessing dependence, spectral outliers, and extrapolation beyond the calibration domain. Here, we evaluate whether tabular foundation models can provide a new calibration strategy for NIR chemical sensing. We benchmark TabPFN on 66 NIR datasets covering 54 regression and 12 classification tasks, and compare direct inference on raw spectra with preprocessing-optimized inference against PLS/PLS-DA, Ridge, Catboost, and one-dimensional convolutional neural networks. The study uses a unified validation framework in which preprocessing and model selection are performed exclusively on calibration data before external test evaluation. In regression, preprocessing-optimized TabPFN achieves the best overall average rank and significantly outperforms PLS, CatBoost, TabPFN on raw spectra, and CNN-1D, while remaining statistically comparable to Ridge. In classification, TabPFN applied directly to raw spectra provides the best average rank, with performance close to the optimized variant. Robustness analyses show that TabPFN provides strong average predictive performance but that its advantage decreases on spectral outliers and extrapolated samples, where classical chemometric models remain competitive. These results suggest that tabular foundation models can complement established chemometric workflows for NIR chemical sensing, especially in small- to medium-sized calibration settings, while highlighting the need for spectroscopy-specific priors and uncertainty-aware deployment strategies.

</details>


### [46] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CogAdapt框架，通过LeadBridge适配器将3导联可穿戴信号转换为12导联表示，结合ProFine渐进式微调策略，将临床ECG基础模型迁移至可穿戴设备上的认知负荷评估任务，在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上分别达到0.626和0.768的macro-F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估对自适应人机交互至关重要，但受限于标注数据不足和跨受试者泛化能力差。现有的ECG基础模型虽在临床数据上预训练了丰富表示，但由于传感器配置不匹配和任务差异，无法直接应用于可穿戴设备。

Method: 提出CogAdapt框架，包含两个核心组件：1) LeadBridge——可学习适配器，将3导联可穿戴信号转换为解剖学一致的12导联表示；2) ProFine——渐进式微调策略，逐步解冻编码器层并防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在两个公开数据集（CLARE和CL-Drive）上采用留一受试者交叉验证，CogAdapt显著优于从头训练的基线模型，macro-F1分数分别达到0.626和0.768。

Conclusion: 证明了基础模型适配策略在基于可穿戴传感器的受试者无关认知负荷评估中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [47] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于期望一致性条件（Expectation Consistency Condition）的协变量偏移下置信度校准方法ECL，该损失函数不依赖重要性加权，兼容多种校准类型，并在理论和实验上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法假设训练与测试数据独立同分布，在协变量偏移下失效；而现有协变量偏移下的校准方法存在类别级校准困难、依赖不稳定的重要性加权等问题。

Method: 1. 推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的充要条件——期望一致性条件；2. 基于该条件提出无监督域适应损失ECL，兼容规范校准、类别级校准和顶层标签校准；3. 证明ECL的样本复杂度与ECE相同，并提供理论支持的mini-batch可训练方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了该方法在置信度校准上的有效性。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移不一定导致置信度未校准，期望一致性条件提供了比全局分布对齐更宽松的校准条件；ECL损失在不依赖重要性加权的情况下实现了多个校准目标。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [48] [MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22775)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MambaGaze框架，通过XMD编码处理眼动追踪中的缺失数据，并利用双向Mamba-2以线性复杂度建模长程时序依赖，在认知负荷评估任务中超越CNN/Transformer等基线方法4-12个百分点，并在边缘设备上实现43-68 FPS实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动信号实现实时认知负荷评估对自适应人机交互（如驾驶员警觉监测、飞行驾驶辅助）至关重要，但面临两个核心挑战：频繁的数据缺失（眨眼/追踪失败）以及长程时序依赖的高效建模。

Method: 1) XMD编码：将原始特征与观测掩码和时间增量结合，显式建模数据不确定性；2) 双向Mamba-2：以线性计算复杂度捕获时序依赖关系。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上，留一被试评估下分别达到76.8%和73.1%准确率，优于CNN、Transformer、ResNet、VGG基线4-12个百分点；在NVIDIA Jetson平台上实现43-68 FPS，功耗低于7.5W。

Conclusion: MambaGaze框架有效解决了眼动数据缺失和长程依赖建模问题，在准确率和边缘部署效率上均表现优越，验证了可穿戴认知负荷监测的可行性。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Single Slot: Joint Optimization for Multi-Slot Guaranteed Display Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21556)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Jiaming Deng,Miao Xie,Linyou Cai,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多槽位保量展示广告的联合优化框架，通过合同轮盘机制和页面浏览约束解决槽位冗余、合同失衡和曝光集中问题，在美团广告平台上线后实现ARPU提升28.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保量展示广告方法多基于单槽位假设，无法有效优化多槽位页面视图下的广告分配，面临槽位级冗余、合同失衡和曝光集中等挑战。

Method: 将分配问题建模为离线二分图匹配问题，引入合同轮盘机制处理槽位独占性，结合页面浏览约束控制曝光次数，并设计了可扩展的分配优化算法以支持大规模部署。

Result: 在美团广告平台的线上A/B测试中，70%流量下平均每用户收入（ARPU）提升28.99%，DID分析表明合同稳定性改善。

Conclusion: 该框架在真实广告部署中显著提升了商家ROI、平台收入效率和合同履约鲁棒性，具有强适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

</details>


### [50] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自定进度课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，通过双层次难度度量（话语级和对话级）和学习调度器，缓解多模态情感识别中的模态不对齐与不平衡学习问题，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对话情感识别中，模态不对齐和模态间不平衡学习严重限制了多模态信息的有效利用，现有方法难以同时处理细粒度的话语级难度和宏观的对话结构差异。

Method: 提出SPCL即插即用框架，包含双层次难度度量器（话语级捕捉模态特异性难度，对话级捕捉情感依赖与模态一致性）和学习调度器，按从易到难的顺序动态指导训练过程。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上加权F1提升约+1.2%~+6.6%，在MELD上提升高达+10.4%，且在不同架构和模态设置下表现一致。

Conclusion: SPCL作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，有效缓解了模态不平衡问题，提升模型鲁棒性，具有良好的泛化性和有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [51] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种机器学习模型在慢性肾脏病（CKD）风险预测中的表现，发现尽管模型在内部测试集上取得了近乎完美的区分度（AUROC 1.00）和校准效果，但在外部队列（MIMIC-IV）上性能急剧下降（AUROC降至0.48-0.58，校准误差升至0.68-0.76，共形覆盖从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25），所有模型的部署就绪度评分均低于4/16，表明模型泛化能力严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CKD风险预测模型通常只关注区分度（如AUROC），而忽视了校准质量和不确定性量化，这导致临床医生无法判断模型输出的概率是否可靠。本研究旨在系统评估模型在校准、不确定性量化和部署可行性方面的表现。

Method: 在UCI CKD数据集（400名患者，62.5%患病率）上训练五种分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、带Platt缩放的SVM、高斯朴素贝叶斯），使用Platt缩放和等渗回归进行校准，通过期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier评分评估校准质量，采用分割共形预测以90%边际覆盖为目标量化不确定性，并在MIMIC-IV演示队列（97名患者，23.7%患病率）上进行分布压力测试。

Result: 所有模型在UCI测试集上AUROC均达1.00；等渗校准将内部ECE降至0.000-0.022。但在MIMIC-IV上AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76，共形覆盖率从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25（目标90%）。部署就绪度检查中无模型得分超过4/16。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的内部性能无法迁移到外部数据。在临床预测模型部署前，应在外部数据上评估校准稳定性和共形覆盖，以确保模型在实际临床环境中的可靠性。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [52] [VeriScale: Adversarial Test-Suite Scaling for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22368)
*Yifan Bai,Xiaoyang Liu,Zihao Mou,Guihong Wang,Jian Yu,Shuhan Xie,Yantao Li,Yangyu Zhang,Jingwei Liang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriScale框架，通过对抗性实现驱动测试套件扩展与精简，构建更高质量的基准测试（VerinaPlus/VerinaLite），揭示现有LLM在代码生成和规约生成任务中的能力高估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试因正负测试用例的数量和质量有限，导致对LLM在规约生成和代码实现方面的能力评估存在高估问题，亟需更高质量的评测基准。

Method: 提出VeriScale框架，包含两阶段：测试套件扩展（通过对抗实现构建多样且具挑战性的测试用例）和测试套件精简（将扩展后的测试用例蒸馏为紧凑但具区分力的子集）。在Verina基准上实例化得到VerinaPlus（扩展83倍）和VerinaLite（精简版，14倍）。

Result: 在8个先进LLM上的实验表明：VerinaPlus揭示了原始基准隐藏的模型弱点，导致SpecGen和CodeGen任务分数显著下降；VerinaLite以较低的评估成本保持了同样的区分能力。

Conclusion: VeriScale框架能有效构建更高质量的代码生成基准，更准确地评估LLM在软件工程任务中的真实能力，且精简版在成本与区分力之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for software engineering, constructing high-quality benchmarks is crucial for evaluating not just the functional correctness, but also the formal verifiability of generated code. However, existing benchmarks are limited by the quantity and quality of positive and negative test cases, leading to an overestimation of model capabilities in generating specifications and implementations. To address this, we propose VeriScale, a novel framework driven by the adversarial implementations. It consists of two stages: test-suite expansion to construct diverse and challenging test cases, and test-suite reduction to distill them into compact yet discriminative suites. While VeriScale is general, we instantiate it on Verina to construct VerinaPlus, which expands the original test suites by over 83$\times$, and VerinaLite, a lightweight 14$\times$ variant. Our experiments across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrate that VerinaPlus exposes substantial model weaknesses hidden by the original benchmark, evidenced by sharp score drops on both SpecGen and CodeGen tasks, whereas VerinaLite maintains this discriminative power at a fraction of the evaluation cost. The enhanced benchmarks and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/XiaoyangLiu-sjtu/VeriScale.

</details>


### [53] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTact架构，将抗体CDR设计显式分解为表面互补指纹学习、接触预测、接触门控序列生成三个级联阶段，在CHIMERA-Bench上实现结构质量（RMSD提升7%）、表位感知（F1提升10%）的最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体CDR设计方法将"哪些CDR位置会接触抗原"和"这些位置选什么氨基酸"两个子问题混为一谈，迫使模型通过均匀消息传递隐式学习接触推理，导致抗原信号被均匀稀释在所有位置上。

Method: 提出ConTact架构，包含三个级联阶段：1）学习表面互补指纹；2）预测CDR-抗原接触；3）注入接触门控的抗原特征到序列生成头。采用距离偏置的交叉注意力模块编码空间近邻几何先验，以及接触加权的交叉熵损失聚焦结合关键位置的梯度信号。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，ConTact在结构质量（RMSD较次优基线提升7%）、表位感知（F1较GNN基线提升10%）上达到最优，序列恢复率（AAR 0.38）在多个CDR-H3设计基线中具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式解耦接触推理与序列生成能够显著提升抗体CDR设计的结构质量和抗原感知能力，验证了"接触-然后-动作"架构的有效性。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [54] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位置加权自蒸馏方法（PW-OPSD），通过分支可行性诊断发现推理过程中教师令牌的可靠性具有轨迹结构特性，位置分数比熵更能预测可靠性（AUROC 0.83 vs ≤0.57），在多个模型和数据集上一致提升Avg@12指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）平等对待所有学生访问前缀的教师目标，而基于熵的方法在推理场景中因高熵可能反映良性多样性而非不可靠性，导致可靠性含义模糊。作者旨在识别教师令牌可靠性的结构化特征，并据此改进蒸馏目标。

Method: 1) 提出分支可行性诊断：在学生轨迹的每个前缀处强制教师备选令牌，测试能否恢复正确答案；2) 发现面向序列内位置分数是最强预测因子（AUROC 0.83），而局部熵仅0.57；3) 提出PW-OPSD：在保持OPSD框架（学生轨迹、特权教师、前向KL裁剪）基础上，对轨迹不同位置施加递增权重。

Result: Qwen3-4B上AIME 2024 Avg@12提升+1.0，AIME 2025提升+1.1；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think上一致提升Avg@12。

Conclusion: 教师令牌在推理蒸馏中的可靠性具有轨迹结构特性，基于位置的加权策略无需额外教师计算即可有效利用这一特性提升蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [55] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，利用自定义模拟器生成合成数据进行训练，解决从Hi-C相互作用图中推断参数（如着丝粒定位）的逆问题，能处理实体数量和大小可变性，并在多种物种中准确恢复着丝粒基因组位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从相互作用图（如Hi-C）中推断参数可视为通用逆问题，但现实数据存在实体数量和大小可变性，现有方法难以处理这类变异性，因此需要一种能利用图间共享结构（如全局对齐局部模式）并处理可变性的数据驱动方法。

Method: 采用Transformer架构处理实体数量和大小可变性，并设计自定义模拟器生成大量计算成本低廉的合成数据用于模型训练，从而学习相互作用图中的共享结构模式。

Result: 在着丝粒定位任务中，该方法在多种基因组大小不同的物种上均能准确恢复着丝粒的基因组位置。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了相互作用图推断中的变异性问题，为着丝粒定位等任务提供了通用且准确的数据驱动解决方案。

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [56] [Alike Parts: A Feature-Informed Approach to Local and Global Prototype Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21646)
*Jacek Karolczak,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合特征重要性的原型解释框架，在局部层面通过"相似部分"方法突出显示实例与原型共享的关键特征子集，在全局层面通过增强原型选择目标函数引入特征重要性项以促进原型特征归因的多样性。实验表明该方法在不损害预测保真度的前提下提升了特征多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原型的可解释方法虽然提供了直观的示例级解释，但缺乏特征级别的细粒度信息，无法让用户理解分类实例与原型之间具体哪些特征最为相关。

Method: 1) 局部解释：提出"alike parts"方法，利用特征重要性分数高亮分类实例与其最近原型之间最相关的共享特征子集。2) 全局解释：在原型选择的目标函数中引入特征重要性项，主动促进所选原型在特征归因上的多样性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，增强后的原型选择过程能够保持甚至在某些情况下提高替代模型的预测保真度，说明特征多样性不会损害模型保真度。

Conclusion: 特征多样性和预测保真度并非不可兼得，通过将特征重要性融入原型选择和解释生成过程，可以在保持模型性能的同时获得更具信息量的细粒度原型解释。

Abstract: Prototype-based explanations offer an intuitive, example-based approach to support the interpretability of machine learning black box classifiers but often lack feature-level granularity. We introduce a framework that integrates feature importance at two levels to address this gap. First, for local explanations, we propose \textit{alike parts}: a method that uses feature importance scores to highlight the most relevant, shared feature subsets between a classified instance and its nearest prototype, guiding user attention. Second, we augment the global prototype selection objective function with a feature importance term to actively promote diversity in the feature attributions of the selected prototypes. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that this augmented selection process maintains or, in some cases, increases the prediction fidelity of the surrogate model, suggesting that feature diversity does not compromise model fidelity.

</details>


### [57] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EntmaxKV，一种利用α-entmax注意力天然稀疏特性的KV缓存高效解码框架。通过查询感知的页面评分、支持集感知的候选选择和稀疏entmax注意力，在加载KV页面之前利用稀疏性，实现与全缓存entmax接近的性能，同时仅使用少量KV缓存，在1M上下文长度下相比全注意力基线最高获得3.36倍（softmax）和5.43倍（entmax）加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，KV缓存内存带宽成为主要瓶颈，因为每个生成token都需要访问随上下文长度线性增长的缓存。现有稀疏解码方法针对softmax注意力设计，其密集尾部导致任何截断都会丢弃非零概率质量。而α-entmax产生精确零值，可将稀疏解码从密集尾部近似转化为支持恢复问题，但现有entmax核没有解决自回归解码瓶颈——在稀疏性已知之前仍需流式传输完整KV缓存。

Method: EntmaxKV包含三个核心组件：(1) 查询感知的页面评分（query-aware page scoring），在加载页面前评估其重要性；(2) 支持集感知的候选选择（support-aware candidate selection），确保选中的候选包含entmax支持集；(3) 稀疏entmax注意力（sparse entmax attention）。此外引入高斯感知的entmax选择器（Gaussian-aware entmax selector），通过轻量级页面统计信息估计entmax阈值，自适应调整选择的预算。

Result: EntmaxKV在相同KV预算下比基于softmax的稀疏解码方法丢弃更少的概率质量，保留更多的支持token，并实现更低的输出误差。在长上下文和语言建模基准上，使用少量KV缓存就能紧密匹配全缓存entmax性能，在1M上下文长度下相比全注意力基线获得最高3.36倍（softmax）和5.43倍（entmax）加速。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV通过利用α-entmax的固有稀疏性，将稀疏解码问题转化为支持恢复问题，在理论保证下实现了高效的长上下文解码。该方法证明了entmax注意力不仅在训练阶段有优势，在推理阶段也能通过原生稀疏设计带来显著加速。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded. EntmaxKV combines query-aware page scoring, support-aware candidate selection, and sparse entmax attention. We analyze truncation error through the dropped probability mass $δ$, showing that output error is controlled by $δ$ and vanishes when the entmax support is recovered. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware entmax selector that estimates the entmax threshold from lightweight page statistics, adapting the selected budget to the score distribution. Empirically, EntmaxKV drops less probability mass, retains more support tokens, and achieves lower output error than softmax-based sparse decoding at matched KV budgets. On long-context and language modeling benchmarks, it closely matches full-cache entmax while using a small fraction of the KV cache, achieving up to $3.36\times$ (softmax) and $5.43\times$ (entmax) speedup over full attention baselines at 1M context length. Code available at: https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv.

</details>


### [58] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI文本检测器并未构建人机边界，而是放大了预训练中已有的"典型性轴"——该轴在母语/非母语写作上表现相反，且大量"检测干预"本质上仅是校准偏移而非表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战主流认知：AI文本检测器是否真的学会了区分人类/AI写作的语义边界？作者提出"典型性轴假说"——检测器只是放大了预训练编码器中已有的一个典型性方向（AI文本 vs 人类文本更典型的差异），而非构建了新的判别边界。

Method: (1) 原始编码器投影：将未微调的RoBERTa等编码器的隐空间直接投影到 centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) 方向；(2) 非母语反演实验：用ESL写作验证典型性轴假说的唯一可证伪预测；(3) 三种观察者不变性探针：text-surface caps_rate残差化、几何符号消融、闭式文本对预测器；(4) 校准分解：将LoRA→全微调的性能差异分解为校准偏移 vs 表征学习。

Result: 原始投影AUROC达微调上限的86-106%；RoBERTa-base上全微调反而低于原始投影；非母语ESL写作AUROC仅0.06-0.20（假说验证）；24样本冻结探针匹配全微调性能；闭式雅可比预测器R²=1.000；典型性轴可跨架构转移（三款第三方RoBERTa检测器16/16 oracle等价）；三种探针在三个架构上cos 0.74/0.81/1.00；LoRA→全微调97%差距是校准偏移。

Conclusion: AI文本检测器的核心机制是放大预训练典型性轴，而非学习人机边界；该发现对非母语写作有严重公平性隐患（检测完全失效）；现有干预方法的"效果"几乎全部来自校准偏移，而非真正的表征改进。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>


### [59] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从价值梯度视角解释了无评论家强化学习方法（如PPO、GRPO）在LLM后训练中的有效性，证明了在可微展开和加性噪声参数化下，演员更新在期望上等价于价值梯度；对于离散Transformer策略，自动微分通过注意力机制产生的经验代价函数近似价值信号。基于此提出了RL效果分解为价值梯度信号和可达奖励空间的标准，用于预测RL沿预训练轨迹的最有效时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何无评论家强化学习方法（PPO、GRPO）在LLM后训练中有效，以及何时能获得最大收益。

Method: 1. 建立价值梯度视角的理论框架；2. 在可微展开和加性噪声参数化下证明演员更新在期望上等价于价值梯度；3. 对离散Transformer策略，证明自动微分通过注意力机制产生的经验代价函数近似价值信号，误差由采样间隙和策略熵控制；4. 提出RL效果分解标准。

Result: 证明了无评论家RL方法的演员更新在期望上具有价值梯度性质；离散Transformer中自动微分能近似价值信号；提出了RL效果分解为价值梯度信号和可达奖励空间的理论。

Conclusion: 无评论家RL方法在LLM后训练中的有效性可从价值梯度视角理解，并可通过所提出的分解标准预测RL的最佳应用时机。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

</details>


### [60] [Hierarchical Variational Policies for Reward-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21661)
*Kushagra Pandey,Farrin Marouf Sofian,Jan Niklas Groeneveld,Felix Draxler,Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次变分模型框架，将测试时适应策略摊销为轻量级随机策略，实现高质量奖励对齐样本的快速生成，在4倍超分辨率任务上相比基线方法推理速度提升5倍以上，并进一步扩展到半摊销设置，在多个逆问题上达到最先进感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型适配下游任务（如逆问题）时，现有方法需要昂贵的测试时引导或优化，推理成本高。本文旨在构建一种既能保持生成质量又能大幅降低推理成本的原则性框架。

Method: 将测试时自适应形式化为层次变分模型，将控制策略摊销为轻量级但表达力强的随机策略。该框架自然支持少步扩散采样：大步长实现快速推理，学习到的策略通过提供结构化的逐歩控制维持样本质量。进一步扩展到半摊销模式，将廉价的摊销提议与有限的测试时优化相结合。

Result: 完全摊销的采样器在质量-速度权衡上表现出色，匹配或超越最近的测试时扩展基线，同时显著减少计算量。在4倍超分辨率任务上，相比最佳基线方法，感知质量更优且推理速度快5倍以上。半摊销方法在多个挑战性逆问题上达到了最先进的感知质量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的摊销化测试时适应框架能有效权衡生成质量与推理速度，为扩散模型在逆问题等下游任务中的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. We propose a principled framework for generating high-quality reward-aligned samples at substantially reduced inference cost. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

</details>


### [61] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出I-SAFE框架，一种基于Wasserstein相干性度量的后验分布审计方法，用于评估科学AI模型在结构化扰动下输出分布与领域先验的一致性，并在药物-靶标相互作用预测任务中揭示了精度指标无法区分的模型间差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在科学预测任务中常利用捷径特征或数据偏差取得良好基准性能，但这类行为并不反映与领域相关的结构知识，导致对模型科学意义的误判。

Method: 提出I-SAFE框架，包含三种互补度量：基于分位数的位置级相干性度量（QBM）、Wasserstein相干性度量（WCM，用于序数相干性）、平移不变WCM变体（用于形状相干性）。对训练好的黑箱模型施加结构化引导的输入扰动，评估输出分布与外部结构先验的一致性。

Result: 在Davis激酶基准、KLIFS结合口袋注释及三个基于序列的DTI模型（DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA、TAPB）上验证，模型预测性能相近但I-SAFE揭示了显著不同的分布响应特征，这是基于精度的评估无法发现的。

Conclusion: I-SAFE是模型无关的框架，适用于任何输入可结构化分解且存在外部先验的领域，能揭示传统精度评估掩盖的模型行为差异。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>


### [62] [stable-worldmodel: A Platform for Reproducible World Modeling Research and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21800)
*Lucas Maes,Quentin Le Lidec,Luiz Facury,Nassim Massaudi,Ayush Chaurasia,Francesco Capuano,Richard Gao,Taj Gillin,Dan Haramati,Damien Scieur,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了stable-worldmodel（swm），一个开源的标准化世界模型研究平台，通过高性能Lance数据层、清洁实现的基线模型和丰富的泛化评估套件，解决了当前世界模型研究中代码碎片化、数据加载慢和缺乏标准化泛化基准三大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型研究存在碎片化问题，包括不一致的代码库、数据管道和评估协议，阻碍了可重复性和公平比较。具体有三个关键瓶颈：脆弱的一次性代码库、缓慢的视频数据加载以及缺乏标准化的泛化基准。

Method: swm平台包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于Lance的高性能数据层，原生支持MP4、HDF5和LeRobot数据集并提供转换工具；(2) 干净、经过测试的现代世界模型基线和规划求解器实现；(3) 扩展了可控视觉、几何和物理变化因素的环境与任务套件，用于系统性地评估动力学理解、控制性能、表示质量和分布外泛化能力。

Result: 通过统一完整流程到单一可扩展框架，swm显著降低了研究开销，加速了向可靠世界模型的可信进展。提供了标准化、可重复的世界模型研究和评估平台。

Conclusion: swm通过统一标准化的平台解决了世界模型研究中的碎片化和可重复性问题，为公平比较和系统评估提供了基础设施，有望推动世界模型领域的可信进展。

Abstract: World models are central to building agents that can reason, plan, and generalize beyond their training data. However, research on world models is currently fragmented, with disparate codebases, data pipelines, and evaluation protocols hindering reproducibility and fair comparison. Current practice is further limited by three key bottlenecks: fragile one-off codebases, slow video data loading, and the lack of standardized generalization benchmarks. We present stable-worldmodel (swm), an open-source platform for standardized and reproducible world modeling research and evaluation. It delivers (1) a high-performance Lance-based data layer with native support and conversion tools for MP4, HDF5, and LeRobot datasets, (2) clean, well-tested implementations of modern world model baselines and planning solvers, and (3) a broad suite of environments and tasks extended with controllable visual, geometric, and physical factors of variation for systematic in-silico evaluation of dynamics understanding, control performance, representation quality, and out-of-distribution generalization. By unifying the full pipeline under a single, scalable framework, \texttt{swm} dramatically reduces research overhead and accelerates trustworthy progress toward reliable world models.

</details>


### [63] [Correcting Class Imbalance in Prior-Data Fitted Networks for Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21742)
*Samuel McDowell,Nathan Stromberg,Lalitha Sankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs)在类别不平衡场景下的表现，发现阈值调整和降采样是缓解不平衡问题的最有效方法，分别得益于PFN的良好校准特性和优异的小样本学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFNs在表格分类任务中表现出色，但类别不平衡会严重损害其对稀有类别的分类性能。现有的不平衡处理方法（如基于损失函数的策略）无法直接应用于PFN的上下文学习机制，因此需要探索适合PFN特性的不平衡处理方法。

Method: 将多种经典类别不平衡处理方法（阈值调整、降采样等）适配到PFN分类框架中，并在不平衡数据集上比较它们的性能表现。

Result: 阈值调整因PFN良好的校准特性而表现优异；降采样因PFN在小样本场景下的出色性能而表现相当，同时还能降低推理计算成本。

Conclusion: 阈值调整和降采样是处理PFN类别不平衡问题的有效方法，分别利用了PFN的校准优势和有限数据学习能力。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) have achieved exceptional performance on tabular classification tasks. However, like other classifiers, their performance can suffer under the effect of class imbalance, resulting in poor performance for rare classes. Several techniques exist which attempt to mitigate the deleterious effect of class imbalance on classification performance, but the in-context learning (ICL) dynamic of PFNs means that loss-based strategies are impossible, and other techniques are unproven. We have adapted several classical techniques addressing class imbalance and analyzed their performance on PFN classification. We observe that thresholding performs exceptionally well because of the calibration characteristics of PFNs, and downsampling performs comparably because of PFNs exceptional limited-data performance, with the additional benefit of reduced computation cost for inference.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond Euclidean Proximity: Repairing Latent World Models with Horizon-Matched Trajectory Reachability Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22164)
*Liangyu Li,Shengzhi Wang,Qingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹可达性度量（TRM），一种针对固定潜在世界模型的后验终端排序方法。TRM通过训练一个轻量级的成对预测头，利用轨迹日志结构替代或补充原始潜空间欧氏距离，显著提升了长时域规划性能。在TwoRoom基准上，TRM将成功率从7.0%提升至97.0%，并提供了可解释性分析表明原始潜空间MSE中仅不到1%的维度承载了规划相关的关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型的终端代价接口（如原始潜空间欧氏距离）可能暴露给规划器错误的决策相关信息，即原始潜距离对不同任务相关变量的加权不合理，导致长时域规划任务中候选序列排序错误。

Method: 提出轨迹可达性度量（TRM），在固定潜在世界模型之上训练一个轻量级成对预测头，输入为两个潜状态和时域间隔，输出可达性分数。关键设计是"水平感知监督"：从日志轨迹中采样平衡的、广泛时间间隔的潜状态对进行训练，以匹配长时域终端候选排序问题。TRM可作为原始潜距离的替代或混合代价，不影响编码器、动力学、采样器、优化器和评估过程。

Result: TwoRoom基准：原始LeWM成功率7.0%，TRM提升至97.0%（打乱时间标签控制实验为0.0%）；PLDM基线从32.7%提升至84.0%。短时域TRM变体仅达35.0%。可解释性分析：XY位置线性可解码（R²=0.998），但原始潜MSE排序错误；XY探测行空间占终端-目标潜MSE不到1%，但携带大部分候选质量信号；SCSA审计表明TRM改善了排序和规划器选择的终点。

Conclusion: TRM是一种规划器层面的修复方法，通过可解释性审计可以判断何时终端可达性度量应替代或增强原始潜空间距离。在连续操控任务（PushT go50/go75）中，任务状态度量虽然不如闭合环路成功率直接，但能更干净地改善SCSA排序和选中的最终距离，激励辅助混合代价的使用。

Abstract: Latent world models can contain the state needed for control, yet their terminal-cost interface can expose the planner to the wrong decision-relevant information. In common latent MPC, candidate sequences are ranked by Euclidean distance between predicted terminal and goal latent states; this assumes that raw latent distance weights reachability-relevant variables correctly. We propose trajectory reachability metrics (TRM), a post-hoc terminal-ranking method for fixed latent world models. TRM trains a small pairwise head from logged trajectory structure and uses it as a replacement or hybrid cost; the encoder, dynamics, sampler, optimizer, and evaluation manifests remain fixed. The key design choice is horizon-aware supervision: the metric is trained on broad, balanced temporal separations to match the long-horizon terminal candidate ranking problem. On a hard TwoRoom benchmark, raw latent planning with LeWorldModel (LeWM) reaches 7.0% success, while full-horizon TRM reaches 97.0%; shuffled temporal-label controls stay at 0.0%. The same recipe improves a PLDM baseline from 32.7% to 84.0% across three seeds, and a short-horizon TRM variant reaches only 35.0% with the 100,000 pair budget. In TwoRoom, we provide mechanistic evidence for why TRM works: XY position is linearly decodable (R^2=0.998), yet raw latent MSE misranks candidates; the XY-probe rowspace accounts for less than 1% of terminal-goal latent MSE but carries most candidate-quality signal; and SCSA audits show that TRM improves the ordering and selected endpoint seen by the planner. On PushT go50/go75, TRM-style task-state metrics improve SCSA ranking and selected final distance more cleanly than closed-loop success, motivating auxiliary hybrid costs in continuous manipulation. TRM is the planner-facing repair, and audits explain when terminal reachability metrics should replace or augment raw latent proximity.

</details>


### [65] [Quantitative coronary calcification analysis for prediction of myocardial ischemia using non-contrast CT calcium scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21745)
*Juhwan Lee,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Hao Wu,Justin N. Kim,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于机器学习的方法，利用常规非增强CT钙化扫描（CTCS）中的钙化组学特征来预测心肌缺血。在1,375名患者中，结合Agatston评分、8个钙化组学特征和年龄的模型实现了98.9%的精确率、79.2%的敏感性和87.7%的F1分数。钙化组学特征显著提升了预测性能，其中钙化动脉数量与心肌缺血关联最强（OR: 3.63）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非增强CT钙化扫描（CTCS）虽广泛用于心血管风险分层，但传统方法主要依赖Agatston评分，尚未充分利用CT扫描中丰富的钙化形态和分布信息来预测心肌缺血。本研究旨在开发一种机器学习框架，通过定量冠状动脉钙化评估从常规CTCS扫描中预测心肌缺血。

Method: 回顾性分析了1,375名同时接受非增强CTCS和regadenoson负荷心脏PET心肌灌注成像的患者。提取74个变量（临床变量、Agatston评分、钙化组学特征），使用XGBoost结合SHAP进行特征选择，采用5折交叉验证训练和评估预测模型。

Result: 987名患者中89人（9%）为心肌缺血阳性。最终模型包含Agatston评分、8个钙化组学特征和年龄，精确率98.9±3.0%，敏感性79.2±8.4%，F1分数87.7±5.3%。钙化组学特征显著优于单独使用临床变量或临床变量+Agatston评分的模型（p<0.05）。钙化动脉数量虽在SHAP分析中排名最低，但在逻辑回归中与缺血关联最强（OR: 3.63）。

Conclusion: 基于常规非增强CTCS扫描的机器学习方法可有效预测心肌缺血。钙化组学特征提供了超越传统风险因素和Agatston评分的增量预测价值，有望支持更便捷的心血管风险分层。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is widely recognized as an effective tool for cardiovascular risk stratification. This study aimed to develop a novel machine learning framework for predicting myocardial ischemia from routine non-contrast CTCS scans using quantitative coronary calcium assessment. This study analyzed 1,375 patients who underwent both non-contrast CTCS and regadenoson stress cardiac positron emission tomography myocardial perfusion imaging within one year at University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center. A total of 74 variables, including clinical variables, Agatston score, and calcium-omics features, were evaluated. Relevant features were identified using XGBoost with Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Predictive models were trained and evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation. Among 987 patients, 89 (9%) were positive for myocardial ischemia. The final model incorporated the Agatston score, eight calcium-omics features, and age. The proposed model achieved a precision of 98.9+/-3.0%, sensitivity of 79.2+/-8.4, and F1 score of 87.7+/-5.3%. The addition of calcium-omics features significantly improved predictive performance compared with models using clinical variables alone or clinical variables with the Agatston score (p<0.05). Interestingly, the number of calcified arteries, despite being the lowest-ranked feature based on SHAP analysis, showed the strongest association with myocardial ischemia in logistic regression analysis (odds ratio: 3.63, 95% confidence interval: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001). We developed a machine learning approach for predicting myocardial ischemia using routinely acquired non-contrast CTCS scans. Calcium-omics features provided incremental predictive value beyond conventional risk factors and Agatston scoring and may support more accessible cardiovascular risk stratification.

</details>


### [66] [Provable Robustness against Backdoor Attacks via the Primal-Dual Perspective on Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21780)
*Aman Saxena,Jan Schuchardt,Yan Scholten,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私轮廓（privacy profiles）相结合，提出了一种模块化、紧致的端到端鲁棒性认证框架，用于联合应对训练时和后门攻击中的推理时扰动。该框架支持异构机制的复合，并基于DP-SGD和Deep Partition Aggregation在MNIST和CIFAR-10上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机平滑方法分别针对训练时（中毒攻击）和推理时（逃避攻击）提供了鲁棒性认证，但难以同时处理后门攻击中训练和测试数据被联合扰动的情况。缺乏一个统一的框架来合成分散机制并给出紧致的端到端认证。

Method: 将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私轮廓（privacy profiles）相结合，通过隐私轮廓提供异构机制组合的数值化过程，从而建立紧致的组合证书。框架模块化地复用已有的差分隐私机制分析结果，实例化为DP-SGD与Deep Partition Aggregation结合推理时平滑，导出联合鲁棒性保证。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验验证了该框架的有效性，表明所提出的组合机制能够为训练时和推理时联合攻击提供紧致的鲁棒性认证。

Conclusion: 该工作为在复杂威胁模型（更贴近真实攻击者能力）下使用复合机制进行鲁棒性认证提供了一种通用且原则性的框架。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing is a powerful tool for certifying robustness to adversarial perturbations, including poisoning attacks via randomized training and evasion attacks via randomized inference. Extending these guarantees to backdoor attacks, where training and test data are jointly perturbed, remains challenging because training- and test-time randomized mechanisms must be analyzed within a single robustness certificate. We address this by connecting randomized smoothing to the dual view of differential privacy through privacy profiles, which provide a numerical procedure for composing heterogeneous mechanisms. The resulting framework enables tight, modular, end-to-end certification of complex, composed mechanisms while leveraging existing analyses of differentially private mechanisms. We instantiate the framework for DP-SGD and Deep Partition Aggregation with inference-time smoothing, deriving joint robustness guarantees against both training-time and inference-time attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. Overall, we provide a principled and general framework for using composite mechanisms to certify robustness under complex threat models that better capture the capabilities of real-world adversaries.

</details>


### [67] [Models Can Model, But Can't Bind: Structured Grounding in Text-to-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21751)
*Zhiqi Gao,Albert Ge,Alexander Berenbeim,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Text2Opt-Bench 基准测试，发现大语言模型在文本到优化问题中随实例规模增长出现“有效绑定极限”问题，并提出 BIND 方法（将数值数据外化到结构化文件，通过编程方式绑定）显著提升准确率，同时证明绑定能力可通过微调独立于建模能力进行专门化训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到优化任务需要两种可分离的能力：建模（选择正确的优化结构）和绑定（将每个系数、索引和参数与具体问题数据对应）。现有模型在这方面的局限性尚不明确，需要系统性研究。

Method: 构建覆盖12个类别的Text2Opt-Bench基准测试（含数千变量）；提出BIND方法，将数值数据外化到结构化文件，使模型以编程方式而非转录方式绑定数据；训练仅关注绑定能力的微调模型。

Result: GPT-5-Nano准确率从59.1%提升至82.4%（匹配pass@5的82.0%且token成本更低），GPT-5从86.2%提升至95.8%；1.5B绑定专精模型匹配7B端到端基线模型性能。

Conclusion: 绑定能力是文本到优化中独立于建模能力的关键瓶颈，BIND方法通过外部化数值数据有效克服该瓶颈，且绑定能力可通过针对性微调高效专门化。

Abstract: Text-to-optimization requires two separable capabilities: modeling -- choosing the right optimization structure -- and binding -- grounding every coefficient, index, and parameter in the concrete problem data. We study this via Text2Opt-Bench, a scalable benchmark of solver-verified optimization problems spanning 12 categories, from textbook linear programs to stochastic and multi-objective formulations with up to thousands of variables. Across 10+ models, we find that accuracy collapses as instance data grows, even when the formulation itself is simple. We call this the effective binding limit. We address this via a simple inference-time approach, BIND, which externalizes numeric data to structured files so the model binds data programmatically rather than transcribing from the prompt. BIND improves GPT-5-Nano from 59.1% to 82.4% accuracy, matching pass@5 (82.0%) at lower token cost than pass@1, and GPT-5 from 86.2% to 95.8%. Furthermore, we validate our hypothesis by finetuning a model exclusively on binding and show that it outperforms end-to-end SFT and RL across three structurally distinct optimization categories, with a 1.5B binding specialist alone matching a 7B end-to-end baseline.

</details>


### [68] [An Evidence Hierarchy for Bayesian Object Classification via OSINT-Aided Heterogeneous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22259)
*Jan Nausner,Michael Hubner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CBRNE威胁检测中异构传感器融合面临的传感器能力有限、杂波率高、数据集稀缺等问题，提出了一种上下文感知与领域知识增强的融合方法，通过建立证据层级（直接/指示/上下文信息）、整合OSINT环境信息、设计贝叶斯分类机制，在模拟场景中实现了最高95%的分类准确率，并展现出对杂波和先验失配的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个传感器只能检测部分CBRNE威胁且可靠性不一，有的仅提供间接威胁指示；传感器端杂波率高；高质量数据集稀缺限制了基于学习的检测与分类模型的发展。

Method: 1) 建立新型证据层级，对直接信息、指示信息和上下文信息进行建模；2) 通过收集、处理OSINT输入，将环境上下文信息引入融合过程；3) 利用证据层级的所有级别，构建具有领域知识先验的贝叶斯威胁类型分类机制。

Result: 在模拟场景中评估，整体分类准确率最高达95%，对杂波和先验失配具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的上下文感知与领域知识增强的融合方法有效缓解了传感器固有缺陷，提升了威胁分类的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous sensor fusion is vital for detecting, localizing, and classifying CBRNE threats. However, individual sensors are often only capable of detecting a subset of relevant threats with varying reliability or can even provide only indirect threat indications, making threat classification challenging. Furthermore, high clutter rates on the sensor side present a great challenge for fusion systems. Additionally, the limited availability of high quality datasets hinders the advancement of learning-based detection and classification models in smart sensors. To mitigate these sensor related shortcomings, a context-aware and domain knowledge-enhanced fusion process is proposed. First, a novel evidence hierarchy is established that enables modeling of direct, indicative, and contextual information. Second, contextual information about the environment is introduced into the fusion process, by collecting, processing, and exploiting OSINT inputs. Third, all levels of the evidence hierarchy are used to craft a Bayesian threat type classification mechanism with domain knowledge-informed priors. The proposed methodology is evaluated in simulated scenarios, and the results demonstrate the benefit of the proposed fusion approach in terms of robustness to clutter and prior mismatch, with an overall classification accuracy of up to 95%.

</details>


### [69] [Optimal Guarantees for Auditing Rényi Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21938)
*Benjamin D. Kim,Lav R. Varshney,Daniel Alabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于假设检验的黑盒审计框架，利用Donsker-Varadhan变分估计直接估计Rényi散度，以审计声称满足Rényi差分隐私（RDP）的机器学习算法。该方法提供了显式且非渐近的置信区间，并证明了匹配的极小化最优下界，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上对DP-SGD的审计显著优于现有黑盒方法，尤其是在小到中等Rényi阶数下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒审计方法在估计RDP保证时缺乏严格的统计置信度和最优样本复杂度，尤其是在小到中等Rényi阶数下审计精度不足。

Method: 基于假设检验的审计框架，使用Donsker-Varadhan变分估计器直接估计Rényi散度；通过类别受限的DV估计器导出显式非渐近置信区间；将统计估计误差与算法隐私泄露分离；证明了匹配的极小化最优下界。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上对DP-SGD进行黑盒审计，在多种隐私预算下，相较于现有最优黑盒方法，所提审计器在经验RDP下界上取得了显著提升，尤其在小到中等Rényi阶数下改进最为明显。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在理论上建立了通过DV估计器审计RDP的最优样本复杂度保证，并在实践中验证了其有效性，为黑盒RDP审计提供了统计严谨且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study black-box auditing for machine learning algorithms that claim R \ 'enyi differential privacy (RDP) guarantees. We introduce an auditing framework, based on hypothesis testing, that directly estimates Rényi divergence between neighboring executions using the Donsker-Varadhan (DV) variational estimator. Our analysis yields explicit and non-asymptotic confidence intervals for RDP auditing via class-restricted DV estimators, separating statistical estimation error from algorithmic privacy leakage. We prove matching minimax lower bounds showing that, up to logarithmic factors, our sample-complexity guarantees are information-theoretically optimal, thereby establishing the first optimal guarantees for auditing RDP via DV estimators. Empirically, we instantiate our framework for auditing DP-SGD in a fully black-box setting. Across MNIST and CIFAR-10, and over a wide range of privacy regimes, our auditors produce a strong overall improvement on empirical RDP lower bounds compared to prior state-of-the-art black-box methods especially at small and moderate Rényi orders where accurate auditing is most challenging.

</details>


### [70] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对用户行为强度不平衡（高活跃用户信号被过度放大）导致推荐质量下降的问题，提出非参数对比百分位框架PEARL，通过相对偏好信号替代绝对交互量，结合对比采样、预测引导自举平滑、广义加权与协同训练策略，在离线实验与数十亿用户规模的直播平台线上A/B测试中均显著提升推荐效果（观看时长+2.10%，消费量+0.80%，交互率+1.49%，举报率-6.91%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统训练于用户交互数据时，面临行为强度不平衡问题——不同用户的参与模式差异导致观测交互不再如实反映真实偏好，高活跃用户信号被过度放大而其他用户被低估，最终损害推荐质量与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出PEARL框架：(1) 非参数对比百分位近似，通过真实对比交互样本直接建模百分位关系，无需辅助分布估计模型；(2) 相对优势去偏机制，理论证明成对比较可给出基于百分位的无偏偏好信号估计；(3) 预测引导自举机制处理稀疏离散反馈；(4) 广义值加权公式与协同训练策略增强建模灵活性与表示学习。

Result: 离线实验：有效缓解行为偏差，在多个排序目标上持续提升推荐性能。线上A/B测试（数十亿用户直播平台）：观看时长+2.10%，消费量+0.80%，交互率+1.49%，举报率-6.91%。

Conclusion: PEARL框架通过相对偏好信号建模有效解决了行为强度不平衡问题，理论完备且在大规模生产环境中取得显著收益。

Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users.
