Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [143] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三元决策树（Ternary Decision Trees），在每个分割节点引入宽度为delta的不确定区域，对落入该区域的样本进行概率加权混合预测并标记为边界不确定。基于CART现有统计量，提出了五种delta估计方法。在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上，所有方法在决策准确率上显著优于标准CART，其中margin方法效率最优（每单位标记率提升0.104准确率），无需额外超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准决策树使用硬二元阈值分割特征空间，对远离决策边界的实例和恰好在决策边界上的实例赋予相同的置信度，这在面对边界样本时缺乏合理的处理机制。

Method: 提出三元决策树，在分割节点添加半宽为delta的不确定区域；提出了五种delta估计方法：quality-plateau（分裂准则曲线平台宽度）、class-overlap（类别分布重叠度）、gain-ratio（分裂质量相对分裂熵）、node-bootstrap（节点重采样的阈值方差）、margin（SVM启发的类间最近样本距离）。在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上采用5折交叉验证评估。

Result: 五种方法在决策准确率上均显著优于标准CART（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p<0.001）。margin方法效率最优（每单位边界不确定标记率提升0.104准确率），在42/72数据集上获胜，无需额外超参数。在Breiman合成数据上margin具有自校准特性，在乳腺X光检查数据集上node-bootstrap通过标记10.8%的筛查病例为边界不确定，实现了+0.71%的决策准确率提升。

Conclusion: 三元决策树通过在边界区域引入不确定性建模和加权混合预测，有效提升了决策准确率。不同场景可选用不同的delta估计方法：margin适用于通用场景，node-bootstrap和quality-plateau更擅长追踪理论不可约误差。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [144] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SeqLoRA，一种基于序列正则化和双层优化的持续学习框架，用于参数高效微调中多概念组合的文本到图像扩散模型，在避免昂贵后验融合的同时提升多概念身份保持和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调可实现文本到图像扩散模型的快速个性化，但组合多个自定义概念时存在表示干扰问题。现有模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后验融合，要么冻结适配子空间，限制了表达能力和概念保真度。

Method: 提出SeqLoRA框架，采用约束持续学习范式，通过双层优化联合优化两个LoRA因子。将残差层激活建模为矩阵次高斯过程，推导遗忘灾难的高概率界，并从理论上证明从数据中学习LoRA基比冻结基方法更有效地最小化残差干扰能量。

Result: 在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA在多达101个概念上提升了身份保持和可扩展性，避免了昂贵的融合操作，并减少了组合生成中的属性干扰。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA通过持续学习框架有效解决了多概念组合中的表示干扰问题，在概念保真度、可扩展性和计算成本之间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [145] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化证据深度学习（EDL）目标函数的方法，通过将Dirichlet期望损失近似为在Dirichlet均值处评估的插件损失，并证明在温和假设下近似误差随证据增长而衰减。该方法在Google语音命令数据集上实现了与经典EDL相当的预测精度和选择性预测性能，且更易于实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDL通过狄利克雷分布提供单次不确定性估计，但其Dirichlet期望目标函数比标准监督学习损失更复杂，难以分析和实现。本文旨在简化EDL的目标函数以降低计算和实现复杂度。

Method: 将EDL的一阶经验风险最小化目标近似为在Dirichlet均值处评估的插件损失，并证明对于包括均方误差和交叉熵在内的广泛损失函数，近似误差随证据增长而衰减。作为特例，该框架为标准softmax分类器在不确定性估计中的使用提供了理论依据。

Result: 在Google语音命令数据集上，简化目标函数在预测精度和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，同时更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程实现。

Conclusion: 本文提出的简化EDL目标函数在保持性能的同时大幅降低了实现复杂度，并且首次在语音识别任务中通过EDL获得了覆盖-精度权衡曲线。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [146] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lumberjack 是一种差分隐私随机森林算法，通过构建大型随机决策树并应用隐私保护剪枝，实现了比现有方法更高的实用性，其中关键创新是一种针对层次数据的 $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP 重击者检测算法，其误差为 $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$，使得在隐私约束下可以使用更深的树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私随机森林方法在保护隐私时性能严重下降，几乎无法实际应用。本文旨在设计一种既能提供严格差分隐私保证，又能保持较高实用性的随机森林算法，缩小隐私保护与模型效用之间的差距。

Method: 提出 Lumberjack 算法：1) 构建大型随机决策树（不直接施加隐私约束）；2) 应用隐私保护剪枝策略，仅保留满足最小节点规模要求的节点；3) 核心创新是一种 $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP 重击者检测算法，用于层次数据，误差以 $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ 增长，支持更深的树结构。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，Lumberjack 在隐私-效用权衡上持续优于现有的差分隐私随机森林方法，树立了新的最先进水平，显著提升了在实用隐私预算下的模型表现。

Conclusion: 精心设计的差分隐私随机森林能够在很大程度上弥补隐私保护带来的效用损失，为未来研究提供了一个有前景且尚未充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [147] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了均匀扩散模型（UDM）中干净数据预测与反向动力学参数化之间的不匹配问题，提出了留一法后验分布（leave-one-out posterior）作为正确的优化目标，并推导了去噪器、留一法后验与得分之间的精确转换关系。进一步引入吸收态重构（absorbing-state reformulation），将UDM分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作，在语言建模任务上证明了留一法参数化的一致改进效果，表明掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间的经验差距主要来自参数化和采样设计而非边际分布本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型中，掩码扩散模型（MDM）和均匀扩散模型（UDM）在干净数据预测的使用上存在差异：MDM中不同参数化方式基本一致，而UDM中标准插件桥参数化（plug-in bridge parameterization）并不被去噪后验所优化，而是被留一法后验所优化，这导致了插件ELBO与通常的交叉熵去噪目标之间存在不匹配问题。作者旨在厘清这种不匹配的根源，并寻找更优的参数化与采样设计。

Method: 1) 理论分析：识别出UDM的标准插件桥参数化实际上优化的是留一法后验而非标准去噪后验；2) 推导了去噪器、留一法后验和得分之间的精确转换关系，从而解耦参数化与训练目标；3) 提出基于留一法预测的改进推断方法：信息型预测-校正采样器（informed predictor-corrector sampler）和改进的温度采样；4) 引入吸收态重构（absorbing-state reformulation），将UDM的联合分布分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作，具有更简化的去噪后验、延续去掩码（carry-over unmasking）和自然的重掩码机制。

Result: 在语言建模任务上，留一法参数化一致地提升了UDM的生成质量；吸收态重构方法能够匹配或超越掩码扩散的性能。实验结果表明掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间的经验差距主要源于参数化和采样设计，而非边际分布本身。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实验验证，本文揭示了UDM中参数化与训练目标之间的不匹配问题，并提供了系统的解决方案。留一法后验和吸收态重构方法有效弥合了均匀扩散与掩码扩散之间的性能差距，为离散扩散模型的参数化设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [148] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"匹配原则"：许多看似独立的鲁棒性、域适应、不变性等方法的共享统计本质是估计标签保持的部署扰动协方差，并沿该协方差范围正则化编码器雅可比矩阵。在线性高斯模型中给出了闭式最优解，并引入无标签的轨迹偏差指数(TDI)作为探测指标。通过13个预注册实验(包括Qwen2.5-7B)验证了理论预测，12个通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前鲁棒性、域适应、光度不变性、遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全性和经典各向异性正则化通常被视为独立问题并拥有独立的方法族。作者认为它们共享一个统计问题结构，应统一处理。

Method: 1) 形式化定义"部署扰动协方差"并建立"匹配原则"；2) 在线性高斯模型中证明闭式最优解，包括立方根注水填充、范围覆盖必要性等；3) 提出无标签的轨迹偏差指数(TDI)；4) 通过13个预注册实验(从经典ML到Qwen2.5-7B)进行验证。

Result: 13个预注册实验中12个通过；唯一的例外(Office-31)被识别为特征值缺口失败并在实验前已命名；在7B规模上，匹配的style-PMH改进了选择性诚实性并保持了Style TDI，而标准DPO使其退化。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于命名了部署扰动协方差这一关键对象，明确了正则化器必须满足的条件，并提供了一个可证伪的闭式理论。不追求在所有榜单上的通用性，而是聚焦于识别核心统计问题并给出可检验的理论框架。

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [149] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合在线3D重建（持久世界模型）和序列策略（情节上下文）的好奇心驱动探索方法，在稀疏奖励、长周期3D环境中实现高效探索，并零样本泛化到新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有好奇心驱动强化学习在复杂逼真3D环境中失效，智能体会陷入局部循环，因为缺乏空间持久性和情节上下文，导致重复访问遗忘状态时获得虚假奖励。

Method: 使用在线3D重建作为持久且持续更新的世界模型，同时将策略参数化为基于RGB观测的序列模型以维护情节轨迹历史，通过两者结合实现有效的好奇心探索。

Result: 在HM3D上纯好奇心训练即超越RL主动建图基线，零样本泛化到Gibson和AI生成世界；下游任务（摘苹果、图像目标导航）中优于从头训练的基线。

Conclusion: 持久世界模型+情节上下文的组合是好奇心驱动探索在复杂3D环境中成功的关键，且学到的端到端策略可直接泛化并高效适配下游任务。

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Autonomous LLM Agents & CTFs: A Second Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21497)
*Youness Bouchari,Matteo Boffa,Marco Mellia,Idilio Drago,Thanh Minh Bui,Dario Rossi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在30个web CTF挑战（覆盖14个漏洞类别）上评估了多种LLM智能体架构，发现：通用智能体claude-code表现与专门设计的架构相当（19/30），但两者均未达到人类水平；结构化专业角色编排优于单体设计，能提高一致性并降低执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究声称LLM智能体在自动化攻击性安全任务中达到接近人类水平，本文旨在重新审视这些结论，通过系统化评估验证其真实能力水平。

Method: 设计了复杂度递增的多模块化智能体架构，在30个web CTF挑战（14个漏洞类别）上使用多个LLM骨干进行测试，并与通用智能体claude-code进行对比。

Result: 1) claude-code与专门架构性能相当（19/30）；2) 所有架构在相同挑战类别上表现困难，均未达到人类水平；3) 结构化专业角色编排优于单体设计，提升运行一致性并降低成本。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在攻击性安全任务中尚未达到人类水平，通用智能体可作为强基线，结构化编排是提升性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed to automate offensive security tasks, with recent studies reporting near human-level success rates in Capture-the-Flag (CTF) challenges. We here revisit these results, providing a second look at these claims. We engineer different agent architectures of increasing complexity and modularity on 30 web-based CTFs challenges spanning 14 vulnerability classes. We instantiate these agents with multiple LLM backbones, and compare them with claude-code, a general-purpose agent that automatically determines its internal architecture. Our evaluation yields three main findings. First, claude-code achieves performance comparable to the engineered architectures (19/30 solved tasks), suggesting that general-purpose agents are strong baselines for offensive security tasks. Second, both our architectures and claude-code struggle in the same challenge categories, revealing persistent barriers that keep current agents below human-level capability. Third, by leveraging our manually designed architectures we can systematically measure the impact of additional components, finding that structured orchestration of specialized roles outperforms monolithic designs, improving run-to-run consistency, and reducing execution costs.

</details>


### [151] [Polars inside Intel SGX2 Enclaves: An Empirical Study of Confidential Analytical Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21797)
*Wei Wang,Burns Smith,Kenny Leftin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Intel SGX2可信执行环境中运行Arrow-native DataFrame引擎Polaris的性能开销，发现端到端开销约为1.5倍，但表加载时间开销从2.27倍增加到4.07倍，而查询开销略有下降；惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27倍，且在大数据量下急切执行会出现内存不足错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE性能研究主要集中在SQL数据库引擎或早期SGX1代，缺乏对Arrow-native DataFrame引擎在SGX2上的系统性能评估，特别是对计算开销与数据摄取开销的分离分析。

Method: 在Azure Blob Storage上使用Gramine运行Polars（Arrow-native DataFrame引擎）于Intel SGX2 enclave中，基于TPC-H SF30基准测试，设置四种数据集宽度配置（约22-73 GB），分别测量标准TPC-H功率分数和仅查询变体以分离计算与数据加载开销，并对比惰性与急切API性能。

Result: 端到端开销保持约1.49-1.56倍；查询开销从1.51-1.52倍降至1.43-1.44倍，而表加载开销从2.27倍增至4.07倍；中位每查询SGX减速为1.45倍，最大2.57倍；惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27倍，急切API在41GB及以上出现OOM。

Conclusion: SGX2能够支持Arrow-native分析处理，安全开销与最近DuckDB-SGX2研究相当，但加载路径放大和API级优化是端到端性能的一阶决定因素。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have renewed interest in confidential analytics, but most prior evaluations focus on SQL database engines or earlier SGX generations. This paper studies an Arrow-native DataFrame engine, Polars, running inside Intel SGX2 enclaves via Gramine on TPC-H SF30 with Azure Blob Storage. We report both the standard TPC-H power score and a query-only variant that removes table-loading time in order to separate compute overhead from data-ingestion overhead. Across four dataset-width configurations (approximately 22-73 GB), end-to-end overhead remains nearly constant at 1.49-1.56$\times$, but this composite metric obscures two distinct behaviors: query-only overhead declines from 1.51-1.52$\times$ to 1.43-1.44$\times$, whereas table-loading overhead rises from 2.27$\times$ to 4.07$\times$. We further show that overhead is not uniform across queries: for the len130 configuration, the median per-query SGX slowdown is 1.45$\times$ with a maximum of 2.57$\times$, and a small set of queries exhibits pronounced run-to-run spikes consistent with stateful EPC pressure. Finally, we compare Polars' lazy and eager APIs under the same TEE setting. Lazy execution is 2.25-2.27$\times$ faster overall, while eager execution fails with out-of-memory errors at 41 GB and above. Relative to the recent DuckDB-SGX2 study, our results suggest that SGX2 can support Arrow-native analytical processing with a similar order of security overhead, but that load-path amplification and API-level optimization are first-order determinants of end-to-end performance.

</details>


### [152] [Chain Reactions: How Nonce Collisions in ECDSA Compromise Polygon MEV Searchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21498)
*Yash Madhwal,Andrey Seoev,Raffaele Della Pietra,Anastasiia Smirnova,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Polygon MEV生态系统中由于非ce重用导致的ECDSA私钥恢复漏洞，攻击者可通过线性代数方法从可预测的签名模式中恢复私钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易依赖ECDSA签名，而nonce的生成安全性至关重要。Polygon MEV生态中searcher为满足密封投标拍卖的亚秒级响应时间需求，采用了可预测的nonce模式，导致严重安全风险。

Method: 通过链上数据分析发现nonce重用模式，利用签名间的线性关系构建线性方程组，实现私钥恢复。攻击包括同一钱包内nonce重用和跨钱包nonce碰撞两种情况。

Result: 成功在Polygon链上发现可被利用的nonce模式，证明了协议引入的延迟压力如何导致生产区块链系统出现灾难性密码学失效，单一实现错误可同时危及多个账户。

Conclusion: 协议设计（密封投标拍卖的亚秒级响应需求）间接导致了密码学实现缺陷（nonce重用），使得被动攻击者可通过初等代数恢复私钥，暴露了区块链协议层面与密码学安全之间的深层矛盾。

Abstract: ECDSA signatures form the bedrock of blockchain transaction authentication, yet their security critically depends on proper nonce generation. We uncover a critical vulnerability in the Polygon MEV ecosystem: systematic nonce reuse that enables complete private key recovery. Analyzing on-chain data reveals that searchers, driven by the need for sub-second response times in sealed-bid auctions, employ predictable nonce patterns. These patterns create linear relationships between signatures, allowing passive attackers to recover private keys using elementary algebra. We provide a compact linear-system formulation for such attacks, including the dangerous case of cross-wallet nonce collisions, and present concrete evidence of exploitable patterns on Polygon. Our findings demonstrate how protocol-induced latency pressures can lead to catastrophic cryptographic failures in production blockchain systems, where a single implementation error compromises multiple accounts simultaneously.

</details>


### [153] [TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22709)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriSweep是一个四无人机编队仿真框架，通过空间分集采集（全频谱锚点、掩码探针、密码探针）和相干合并（+4.8 dB SNR增益）实现对嵌入式微控制器（0.25-1.5 m距离）的自主电磁侧信道分析，在ANSSI ASCAD掩码AES-128数据集上达到18±1.7的密钥排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁侧信道分析假设探针静止且紧贴芯片，低估了空中 adversaries 的威胁能力，亟需探索无人机编队实现远距离自主侧信道攻击的可行性。

Method: 提出四无人机编队架构：1) Anchor（全频谱采集）；2) Mask Probe（掩码寄存器加载泄漏）；3) Cipher Probe（掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）；4) Accumulator（静止、相干合并+二阶掩码消除）。采用基于互相关的对齐补偿无人机悬停振动，双通道CNN进行分类。

Result: 在0.25 m距离下，掩码AES-128主数据集密钥排名18±1.7（五种子）；相干合并实现+4.8 dB SNR增益；互相关对齐将100样本抖动变体的单机排名从89降至21；双通道CNN损失0.454（随机基线5.545）。

Conclusion: 仿真表明无人机编队自主远距离EM-SCA在原理上可行且性能显著优于单机方案，但尚未进行硬件原型验证，硬件实现是下一步计划。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.

</details>


### [154] [ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY: A Cross-Build Binary Dataset with Temporal Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21615)
*Chang Liu,Noah Fleischmann,Nicolò Altamura,Edward Raff,James Holt,Kristopher Micinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY，一个整合了跨构建多样性、跨版本历史及CVE标签的统一二进制语料库，包含73,610个二进制文件，覆盖248个开源项目，支持GCC/Clang/MSVC多编译器、多优化级别及Linux/Windows平台的多年度历史构建，并通过三项分析验证其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制语料库仅能捕获一个或两个轴的二进制变异性（如仅提供跨编译器构建而无时间轴，或仅有单构建二进制文件的CVE标签），未能将跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签整合为可查询的结构化数据。

Method: 构建统一框架，将每个二进制的编译上下文、源代码、脆弱函数和包版本作为一等元数据存储；数据库将每个二进制与其源代码、函数、调试信息、变体构建、历史版本和脆弱函数关联；通过三项分析验证：（1）三阶段LLM基准测试；（2）对比MalConv嵌入、jTrans函数嵌入和TLSH模糊哈希的聚类效果；（3）贝叶斯回归分解二进制相似性。

Result: 数据集包含73,610个二进制文件、248个开源项目、多编译器（GCC/Clang/MSVC）、多优化级别、Linux/Windows平台及多年度历史构建。三项分析分别评估了LLM推理能力、嵌入空间聚类效果和相似性贡献因素。

Conclusion: ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY填补了现有语料库的空白，将跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签统一为可查询的结构化框架，为二进制安全分析（特别是LLM推理和漏洞检测）提供了更全面的数据基础。

Abstract: Existing binary corpora typically capture only one or two axes of binary variation: they either provide cross-compiler builds without a temporal axis, or CVE labels for single-build binaries. None combine cross-build diversity, cross-version history, and CVE labels into a queryable structure. We present ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY, which consolidates these dimensions into a unified framework where every binary's compilation context, source code, vulnerable functions, and package version are stored as first-class metadata.
  ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY comprises 73,610 binaries spanning 248 open-source projects, compiled across GCC, Clang, and MSVC at multiple optimization levels on Linux and Windows, with multi-year historical builds. Each binary is indexed in a database that links it to its source code, functions, debug info, variant builds, historical versions, and vulnerable functions. Three analyses demonstrate this structure's value: (1) a three-stage LLM benchmark (recognition, strategy-guided detection, and cross-build transfer) to test whether LLMs reason about binary vulnerabilities or pattern-match on build-specific artifacts; (2) a comparison of MalConv embeddings, jTrans function embeddings, and TLSH fuzzy hashes quantifying how same-package versions cluster in each space; and (3) a Bayesian regression decomposing binary similarity into contributions from temporal distance, file changes, and commits.

</details>


### [155] [Adversarial Reframing: A Framework for Targeted Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21674)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Swati Kar,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: THREAT是一个推理驱动的多LLM协作框架，通过迭代搜索将提示发现建模为非凸优化问题，以更高成功率和更低计算成本生成越狱提示，暴露对齐LLM的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在真实场景中广泛部署，但仍易受基于提示的越狱攻击绕过安全过滤器，亟需高效的自动化越狱框架来发现这些漏洞并加强模型安全。

Method: 将提示发现问题公式化为非凸优化问题，提出THREAT框架，协调多个LLM在迭代搜索循环中进行推理驱动搜索，以高效发现文本越狱提示。

Result: 在多种数据集和模型架构上，THREAT相比现有方法实现了更高的攻击成功率（ASR）和更低的计算成本；其生成的提示被标记为有害的比例低于1%，而对应未修改提示的拒绝率约为50%。

Conclusion: THREAT揭示了已对齐LLM中先前未被检测到的漏洞，可作为主动增强基础模型安全性的实用工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in diverse real-world settings, yet remain vulnerable to jailbreaking, where prompt-based attacks bypass safety filters. We present THREAT (Targeted Harmful generation via Reframing and Exploitation of Adversarial Tactics), a reasoning-driven framework that coordinates multiple LLMs in an iterative search loop to find textual jailbreak prompts. We formulate prompt discovery as a nonconvex optimization problem and provide an efficient solution that lowers runtime and improves attack effectiveness. Across diverse datasets and model architectures, THREAT delivers higher attack success rates with lower computational cost than prior methods. The crafted prompts were flagged as harmful in fewer than 1% of cases, compared with about 50% refusals for the corresponding unmodified prompts. These findings reveal previously undetected vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs and position THREAT as a practical tool for proactively strengthening the safety of foundation models.

</details>


### [156] [FuzzingBrain V2: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Vulnerability Discovery and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21779)
*Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Kewen Zhu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FuzzingBrain V2 是一个基于多智能体的全自动漏洞检测系统，结合OSS-Fuzz、控制流抽象（Suspicious Point）、分层函数分析与双层面模糊测试、MCP工具，在AIxCC 2025决赛数据集上达到90%检出率，并在真实世界中发现29个零日漏洞（2个已分配CVE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测存在三大问题：高误报率且不可复现验证、子优粒度导致定位不准（函数级上下文不足，行级缺乏上下文）、难以推理复杂跨函数依赖和触发条件的漏洞。

Method: (1) 基于Google OSS-Fuzz的全自动化分析，确保漏洞可复现；(2) Suspicious Point——基于控制流的新型抽象，实现最优粒度的精确定位；(3) 逻辑驱动的分层函数分析+双层面模糊测试，在资源约束下增强函数覆盖；(4) 基于MCP的静态与动态分析工具+上下文工程，增强复杂漏洞推理能力。

Result: AIxCC 2025决赛C/C++数据集上检出率90%（40个漏洞中检出36个）；真实部署中发现29个零日漏洞（12个开源项目），全部被维护者确认修复，2个已获CVE编号。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain V2通过结合模糊测试可复现验证与多智能体LLM分析，有效解决了高误报和复杂漏洞推理难题，在竞赛与现实场景中均展现出优异的检测能力。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities pose critical security threats, with nearly 50,000 CVEs reported in 2025. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated vulnerability detection, three key challenges remain. First, LLM-generated vulnerability reports suffer from high false positive rates and lack
  reproducible verification. Second, existing LLM-based approaches use suboptimal granularities for vulnerability localization: function-level analysis overlooks bugs when context becomes extensive, while line-level analysis lacks sufficient context. Third, existing approaches have difficulty reasoning about
  vulnerabilities with complex cross-function dependencies and triggering conditions.
  We present FuzzingBrain V2, a multi-agent system that addresses these gaps through four key contributions: (1) fully automated vulnerability analysis built on Google's OSS-Fuzz, ensuring all reported vulnerabilities are fuzzer-reproducible; (2) Suspicious Point, a novel control-flow-based abstraction for precise
  vulnerability localization at the optimal granularity; (3) logic-driven hierarchical function analysis with dual-layer fuzzing enhancing function coverage under resource constraints; (4) MCP-based static and dynamic analysis tools with context engineering enhancing complex vulnerability reasoning.
  On the AIxCC 2025 Final Competition C/C++ dataset, FuzzingBrain V2 achieved 90% detection rate (36 of 40 vulnerabilities). In real-world deployment, FuzzingBrain V2 discovered 29 zero-day vulnerabilities across 12 open-source projects, all confirmed and fixed by maintainers, with 2 assigned CVE IDs.

</details>


### [157] [Graph Structure of Chebyshev Permutation Polynomials over Binary and Ternary Adic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21819)
*Xiaoxiong Lu,Yuling Dai,Chengqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了环 $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$ 上Chebyshev置换多项式的函数图结构，给出了路径长度和循环结构的显式刻画，证明了图具有强正则性，包括固定长度的循环数量和可预测的分支模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限域上非线性映射的函数图结构对于分析其动力复杂性及其在密码学和伪随机生成中的潜在应用至关重要，本文旨在扩展先前在素幂环上的研究。

Method: 基于Chebyshev多项式模2和模3幂次的新性质，将环中元素视为顶点，多项式映射关系视为有向边，分析函数图的路径长度和循环结构。

Result: 证明了尽管存在二元和三元分量的复杂性，函数图仍表现出强正则性，包括固定长度的循环数量恒定以及随 $k_1$ 和 $k_2$ 增长的可预测分支模式。

Conclusion: 本文结果扩展了素幂环上的研究，为理解数字非线性映射中复杂性的涌现提供了见解，并支持其密码学应用的安全性分析。

Abstract: Understanding the functional graph of a nonlinear map over a finite domain is crucial for analyzing its dynamical complexity and potential applications in cryptography and pseudorandom generation. In this paper, we investigate the graph structure of Chebyshev permutation polynomials over the ring $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$, where $k_1$ and $k_2$ are positive integers and $0\in\{k_1, k_2\}$. Each element of the ring is regarded as a vertex, and the mapping relation defined by the polynomial corresponds to a directed edge. Building on new properties of Chebyshev polynomials modulo powers of $2$ and $3$, we provide an explicit characterization of path lengths and cycle structures in the functional graph. We show that, despite the complexities introduced by the binary and ternary components, the graph exhibits strong regularities, including a constant number of cycles of a given length and predictable branching patterns as $k_1$ and $k_2$ increase. Our results extend previous studies over prime-power rings, offering insights into the emergence of complexity in digital nonlinear maps and supporting the security analysis of their cryptographic applications.

</details>


### [158] [A Large Language Model Approach to Generating Bypass Rules for Malware Evasion in Analysis Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21821)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABLE系统，利用大语言模型自动生成YARA规则以绕过沙箱环境中的逃逸检测，通过分析恶意软件执行轨迹、多推理策略、自动清洗和迭代优化，在334个真实样本上实现79%的绕过成功率，比现有平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沙箱逃逸检测依赖人工逆向分析的硬编码绕过规则，无法有效应对快速演变的逃逸技术，亟需自动化、可扩展的绕过规则生成方法。

Method: ABLE利用LLM分析因潜在逃逸行为而终止的恶意软件执行轨迹，采用多推理策略生成目标YARA绕过规则，结合自动清洗管道和基于反馈的迭代优化来修正语法错误并提升规则有效性。

Result: 在334个真实恶意软件样本和四个开源LLM上，ABLE达到79%的绕过成功率，其中迭代优化贡献了29.5%的成功案例；相比现有平台，多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类并暴露了先前隐藏的行为。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的自动化沙箱绕过规则生成方法显著优于传统人工逆向方法，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Sandbox evasion remains a critical challenge for automated malware analysis, as modern malware employs environment checks to detect analysis platforms and suppress malicious behavior. Existing approaches rely on manually crafted bypass rules that require deep reverse engineering of each evasion mechanism -an approach that cannot scale against rapidly evolving evasion techniques. In this paper, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate YARA rules that bypass evasion checks in sandbox environments. We propose ABLE, which analyzes execution traces from malware terminated due to potentially evasive behavior and employs multiple reasoning strategies to generate targeted bypass rules. To address syntactic errors and improve the efficacy of the bypass rules in the LLM outputs, we introduce an auto-sanitization pipeline and feedback-driven iterative refinement. We evaluate ABLE on 334 real-world malware samples across four open-weight LLMs. ABLE achieves a 79% bypass success rate, with iterative refinement contributing 29.5% of successful cases. Compared to existing analysis platforms, ABLE identifies 47% more malware family classifications and exposes previously hidden behaviors.

</details>


### [159] [SPIDER: Two Server Functionality for the Cost of Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21857)
*Ofir Dvir,Kali Hale,Javin Zipkin,Divyakant Agrawal,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出baseSPIDER和SPIDER两种私有信息检索方案：baseSPIDER在单服务器有状态客户端场景下实现了渐进最优通信复杂度并改进了常数因子；SPIDER是首个无需服务器协作即可在默认数据库接口上运行的单一服务器PIR方案，通过简单转换baseSPIDER实现，消除了部署障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单服务器PIR方案在通信复杂度上存在常数因子效率问题，且大多需要服务器提供专用API、协议特定交互或辅助状态支持，导致部署门槛高，难以直接应用于现有系统。

Method: baseSPIDER采用预处理和存储提示信息的单服务器有状态客户端范式，实现渐进最优通信复杂度并改进常数因子。SPIDER通过将baseSPIDER转换到默认服务器接口范式构建，无需服务器协作或专用API。该转换方法可推广至其他三种近期PIR方案。

Result: baseSPIDER在单服务器有状态客户端设置中匹配最优渐进复杂度并提升常数因子；SPIDER成为首个在默认服务器接口上运行的单一服务器PIR方案。转换方法可适配其他三种PIR方案，但SPIDER设计更简单，代价是客户端计算开销更高。

Conclusion: 本文通过baseSPIDER和SPIDER推动了PIR的实际部署可行性，SPIDER的默认服务器设计消除了传统PIR的部署障碍，其转换方法具有通用性和独立研究价值。

Abstract: We introduce baseSPIDER and SPIDER, private information retrieval (PIR) schemes that embody two technical advancements. The baseSPIDER protocol operates with a single server and a stateful client that performs pre-processing and stores hints for future queries. In this setting, baseSPIDER introduces a new approach that matches the asymptotically optimal communication complexity of state-of-the-art schemes while improving constant factors--an advantage that is particularly significant for databases with large entries. In addition, baseSPIDER offers a conceptually simpler design relative to prior protocols. SPIDER operates over a default database interface and requires no cooperation from the server at any stage. To our knowledge, SPIDER is the first single-server PIR construction of this design, achieving privacy without specialized APIs, auxiliary server state, or protocol-specific interaction beyond conventional indexed access. SPIDER is built via a simple transformation of baseSPIDER to the default server setting, eliminating deployment barriers and enabling immediate applicability to existing systems. This transformation can be applied more broadly to three recent PIR solutions, adapting them for use in the default-server paradigm and yielding solutions of independent interest. SPIDER compares to the resulting modified solutions by exhibiting a simpler design while incurring higher client computational work.

</details>


### [160] [Blind Spots in the Guard: How Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks Evade Detection in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22001)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并形式化了LLM注入检测中的"领域伪装注入"漏洞：当注入载荷模仿目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构时，标准检测器失效，检测率从93.8%骤降至9.7%（Llama 3.1 8B）和从100%降至55.6%（Gemini 2.0 Flash）。跨45个任务、3个领域和2个模型家族，伪装检测缺口（CDG）显著且统计可信。Llama Guard 3检测率为零。多智能体辩论架构放大静态注入攻击，而针对性检测增强仅提供部分修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM注入检测器基于静态模板载荷校准，存在系统性的盲点：当注入载荷模仿目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构时，标准检测器无法识别，需要正式定义并量化这一漏洞。

Method: 1) 形式化定义领域伪装注入（Domain Camouflaged Injection）和伪装检测缺口（CDG）；2) 构建跨45个任务、3个领域、2个模型家族的评估体系；3) 测试多种检测器（少样本检测器、Llama Guard 3、多智能体辩论架构）；4) 评估针对性检测增强效果。

Result: 静态检测率从93.8%降至9.7%（Llama）和100%降至55.6%（Gemini）；CDG统计显著（p<0.001）；Llama Guard 3检测率为0%；多智能体辩论将静态攻击放大9.9x（小模型）；检测增强仅带来10.2%（Llama）和78.7%（Gemini）的提升。

Conclusion: 领域伪装注入是LLM安全系统架构性的、非附带的漏洞，尤其对弱模型影响严重；现有检测方法无法有效防御；多智能体架构可能放大而非缓解此类攻击。

Abstract: Injection detectors deployed to protect LLM agents are calibrated on static, template-based payloads that announce themselves as override directives. We identify a systematic blind spot: when payloads are generated to mimic the domain vocabulary and authority structures of the target document, what we call domain camouflaged injection, standard detectors fail to flag them, with detection rates dropping from 93.8% to 9.7% on Llama 3.1 8B and from 100% to 55.6% on Gemini 2.0 Flash. We formalize this as the Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG), the difference in injection detection rate between static and camouflaged payloads. Across 45 tasks spanning three domains and two model families, CDG is large and statistically significant (chi^2 = 38.03, p < 0.001 for Llama; chi^2 = 17.05, p < 0.001 for Gemini), with zero reverse discordant pairs in either case. We additionally evaluate Llama Guard 3, a production safety classifier, which detects zero camouflage payloads (IDRcamouflage = 0.000), confirming that the blind spot extends beyond few-shot detectors to dedicated safety classifiers. We further show that multi-agent debate architectures amplify static injection attacks by up to 9.9x on smaller models, while stronger models show collective resistance. Targeted detector augmentation provides only partial remediation (10.2% improvement on Llama, 78.7% on Gemini), suggesting the vulnerability is architectural rather than incidental for weaker models. Our framework, task bank, and payload generator are released publicly.

</details>


### [161] [Parser-Free Querying of Security Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22027)
*Evan Luo,Julien Piet,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sieve是一个通过大语言模型将自然语言安全查询自动转换为可执行代码的系统，在133个安全查询测试中，相比手动脚本编写将错误率降低了3倍以上，尤其擅长多行时间关联查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全日志查询面临两难：传统基于结构化模式的归一化查询方法虽然功能强大，但需要为每种新日志格式持续投入工程成本；而直接使用grep等工具查询原始日志虽然成本低，但要求分析师了解每种日志源的消息变体，且无法表达安全调查所需的多行时间序列查询。

Method: Sieve通过轻量级、自动提取的日志格式上下文来引导大语言模型，每个查询仅需一次LLM调用，随后执行确定性执行，生成可执行的查询代码。

Result: 在5种日志类型上的133个安全查询评估中，Sieve在复杂时间序列和跨事件查询上的错误率比手动分析师脚本编写降低了3倍以上，在多行关联任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码能够弥合结构化日志查询的表达能力与直接处理原始文件的即时性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Security analysts routinely query system logs to detect threats and investigate incidents, but each log source uses its own semi-structured format: logs are cheap to produce, but expensive to use. The standard approach, building per-source parsers to normalize logs into structured schemas, is powerful but requires continuous engineering effort for each new format. Querying raw logs directly with tools like grep avoids this cost, but requires analysts to know each source's message variants and cannot express the multi-line temporal queries that security investigations demand. We present Sieve, a system that generates executable query code from natural-language security questions by grounding a large language model with lightweight, automatically extracted log-format context, requiring only one LLM call per query followed by deterministic execution. Evaluating 133 security queries across 5 log types, we find that Sieve achieves over a 3x reduction in error rate on complex temporal and cross-event queries compared to manual analyst scripting, with the largest gains on the multi-line correlation tasks most critical to active investigations. Our results and benchmark provide evidence that LLM-generated code can bridge the gap between the expressiveness of structured log querying and the immediacy of working directly with raw files.

</details>


### [162] [Safeguarding Text-to-Image Generative Models Against Unauthorized Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22060)
*Yilan Gao,Sida Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WaveGuard是一个基于生成器的单次保护框架，通过对合成图像注入频率感知的、结构化的、不可感知的扰动，在保持视觉质量的同时降低被保护图像被用于未经授权的模型窃取（知识蒸馏）的训练价值，实现了效果-保真度-效率的有利平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭权重生成服务通过API部署时面临模型窃取威胁：攻击者可反复查询API收集大量合成图像，用于训练私有替代模型，实现未经授权的知识蒸馏和能力复制。现有防御需要一种能保持发布图像视觉保真度、提供扰动幅度的显式控制、并能高效扩展到大量输出发布的实用方案。

Method: WaveGuard采用单次、基于生成器的保护框架，包含一个频率感知的扰动生成器，用于注入结构化的、不可感知的扰动。该扰动在维持良性观看者的感知可用性的同时，降低被保护图像作为未经授权的学生模型训练数据的效用。用户可指定扰动预算以显式控制扰动幅度。

Result: 在WikiArt相关合成输出蒸馏设置下的广泛实验表明，WaveGuard实现了效果-保真度-效率的有利权衡，具有显式的不可感知性控制，并在保护效率上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: WaveGuard能有效防御针对闭权重生成服务的模型窃取攻击，通过频率感知的扰动注入在保持图像视觉质量的同时降低其被用于知识蒸馏的价值，且具备高效的规模化部署能力。

Abstract: Closed-weight generative services are increasingly deployed through query-based APIs, where users can obtain generated outputs while model parameters remain inaccessible. However, such deployment does not prevent model stealing: an attacker can repeatedly query the service, collect large volumes of released synthetic images, and use them as training data for a private substitute model. This query-output-driven process enables unauthorized knowledge distillation and capability replication without direct access to the original weights. To mitigate this threat, a practical defense should preserve the visual fidelity of released images, provide explicit control over perturbation magnitude, and scale efficiently to large-volume output release. We present WaveGuard, a single-pass, generator-based protection framework that safeguards released synthetic images under a user-specified perturbation budget. WaveGuard employs a frequency-aware perturbation generator to inject structured, imperceptible perturbations that maintain perceptual utility for benign viewers while reducing the usefulness of protected images as training data for unauthorized student models. Extensive experiments under WikiArt-related synthetic-output distillation settings show that WaveGuard achieves a favorable efficacy--fidelity--efficiency trade-off, with explicit imperceptibility control and substantial gains in protection efficiency.

</details>


### [163] [QT-PUF: Quantum Tunneling Leakage Based PUF for Implantable IoMT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22113)
*Yueqi Ma,Vivek Mohan,Chip-Hong Chang,Emmanuel M. Drakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QT-PUF，一种基于量子隧穿门漏电的物理不可克隆函数，用于IoMT设备安全认证，在65nm CMOS工艺下实现极低功耗（96.04 nW/bit）、高熵（0.9999998）和良好可靠性（BER<0.000163），无需外部激励或稳定电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT设备（可穿戴与植入式医疗器件）面临物理篡改和伪造威胁，传统PUF方案（基于存储器、环形振荡器等）因功耗高、需外部激励或稳定性差，不适用于超低功耗IoMT场景。

Method: 利用标准CMOS工艺中工艺偏差导致的量子力学栅极隧穿漏电流作为熵源，设计伪电阻I-V前端差分读取电路，将皮安级漏电流变化转为数字响应，无需外部激励或稳定电路，静态偏置工作。

Result: 65nm CMOS仿真：熵0.9999998，片间汉明距离0.5001，平均功耗96.04 nW/bit（19.21 fJ/bit），在1.0-1.3V和10-70°C范围内平均BER低于0.000163。

Conclusion: QT-PUF以极低功耗和面积开销实现高安全性物理认证，适合植入式医疗等超低功耗IoMT设备。

Abstract: The Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) marks a shift toward decentralized healthcare, enabling continuous monitoring and personalized care through connected wearable and implantable devices. However, ensuring the trust and integrity of these devices themselves remains a major challenge, as physical compromise or counterfeiting can directly endanger patient safety, privacy, and data integrity. This work presents QT-PUF, a gate-tunneling-leakage-based physical unclonable function (PUF) that leverages quantum-mechanical gate leakage resulting from process-induced variations in standard CMOS devices. A differential readout circuit with a pseudo-resistor I-to-V frontend is proposed to convert the picoampere-level leakage variations into digital responses. Unlike existing PUFs such as those based on memory, ring oscillators, or arbiters, which are less suitable for ultralow-power IoMT devices (due to additional circuitry, power overhead, or poor stability), QT-PUF requires no external excitation or stabilization and operates under static bias. Simulation-based measurements for a $\mathbf{65}$~nm CMOS process demonstrate an entropy of $\mathbf{0.9999998}$, an FHD of $\mathbf{0.5001}$, and an average power (energy) consumption of $\mathbf{96.04}$~nW/bit ($\mathbf{19.21}$~fJ/bit, respectively) at $\mathbf{1.2\,V}$ and $\mathbf{35\,^{\circ}C}$ for the proposed PUF. It operates reliably across $\mathbf{0.9}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{0}\text{--}\mathbf{100\,^{\circ}C}$ with an average BER below $\mathbf{0.000163}$ across $\mathbf{1.0}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{10}\text{--}\mathbf{70\,^{\circ}C}$ within the operating conditions of typical implantable devices.

</details>


### [164] [PACT: Reducing Alert Fatigue in Low-Prevalence SOC Streams with Triggered Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22324)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PACT框架，通过Pareto感知的主动学习控制器，在低警报率安全场景中平衡假阳性负担与召回率，相较于基线方法降低43%和21%的假阳性负担，同时显著减少分析师查询次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临持续的警报疲劳问题：低阳性率流中即使很低的假阳性率也会产生大量调查负担，而聚合F1分数会掩盖分析师的实际负担。

Method: PACT包含三个组件：已部署的冻结XGBoost-Focal筛选器、自适应窗口分数偏移触发器和混合获取规则（结合阈值相对不确定性与高分数采样），构成Pareto感知的触发式主动学习控制器。

Result: 在AIT-ADS和BOTSv1两个低阳性率基准上，PACT在自适应方法中达到最低的良性归一化假阳性负担；相比冻结基线分别降低43%和21%负担；相比周期性均匀随机更新减少3.8倍和5.2倍分析师查询；触发消融实验显示主动获取贡献超出单纯触发时机控制，但代价是自由运行触发下阳性窗口召回率下降约10个百分点。

Conclusion: 纯假阳性最小化会导致不可接受的召回率下降，PACT提供了一种有效的帕累托权衡方案。

Abstract: Security operations centers face persistent alert fatigue: in low-prevalence streams, even low false-positive rates generate substantial investigation load, while aggregate F1 scores obscure analyst burden. We introduce PACT, a Pareto-aware controller for triggered active learning, which wraps an already-deployed frozen XGBoost-Focal screener with an adaptive windowing score-shift trigger and a hybrid acquisition rule combining threshold-relative uncertainty with high-score sampling. On two public low-prevalence benchmarks, AIT-ADS (AIT Alert Data Set), and BOTSv1 (Boss of the SOC version 1), PACT attains the lowest benign-normalized false-positive (FP) burden among the adaptive methods tested. It reduces burden by 43% and 21%, respectively, relative to a frozen baseline, while using 3.8x and 5.2x fewer analyst queries than periodic uniform-random updating. A matched-trigger ablation controls trigger timing and shows that acquisition contributes beyond timing alone, at the cost of approximately ten percentage points of positive-window recall under free-running triggers. A frozen threshold-only baseline pushes FP lower still but collapses BOTSv1 recall by 55 percentage points. Under the evaluated workload assumptions, pure FP minimization trades unacceptable recall for that lower burden.

</details>


### [165] [Building Europe's Quantum Shield: The Strategic view for a Continent-Wide Quantum Key Ditribution (QKD) Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22332)
*Leandros Maglaras,Ilias Papastamatiou,Alexios Aivaliotis,Evangelos Markatos,Konstantinos Karantzalos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SEEWQCI项目通过结合光纤电缆、卫星通信和增强安全规则，构建欧洲通信基础设施（EuroQCI），以抵御量子计算对现有安全系统的威胁，保护电力网和医院等关键服务及欧洲公民数据安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的快速发展在带来科学突破的同时，能够破解当前安全系统，对现有数字系统构成新风险，因此需要部署抗量子安全基础设施。

Method: SEEWQCI项目采用光纤电缆与卫星通信相结合的混合架构，并部署增强的安全规则，构建多层次数字防护体系。

Result: 形成了一套结合地面光纤和空间卫星的综合通信安全基础设施（EuroQCI），重点保障关键服务（如电网、医院）的数据安全。

Conclusion: SEEWQCI项目是欧盟应对量子计算安全威胁的关键举措，通过混合通信架构有效保护欧洲公民数据和关键基础设施。

Abstract: The fast growth of quantum computing can lead to amazing scientific breakthroughs while on the same time can be used to break today's security systems, raising new risks for existing digital systems. Facing this challenge, the European's Union's deployement of the European Communication Infrastructure (EuroQCI) is crucial. The SEEWQCI project combines fiber cables, satellite communications and enhanced security rules to build a strong digital shield. Its focus is to protect vital services like power grids and hospitals keeping Europeans' data safe.

</details>


### [166] [A First Measurement Study on Authentication Security in Real-World Remote MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22333)
*Huijun Zhou,Xiaohan Zhang,Haozhe Zhang,Haoyang Zhang,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对真实世界远程MCP服务器的认证安全进行大规模测量研究，发现40.55%的服务器未实施认证，96.6%的OAuth服务器存在动态客户端注册缺陷，总计识别325个认证漏洞并获9个CVE编号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MCP协议成为LLM与外部服务的关键接口，远程部署中客户端与服务器之间的认证边界变得安全关键，但该领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过扫描发现7,973个活跃远程MCP服务器，结合被动流量检测与主动动态探测的半自动化框架，对119个可测试的OAuth MCP服务器进行系统性漏洞检测。

Result: 40.55%服务器无认证；所有119个测试服务器均存在至少一个缺陷，共325个；动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%的服务器；获取9个CVE编号。

Conclusion: MCP生态系统存在普遍的认证弱点，尤其是OAuth部署面临开放客户端环境、动态客户端注册和委托授权等新型攻击面，亟需强化基于OAuth的远程部署安全性。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is emerging as a common interface connecting large language models (LLMs) with external services. Remote deployments are becoming increasingly important as agents connect to user-linked online services, such as social, productivity, and financial services. In such deployments, the authentication boundary between MCP clients and remote servers becomes security-critical, yet remains underexplored.
  We present the first measurement study of authentication security in real-world remote MCP servers. We identify 7,973 live remote MCP servers, finding that 40.55% expose tools without authentication. Among authenticated servers, OAuth is the dominant authorization mechanism for reaching remote services, and OAuth deployments in the MCP ecosystem commonly exhibit three characteristics: open client environments, dynamic client registration, and delegated authorization. These characteristics distinguish MCP deployments from traditional OAuth and introduce new attack surfaces. Guided by this observation, we derive a taxonomy of authentication flaws comprising three MCP-specific categories and conventional OAuth misconfigurations, for a total of four categories and nine concrete flaw types. To evaluate these flaws at scale, we implement a semi-automated detection framework that combines passive traffic inspection with active dynamic probing. Applying it to 119 testable real-world OAuth-enabled MCP servers, we find that each server exhibits at least one flaw, with a total of 325 flaws identified, among which dynamic client registration flaws affect 96.6% of tested servers. Many of these flaws can lead to sensitive information leakage and account takeover. Through responsible disclosure, we obtained 9 CVE IDs. Our findings expose pervasive authentication weaknesses in the MCP ecosystem and underscore the urgent need for hardened OAuth-based remote deployments.

</details>


### [167] [TimeGuard: Channel-wise Pool Training for Backdoor Defense in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22365)
*Quang Duc Nguyen,Siyuan Liang,Yiming Li,Fushuo Huo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了13种后门防御在时间序列预测中的失效原因，发现数据纠缠导致信道级信号稀释、任务形式转换导致训练损失退化两大问题，并提出TimeGuard防御方法，采用通道级池训练、时间感知初始化与距离正则化损失选择，在多个数据集和架构上显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测（TSF）易受后门攻击威胁，但针对TSF的后门防御研究严重不足，主要面临数据纠缠和任务形式转换两大挑战，现有防御方法直接迁移效果不佳。

Method: TimeGuard采用通道级池训练（channel-wise pool training）为核心范式，通过时间感知标准初始化高置信度池以缓解信号稀释；引入距离正则化损失选择（distance-regularized loss selection）机制，在训练过程中逐步扩展可靠池并缓解损失退化问题。

Result: 在多个数据集、预测架构和TSF后门攻击下，TimeGuard将MAE_P提升至领先基线的1.96倍，同时将干净性能损失控制在5% MAE_C以内，显著增强了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 系统评估揭示的两种失效机制为TSF后门防御提供了理论依据，TimeGuard作为首个针对性训练时防御方案，有效解决了数据纠缠和任务形式转换带来的挑战，为时间序列安全领域提供了新范式。

Abstract: Time Series Forecasting (TSF) plays a critical role across many domains, yet it is vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, backdoor defenses tailored to TSF remain underexplored, due to data entanglement and task-formulation shift challenges. To fill this gap, we conduct a systematic evaluation of thirteen representative backdoor defenses across the TSF life cycle and analyze their failure modes. Our results reveal two fundamental issues: (1) data entanglement induces channel-level signal dilution, rendering sample-filtering and trigger-synthesis defenses ineffective at localizing backdoors; and (2) task-formulation shift leads to training-loss degeneration, causing poisoned and clean windows to become indistinguishable at training stages. Based on these findings, we propose a training-time backdoor defense for TSF, termed TimeGuard. Our method adopts channel-wise pool training as the core paradigm and initializes a high-confidence pool using time-aware criteria to mitigate signal dilution. Moreover, we introduce distance-regularized loss selection to progressively expand the reliable pool during training and ease loss degeneration. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, forecasting architectures, and TSF backdoor attacks demonstrate that TimeGuard substantially improves robustness, boosting $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{P}$ by $1.96\times$ over the leading baseline, while preserving clean performance within 5% $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{C}$.

</details>


### [168] [Characterizing the Fault Response of the Intel Neural Compute Stick 2 Under Single-Pulse Electromagnetic Fault Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22437)
*Štefan Kučerák,Jakub Breier,Xiaolu Hou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地对Intel Movidius Myriad X (NCS2)进行了单脉冲电磁故障注入(EMFI)实验，揭示了四类故障响应：无影响、轻微静默数据损坏、严重持续性精度退化（需模型重载恢复）、设备挂起。关键发现是严重退化类故障在热点位置可达18-31%触发率，且推理API无法检测，即使设备空闲时注入亦可诱发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经推理加速器正越来越多地部署于安全攸关的边缘应用，但其在瞬态硬件扰动下的故障响应在公开文献中缺乏系统性的刻画，仅有一项可行性研究针对NCS2。

Method: 对运行三个ImageNet训练CNN模型（ResNet-18/50, VGG-11）的NCS2执行系统性的单脉冲电磁故障注入，包含1,536次热点定位试验和约16,000次参数搜索试验，分析故障结果分类。

Result: 识别出四类可复现的结果：无精度变化、轻微静默数据损坏、严重持续性退化（跨推理持续，需模型重载恢复）、设备挂起（需USB断电重启）。严重退化类在热点位置触发率18-31%，后崩溃top-1精度低于5%。该退化也可在设备空闲时诱导产生。

Conclusion: 现有推理API无法检测严重持续性退化故障，模型加载时的完整性检查不足以防范此类攻击。需要应用层可实现的缓解策略，无需修改固件或运行时。

Abstract: Vision processing units and other commercial neural-network inference accelerators are increasingly deployed in safety-relevant edge applications, but their fault response under transient hardware disturbances remains poorly characterized in the open literature. For the Intel Movidius Myriad X, packaged as the Intel Neural Compute Stick 2 (NCS2), only a single feasibility study has been published. We report a systematic single-pulse electromagnetic fault injection (EMFI) campaign on the NCS2 running three ImageNet-trained convolutional neural networks (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, VGG-11) on the OpenVINO runtime. Across 1,536 spot-test trials at characterized hotspots and approximately 16,000 parameter-search trials, single pulses produce four reproducible outcome classes: no measured accuracy change, minor silent data corruption, major persistent degradation that survives across subsequent inferences until model reload, and device hangs requiring USB power-cycling; these outcomes are respectively interpreted as no-effect, SDC with possible SET-like or small persistent-state mechanisms, SEU-like persistent corruption, and SEFI-like loss of functionality. Two findings are central. First, the major-degradation class can be induced at 18-31% of trials at characterized hotspots, with post-collapse top-1 accuracy below five percent and persistence across all subsequent inferences until explicit model reload - a regime that no inference-API-level mechanism detects. Second, this regime is also inducible by pulses delivered to an idle device with the model already loaded, demonstrating that load-time integrity checks alone are insufficient. We discuss mitigation strategies graded by class, focusing on mechanisms implementable at the application level without modification to the device firmware or the OpenVINO runtime.

</details>


### [169] [EnCAgg: Enhanced Clustering Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning against Dynamic Model Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22506)
*Tianyun Zhang,Zhen Yang,Haozhao Wang,Ru Zhang,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于少量已知良性客户端作为参考的鲁棒聚合方法，通过密度聚类、生成伪梯度和重聚类三步策略，在动态投毒场景下有效识别并过滤恶意梯度，同时保留尽可能多的良性梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临模型投毒攻击威胁，现有防御方法依赖固定阈值或固定聚类数，难以适应恶意客户端的动态投毒策略，且因客户端数据异构性导致良性梯度丢失。

Method: 1) 基于密度的低维梯度聚类：将梯度投影到两个最大差异维度上，进行密度聚类以识别恶意梯度并保留良性离群点；2) 增强聚类低维梯度生成器：学习生成与良性聚类边界对齐的伪梯度，作为连接稀疏良性离群点的桥梁；3) 低维梯度重聚类：将生成的伪梯度与真实梯度一起聚类，恢复被误分类为噪声点的良性梯度。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和MIND数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在动态投毒场景下表现出优越的保真度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过少量已知良性客户端作为参考，结合密度聚类、伪梯度生成与重聚类，有效解决了动态投毒攻击下恶意梯度识别和良性梯度保留的难题。

Abstract: Federated learning faces increasing threats from model poisoning attacks, which harms its application to improve privacy. Existing defense methods typically rely on fixed thresholds or perform clustering with a fixed number of clusters to distinguish malicious gradients from benign ones. However, these methods are difficult to adapt to dynamic poisoning strategies of malicious clients, and often result in the loss of benign gradients due to the heterogeneity of clients' local datasets. To address these problems, we propose a novel robust aggregation method that leverages a small number of known benign clients as references, enabling accurate identification and filtering of malicious gradients while retaining as many benign gradients as possible, even when the number of malicious clients is unknown and variable. First, we introduce a density-based low-dimensional gradient clustering method, which projects gradients onto the two most divergent dimensions and applies density-based clustering to identify malicious gradients while retaining clustered benign gradients and potentially benign outliers. Second, we design an enhancing clustering low-dimensional gradient generator model, which learns to generate pseudo-gradients aligned with the boundary of the benign cluster. These pseudo-gradients act as bridges to connect sparse benign gradient outliers. Third, we introduce low-dimensional gradient re-clustering that clusters the generated pseudo-gradients together with real gradients to recover benign gradients misclassified as noise points, enabling more benign gradients to participate in aggregation. Extensive experiments on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and MIND datasets demonstrate that our method exhibits superior fidelity and robustness under dynamic poisoning scenarios.

</details>


### [170] [A Formal Basis for Quantum Cryptographic Exposure Measurement under HNDL Threat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22569)
*Matheus Rufino,Rafael Duarte Marcelino,Julio Smanioto Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于HNDL妥协概率的分解框架，该框架通过时间风险、加密脆弱性与操作暴露的乘性项以及防御-攻击强度比决定的分母饱和项构成，论证了可加性评分框架无法复现这一结构，为部分可观测条件下的操作暴露优先级排序提供了结构性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前加密流量面临"先窃取、后解密"的量子计算威胁，现有评估框架多为可加性评分模型，但缺乏对加密脆弱性与操作暴露之间交互作用的结构性刻画，无法准确反映组织的真实暴露风险。

Method: 基于三个关于对抗生产与价值衰减动态的假设，推导出HNDL妥协概率的分解形式：时间危险函数 × (加密脆弱性 × 操作暴露) / (1 + 防御-攻击强度比)；并论证了可加性评分框架因构造缺失交互项而无法复现该结构。

Result: HNDL妥协概率的结构性分解成立，各维度的边际敏感度内生依赖于组织在脆弱性-暴露平面中的位置，而非全局常数；可加性框架在结构上无法再现该交互效应。

Conclusion: 该框架为部分可观测条件下基于结构性基础的HNDL暴露优先级排序提供了理论依据，优于传统可加性校准方法。

Abstract: An adversary copies your encrypted traffic today and waits for a quantum computer to decrypt it later. How exposed are you?
  We show that the functional form of the answer is not merely a calibration choice -- it is structurally justified by three assumptions about adversarial production and value-decay dynamics. Under those assumptions, the HNDL compromise probability factorises into a temporal hazard, a multiplicative cryptographic-vulnerability and operational-exposure term, and a saturation denominator governed by the defense-attack intensity ratio; the marginal sensitivity to each dimension is endogenous to the organisation's position in the vulnerability-exposure plane, not a fixed global constant. Additive scoring frameworks cannot reproduce this structure because the interaction between cryptographic vulnerability and operational exposure is absent by construction, regardless of calibration. The resulting framework provides a structurally grounded basis for operational HNDL exposure prioritisation under partial observability.

</details>


### [171] [Building an Open Source Operational Technology Pentesting Platform: Lessons from LINICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22590)
*Awais Rashid,Joseph Gardiner,Louise Evans*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LINICS，一个用于OT渗透测试和安全分析的开源平台，填补了OT领域缺少类似Kali Linux这样专业安全工具平台的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT安全领域已有Kali Linux等开源平台，但支撑工业控制系统的操作技术(OT)领域缺乏类似的专门安全分析平台，存在工具分散、获取困难的问题。

Method: 作者基于架构设计、构建和发布的实践经验，开发了LINICS开源平台，专门用于OT渗透测试和安全分析。

Result: 成功构建并发布了LINICS平台，为OT安全从业者提供了一个集成的开源安全分析工具集。

Conclusion: LINICS填补了OT安全领域缺少集成化开源平台的空白，有望降低OT安全分析的门槛，促进工业控制系统安全研究的发展。

Abstract: Information Technology (IT) security professionals have ready access to open-source platforms such as Kali Linux. But no such platform exists for Operational Technology (OT) that underpins Industrial Control Systems. We discuss experiences of architecting, building and releasing LINICS, an open-source platform for OT pentesting and security analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [A Symbolic Homotopy Algorithm for Solving Composable Polynomial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22514)
*Thi Xuan Vu*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用可组合结构（每个多项式可表示为复合函数）来高效求解多项式系统孤立正则解的 probabilistic 算法，其算术复杂度在输入规模和解的数量上是多项式的。关键应用包括代数独立多项式子环下的系统，以及有限反射群（如对称群、超八面体群、二面体群及例外反射群）的不变多项式系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多项式系统求解算法在处理高维或结构复杂的系统时计算代价高昂。本文旨在利用系统固有的可组合结构（即每个方程可表示为某些基本多项式的复合）来降低问题维度，从而提高求解效率。

Method: 提出一个概率性算法，通过将原系统约化为关于中间变量 \(g_j\) 的系统来利用可组合结构。算法计算所有孤立正则解，其算术复杂度在输入规模和解的数量上呈多项式增长。

Result: 算法在算术复杂度上达到了多项式级别的效率提升，适用于两类重要场景：1) 多项式属于代数独立多项式生成的子环；2) 有限反射群（如 \(S_n, B_n, I_2(m), E_6, E_7, E_8, F_4, H_3, H_4\)）的不变多项式系统。

Conclusion: 组合结构可以显著提升多项式系统求解的效率，且算法在理论上保证了多项式复杂度。该工作为求解具有对称性或不变性结构的系统提供了实用的计算工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of computing the isolated regular solutions of a system \((f_1,\ldots,f_n)\) of \(n\) polynomial equations in \(n\) variables \((X_1, \dots, X_n)\) over a field of characteristic zero \(k\). We focus on systems with a \emph{composable structure}, where each polynomial \(f_i\) can be expressed as a composition \( f_i = h_i(g_1,\dots,g_n)\). Exploiting this structure allows us to reduce the original system to one in the \(g_j\) variables, thereby significantly improving the efficiency of symbolic solution algorithms. We present a probabilistic algorithm that computes all isolated regular solutions, with arithmetic complexity being polynomial in the input size and in the number of solutions.
  A first important application is when \(f_1, \dots, f_n\) belong to the subring \(k[g_1, \dots, g_n]\), where \(g_1, \dots, g_n\) are algebraically independent polynomials in \(k[X_1, \dots, X_n]\). Another important application is to systems of invariant polynomials under finite reflection groups, since by the Chevalley-Shephard-Todd theorem their invariant rings are polynomial algebras. Typical examples include the symmetric groups \(S_n\), the hyperoctahedral groups \(B_n\), the dihedral groups \(I_2(m)\), and the exceptional finite reflection groups \(E_6, E_7, E_8, F_4, H_3, H_4\).

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Impact of Atmospheric Turbulence and Pointing Error on Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22268)
*Celia Sánchez-de-Miguel,Antonio M. Mercado-Martínez,Beatriz Soret,Antonio Jurado-Navas,Miguel Castillo-Vázquez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个增强型图像模拟器，用于在遥感图像中引入大气湍流和卫星指向抖动等物理退化效应，并通过船舶检测任务（YOLOv8和RetinaNet）验证了这些退化对AI模型性能的显著影响，揭示了当前训练数据集缺乏真实物理退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练AI检测模型的数据集很少考虑大气湍流和卫星指向抖动等真实物理退化效应，导致模型在实际运行环境中性能不可靠。

Method: 基于先前工作，提出了一个增强型图像模拟器，能够模拟垂直路径大气湍流和卫星平台/传感器振动引起的指向抖动，生成物理上真实的失真图像。以船舶检测为案例，使用YOLOv8和RetinaNet在不同湍流和指向误差水平下进行评估。

Result: YOLOv8的召回率从理想条件下的91%降至弱湍流下的60%，强湍流或抖动下低于40%。RetinaNet表现出更强的鲁棒性，在退化条件下保持约75%的召回率。

Conclusion: 将真实的物理退化纳入EO训练数据集对于确保AI模型在实际运行环境中的可靠性能至关重要，这在海上监视应用中得到了验证。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) imagery is often degraded by atmospheric turbulence and pointing jitter; yet, these effects are rarely considered in datasets used to train AI-based detection models. Based on prior work, this paper presents an enhanced image simulator that enables the incorporation of vertical-path atmospheric turbulence and satellite pointing jitter, arising from platform and sensor vibrations, to generate physically realistic distorted images. As a case study, vessel detection is evaluated using YOLOv8 and RetinaNet on images generated by the proposed simulator under different levels of turbulence and pointing errors. Results show that YOLOv8 recall decreases from 91% under ideal conditions to 60% in the presence of weak turbulence, and falls below 40% under strong turbulence or jitter. In contrast, RetinaNet demonstrates greater robustness, maintaining approximately 75% recall across degraded conditions. These results highlight the importance of incorporating realistic physical degradations into EO training datasets to ensure reliable performance of AI-based models in operational environments, as demonstrated in maritime surveillance applications.

</details>


### [174] [Throughput and Delay Performance of Slotted Aloha in SmartBANs under Saturation Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22317)
*Anastasios C. Politis,Constantinos S. Hilas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文为欧洲电信标准协会（ETSI）SmartBAN规范定义的时隙Aloha协议在饱和条件下建立了二维离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）模型，评估其饱和吞吐量和平均端到端延迟性能，并通过仿真验证了模型的高精度，可用于协议性能预测与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估ETSI SmartBAN规范中时隙Aloha协议在饱和条件下的性能，填补该协议在理论建模方面的空白，为协议优化提供理论依据。

Method: 构建二维离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）模型，对时隙Aloha协议的运行细节进行数学建模，推导饱和吞吐量和平均端到端延迟的理论表达式，并通过仿真验证模型准确性。

Result: 所提DTMC模型的理论结果与仿真结果高度吻合，验证了模型的有效性；模型能够准确预测协议在饱和条件下的吞吐量和延迟性能。

Conclusion: 该DTMC模型可作为ETSI SmartBAN时隙Aloha协议性能预测与优化的有效工具，具有较高的实用价值。

Abstract: This letter evaluates the performance of the slotted Aloha protocol defined by the European Telecommunication Standard Institute (ETSI) SmartBAN specification, under saturation conditions. For this purpose, we develop a two-dimensional Discrete Time Markov Chain (DTMC) to model the operational details of the protocol and assess its performance in terms of saturation throughput and average end-to-end delay. The accuracy of the proposed model is validated by means of simulation which reveals a very good match among theoretical and simulation results. The model can be used for protocol performance prediction and optimization purposes.

</details>


### [175] [SCALE: Sensitivity-Aware Federated Unlearning with Information Freshness Optimization for Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22589)
*Zihao Ding,Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SCALE框架，一种结合历史贡献分析与信息新鲜度感知自适应稀疏化的双重遗忘学习框架，用于解决移动边缘计算中联邦遗忘学习精度低和缺乏时间信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘学习方法存在遗忘精度低、缺乏时间信息反映的问题，导致遗忘性能次优，无法有效满足移动边缘计算系统中的数据污染和隐私法规要求。

Method: 提出SCALE双重遗忘框架：1) 基于历史贡献的层敏感性分析，识别受目标客户端影响最大的层；2) 在权重子组级别进行自适应稀疏化，平衡信息新鲜度与遗忘效果。

Result: 理论分析证明了收敛性和加速优势；实验和测试床结果表明，与最先进基线相比，遗忘效果显著提升。

Conclusion: SCALE框架通过双层级遗忘机制有效解决了联邦遗忘学习中的精度问题和时间信息缺失，在移动边缘计算场景下实现了更优的遗忘性能。

Abstract: Federated Unlearning (FU) is emerging as a powerful tool that enables the selective removal of client data to effectively address data contamination and meet strict privacy regulations in mobile edge computing (MEC) systems. Although FU has recently drawn attention in the AI community, existing approaches suffer from low unlearning precision and lack temporal information reflection, which results in suboptimal forgetting performance. To address these issues, we propose SCALE, a dual-level unlearning framework combining historical contribution analysis with information freshness-aware adaptive sparsification. Our framework first employs a historical contribution-based layer sensitivity analysis to identify layers most influenced by target clients, then performs fine-grained unlearning through adaptive sparsification at the weight sub-group level to balance information freshness with forgetting effectiveness. Through theoretical analysis, the proposed framework demonstrates the convergence properties and acceleration advantages. Our experiments and testbed results demonstrate superior unlearning effectiveness compared to state-of-the-art baselines, with significantly improved forgetting performance.

</details>


### [176] [An intensive vRAN deployment with OpenAirInterface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22638)
*Romain Beurdouche,Raymond Knopp*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文描述了如何通过适配软件栈（OpenAirInterface）来利用硬件加速能力，实现5G虚拟化无线接入网（vRAN）的高密度部署，并在共享服务器上运行多个vRAN实例，同时分析了不同计算机架构对部署性能的影响及局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G vRAN对计算机架构提出新挑战，需要在性能、灵活性和效率之间取得平衡，现有通用处理器和硬件加速方案仍需进一步优化以支持大规模vRAN部署。

Method: 基于OpenAirInterface 5G软件栈，针对不同计算机架构（通用处理器和硬件加速器）适配软件栈，通过优化使其充分利用硬件特性，并探索在共享服务器上扩展多个vRAN实例的部署方式。

Result: 成功实现了对软件栈的改进并提升了性能，观察并分析了不同计算机架构对部署行为的影响，识别了当前部署方案的局限性。

Conclusion: 本研究为实现更优的vRAN部署提供了实践经验，指出了当前方案的局限性和未来改进方向。

Abstract: The advent of 5G virtualized Radio Access Networks (vRANs) brings a new challenge with regards to computer architectures. It requires to select or design computing technologies that provide a sufficient level of performance while maximizing the flexibility and efficiency of the implemented networks. Several solutions addressing this challenge were proposed, relying on general purpose processors as well as hardware accelerators. This work describes our effort to enable an intensive vRAN deployment using the 5G software stack OpenAirInterface on top of these computer architectures. We had to adapt the software stack to leverage the capabilities of hardware and to find how to scale up the vRAN deployment with several vRAN instances sharing a server. We describe in this work our improvements to the stack and their effect on performance. We also share our observations on the behavior of the computer architectures and how they affect our deployment. We finally discuss the limitations of our deployment and further efforts to implement better vRAN deployments.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Flying Together: Human-Guided Immersive Shared Control for Aerial Robot Teams in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21680)
*Lou De Bel-Air,Luca Morando,Ruitao Chen,Keru Wang,Benjamin Jarvis,Charbel Toumieh,Yang Zhou,Ken Perlin,Dario Floreano,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于虚拟现实的共享控制框架，用于多无人机在未知受限环境中的协同导航，通过用户引导的运动基元规划器和导纳控制器实现实时人机协同探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主多机器人系统在非结构化环境中难以适应未知条件并捕捉操作者的意图，需要一种能融合人类指导的灵活控制方法。

Method: 构建VR共享控制框架，核心包括：(1) 用户引导的运动基元规划器，实时计算连续无碰撞轨迹并融合操作者输入；(2) 导纳控制器，使操作者能灵活影响队形行为；(3) 双边VR界面，操作者通过迁移点引导机群并接收实时视觉反馈；支持物理与仿真无人机混合现实操作。

Result: 实验表明共享控制提升了避障能力、保持了机间间距，并降低了操作者负担，验证了沉浸式人在回路多机器人导航的可行性与优势。

Conclusion: VR共享控制框架能有效结合人类直觉与自主规划，在未知受限环境中实现安全、高效的多无人机协同导航。

Abstract: While autonomous multi-robots can achieve safe and coordinated navigation, they often struggle to adapt to unforeseen conditions and to capture operator-driven objectives in unstructured environments. We present a Virtual Reality (VR)-based shared control framework for teams of drones operating in constrained and unknown environments, enabling real-time, user-guided exploration. At the core of our approach is a novel, user-guided motion-primitive-based planner that computes continuous, collision-free trajectories while continuously integrating operator input. This planner is coupled with an admittance controller, allowing the operator to flexibly influence team behavior and guide drones toward regions of interest that autonomous planners may overlook. The system supports mixed-reality operations with both physical and simulated drones, and implements a bilateral VR-based interface, allowing the operator to guide the robot team via migration points while receiving immediate visual feedback of the team state. Experimental results show that shared control improves obstacle avoidance, maintains inter-agent spacing, and reduces operator effort, demonstrating the feasibility and advantages of immersive, human-in-the-loop multi-robot navigation.

</details>


### [178] [Closed-Loop Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Deformable Microfiber Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21688)
*Alessandro Amici,Houari Bettahar,Veeti Jaakkola,Quan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在简化摩擦无关模拟器中训练强化学习策略，直接迁移至真实双夹持微操作系统的闭环 sim-to-real 方法，实现微纤维形状控制，在多种配置下达到亚毫米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观尺度下的表面和界面相互作用难以精确建模，限制了传统基于模型的控制和 sim-to-real 学习方法在自主接触式微操作中的应用。

Method: 在简化无摩擦模拟器中训练几何形状调节的 RL 策略，依靠部署时的实时视觉反馈闭环迭代修正未建模表面相互作用的影响，直接迁移至物理系统无需再训练或域自适应。

Result: 在24种不同初始配置下平均形状误差为270±80 μm；在9种不同纤维直径（50、80、120 μm）和操作长度（10、15、20 mm）组合下均达到亚毫米级最终形状误差。

Conclusion: 在简化模拟器中学习的策略能够在表面接触下实现可重复的真实微纤维形状调节，前提是 sim-to-real 失配的任务相关效应在闭环反馈中保持可观测和可修正。

Abstract: Autonomous contact-based micromanipulation is challenging because surface and interfacial interactions at the microscale are difficult to model accurately, limiting the use of conventional model-based control and sim-to-real learning. We present a closed-loop sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) approach for microfiber shape control on a surface. The central idea is to train geometric shape regulation in a simplified frictionless simulator and rely on real-time visual feedback during deployment to iteratively correct the observed effects of unmodeled surface interactions. An RL policy trained entirely in simulation is transferred directly to a physical dual-gripper micromanipulation system operating at 40 Hz, without retraining or domain adaptation. Using silk microfibers as a testbed, the policy achieves a mean point-wise shape error of 270 $\pm$ 80 $μ$m across twenty-four diverse initial configurations. Across nine specimens covering all combinations of three fiber diameters (50, 80, and 120 $μ$m) and three manipulated lengths (10 mm, 15mm, and 20 mm), the same policy achieves sub-millimeter final shape error without any retraining or retuning. These results show that a policy learned in a simplified simulator can achieve repeatable real-world microfiber shape regulation under surface contact, provided that the task-relevant effects of the sim-to-real mismatch remain observable and correctable within the closed feedback loop.

</details>


### [179] [Distributed Multi-Coverage for Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21686)
*Mariem Guitouni,Aaron T. Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分布式多覆盖算法，使机器人集群在仅依赖局部感知和局部通信、无全局协调的情况下，能够根据资产重要性提供冗余覆盖，并在部分机器人失效时维持任务可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机集群在监视、环境监测和基础设施检查等任务中需要可靠的覆盖，但机器人故障不可避免。资产需要被多台机器人冗余观测（多覆盖），且覆盖要求因资产重要性而异。现有集中式方法（整数规划）虽可最优求解，但实际部署面临通信范围有限、机载计算受限、局部故障不应导致任务中止等约束，亟需分布式解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种分布式多覆盖算法，适用于仅具备局部感知、局部通信且无全局协调的机器人集群。

Result: （摘要中未明确给出具体实验结果，需阅读全文。）

Conclusion: 该方法满足了实际部署中分布式、局部通信、局部感知、无全局协调的需求，能够在机器人故障时维持任务可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous drone swarms deployed for surveillance, environmental monitoring, and infrastructure inspection must maintain reliable coverage of critical assets despite robot failures. This requires multicoverage: each asset must be observed by multiple robots for redundancy, with coverage requirements varying by asset importance. While recent work has solved the centralized problem optimally using integer programming, practical deployments face constraints that demand distributed solutions: robots operate with limited communication ranges, onboard computation restricts global planning, and partial system failures must not cause mission abort. We present a distributed multicoverage algorithm for robot swarms operating with local sensing, local communication, and no global coordination.

</details>
